
===== cell 1 (kind 1) =====
# gl(3), L = 2, adjoint [2,1,0]: the (inf, inf) two-vacuum family as a bracket formula, and all six CG data from the bracket Q-number alone (Stage C + Q-side payoff, 2026-10-11)

Implements `L2-210-two-vacua-brackets-spec.md`, Tasks 0-5, for the adjoint [2,1,0] only (plus the [3,1,0] note of Task 4). Built by a sonnet wolfbook-builder; planned and reviewed by the main session. Nothing outside this notebook was edited.

**Family.** Companion frame, new conventions: Phi(a, a') = gaussCov[{a,0,a}] (x) gaussCov[{a',0,a'}] = Omega_c[(1/a, inf)] (x) Omega_c[(1/a', inf)] (first rows (1,a,a^2), (1,a',a'^2)); diagonal frame Omega_{(w1,inf)} (x) Omega_{(w1',inf)}, w1 = 1/a, w1' = 1/a'. Chain data: h = 1/2, theta = (2/7, -3/5) (t = theta12/h = 62/35), z = (3/2, 5/4, 2/3), 60 digits for the finite-twist part (Part A), 200 digits for the small-eps part (Part B). In the printed text `aS` = a, `bS` = a', `sS` = s = a'/a, `tt` = t.

**Task map (cell numbers are of this notebook; the printed output of every code cell is the markdown cell right below it).**

| task | code cells | printed results |
|---|---|---|
| setup (Stage B cells 2-12 + tools) | 3 ... 19 | 20 |
| 0 regression: blocks in (a, a'), ranks, count, 64-state identity | 22 | 23 |
| 1 minimal product count and closed forms in (a, a'), identity, structure | 26, 28, 30 | 27, 29, 31 |
| 3 Q-side: setup, dictionary, Qnum on 21 states, eps-exponents, six CG data, mixing, cost | 34 ... 46; 49, 51, 53, 55 | 50, 52, 54, 56 |
| 2 t-dependence (driver, fit, closed forms in (s, t)), u-form | 59, 61, 63 | 60, 62, 64 |
| 4 [3,1,0] note | 67 | 68 |
| 5 summary | | last markdown cell |

The tasks appear in the order 0, 1, 3, 2, 4 because the Task 2 and Task 4 drivers WIPE `Global``, re-run the finite-twist cells of this notebook with other parameter lines (read from this notebook's own file `L2-210-two-vacua-brackets.wb`) and store their results in the contexts `T2``, `T4`` (so no Module/With local may be used across the wipe: they live in `Global``). Run order: top to bottom; the Task 2 driver, then the fit and u-form cells, then the Task 4 driver.

**Notebook state (as in the earlier stages).** The file was written as JSON; the cells were NOT executed natively by VS Code (`wolfbook_runCell` on a freshly created file answers "NOT executed"; `wolfbook_newNotebook` / `wolfbook_saveNotebook` were not available). Every code cell was evaluated in the shared kernel K1 from exactly its saved source text (read from the scratch copy of the sources, identical to the cell text), and the printed results are stored in the markdown cells below the code cells. Rerun the cells in VS Code to obtain native outputs.

===== cell 2 (kind 1) =====
## Part A (finite twist, 60 digits): setup of Stage B (plain GT generators, transfer matrices, eigenstates, dual Q's, SoV basis, wavefunction rule, K tensor, tools) and Task 0

One switch: the line `Λ = {2, 1, 0}; nL = 2;` of the first code cell (read by the drivers). The first code cell wipes `Global``.

===== cell 3 (kind 2) =====
(*@a01*)
Quiet[ClearAll["Global`*"]];
(* GT basis and gl(3) generators (plain, exact rationals), verbatim from the clean L=1 notebook *)
ps[rep_] := ps[rep] = Block[{res = (Prepend[rep, #1] &) /@ Tuples[Table[Range[rep[[1, i + 1]], rep[[1, i]]], {i, 1, Length[rep[[1]]] - 1}]]}, If[Length[res[[1, 1]]] == 1, Reverse[Sort[res]], Reverse[Sort[Flatten[ps /@ res, 1]]]]];
dim[rep_] := dim[rep] = Length[ps[{rep}]];
J0[k_][pat_] := J0[k][pat] = Sum[pat[[k, i]], {i, 1, k}] - Sum[pat[[k - 1, i]], {i, 1, k - 1}];
Jp[k_][pat_] := Jp[k][pat] = -Sum[(Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k + 1, j]] + j, {j, Range[k + 1]}]/Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k, j]] + j, {j, Complement[Range[k], {i}]}])*v[ReplacePart[pat, {k, i} -> pat[[k, i]] + 1]], {i, 1, k}];
Jm[k_][pat_] := Jm[k][pat] = Sum[(Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k - 1, j]] + j, {j, Range[k - 1]}]/Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k, j]] + j, {j, Complement[Range[k], {i}]}])*v[ReplacePart[pat, {k, i} -> pat[[k, i]] - 1]], {i, 1, k}];
JJ[k_, l_, rep_] := JJ[k, l, rep] = Which[
  k == l, DiagonalMatrix[J0[k] /@ ps[{rep}]],
  l == k + 1, Transpose[Table[(Coefficient[Jp[k][ps[{rep}][[i]]], v[#1]] &) /@ ps[{rep}], {i, 1, Length[ps[{rep}]]}]],
  l == k - 1, Transpose[Table[(Coefficient[Jm[l][ps[{rep}][[i]]], v[#1]] &) /@ ps[{rep}], {i, 1, Length[ps[{rep}]]}]],
  l > k + 1, JJ[k, k + 1, rep] . JJ[k + 1, l, rep] - JJ[k + 1, l, rep] . JJ[k, k + 1, rep],
  l < k - 1, JJ[k, k - 1, rep] . JJ[k - 1, l, rep] - JJ[k - 1, l, rep] . JJ[k, k - 1, rep]];
checks = <||>; tims = <||>;
(* the ONLY rep switch is the next line *)
Λ = {2, 1, 0}; nL = 2; d1 = dim[Λ]; d2 = d1^nL;
hN = 1/2; thN = {2/7, -3/5}; zN = {3/2, 5/4, 2/3}; prec = 60;
tPar = (thN[[1]] - thN[[2]])/hN;
id1 = IdentityMatrix[d1, SparseArray]; id2 = IdentityMatrix[d2, SparseArray];
Ee[i_, j_] := Ee[i, j] = SparseArray[JJ[i, j, Λ]];
E1[i_, j_] := E1[i, j] = KroneckerProduct[Ee[i, j], id1]; E2[i_, j_] := E2[i, j] = KroneckerProduct[id1, Ee[i, j]];
wtL1 = Table[Table[J0[k][p], {k, 3}], {p, ps[{Λ}]}];
wtBasis = Flatten[Table[wtL1[[i]] + wtL1[[j]], {i, d1}, {j, d1}], 1];
uv[n_] := UnitVector[d1, n]; e1 = uv[1];
(* exp of a nilpotent matrix: finite sum (MatrixPower[nilpotent, 0] errors) *)
nexp[A_] := id1 + Sum[MatrixPower[A, k]/k!, {k, 1, 2 (Λ[[1]] - Λ[[3]])}];
nuP[n_][x_] := Product[x - thN[[s]] + hN Λ[[n]], {s, nL}];
(* exact sparse transfer matrices: Lax L_ij = (x - theta_s) delta_ij + h E_ij, twist M on the right, quantum minors with k-th factor at x-(k-1)h *)
lax1[s_, x_] := Table[KroneckerDelta[i, j] (x - thN[[s]]) id1 + hN Ee[i, j], {i, 3}, {j, 3}];
lax2[x_] := lax2[x] = With[{L1 = lax1[1, x], L2 = lax1[2, x]}, Table[Sum[KroneckerProduct[L1[[i, k]], L2[[k, j]]], {k, 3}], {i, 3}, {j, 3}]];
twistR[T_, M_] := Table[Sum[T[[i, l]] M[[l, j]], {l, 3}], {i, 3}, {j, 3}];
mono2[M_][x_] := mono2[M][x] = twistR[lax2[x], M];
qmin[M_][rI_, cJ_][x_] := Sum[Signature[σ] Dot @@ Table[mono2[M][x - (k - 1) hN][[σ[[k]], cJ[[k]]]], {k, Length[cJ]}], {σ, Permutations[rI]}];
tL2[a_, x_, M_] := tL2[a, x, M] = Sum[qmin[M][rows, rows][x], {rows, Subsets[Range[3], {a}]}];
Zd = DiagonalMatrix[zN]; Id3 = IdentityMatrix[3]; χ3z = Times @@ zN;
zeroM[m_] := Union[Flatten[Normal[m]]] === {0};
{tims["setup t_a"], checks["qdet: t3(x) = chi3 nu1(x) nu2(x-h) nu3(x-2h) (exact, x = 3/7)"]} = AbsoluteTiming[zeroM[tL2[3, 3/7, Zd] - χ3z nuP[1][3/7] nuP[2][3/7 - hN] nuP[3][3/7 - 2 hN] id2]];
Grid[{{"Λ", Λ}, {"dims (one site, two sites)", {d1, d2}}, {"HW pattern = index 1", ps[{Λ}][[1]]}, {"(h, θ, z, t = θ12/h)", {hN, thN, zN, tPar}}, {"quantum determinant exact", checks["qdet: t3(x) = chi3 nu1(x) nu2(x-h) nu3(x-2h) (exact, x = 3/7)"]}, {"time (s)", tims["setup t_a"]}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 5 (kind 2) =====
(*@a02*)
(* eigenstates of the twisted chain: weight sectors, joint diagonalisation of t1 + pi t2 at x = 3/7 (prec 60); eigenvalue polynomials by 7-point interpolation (degree 2a <= 6) *)
uPts = 1/7 + Range[0, 6]/3;
{tims["numeric t_a at 7 points"], tNum} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[N[tL2[a, uPts[[k]], Zd], prec], {a, 3}, {k, 7}]];
mixN = N[tL2[1, 3/7, Zd], prec] + N[Pi, prec] N[tL2[2, 3/7, Zd], prec];
secList = GatherBy[Range[d2], wtBasis[[#]] &];
{tims["eigensystems per sector"], states} = AbsoluteTiming[Flatten[Table[With[{es = Eigensystem[Normal[mixN[[sec, sec]]]]}, Table[<|"mu" -> wtBasis[[sec[[1]]]], "ev" -> es[[1, r]], "vec" -> Normal[SparseArray[Thread[sec -> es[[2, r]]], {d2}]]|>, {r, Length[sec]}]], {sec, secList}], 1]];
nS = Length[states]; muS = states[[All, "mu"]]; vecS = states[[All, "vec"]];
(* a state is a trusted eigenvector only if the three t_a polynomials reproduce it at an eighth point *)
lamPts[ψ_] := With[{k = Ordering[Abs[ψ], -1][[1]]}, Table[(tNum[[a, j]] . ψ)[[k]]/ψ[[k]], {a, 3}, {j, 7}]];
polyOf[vals_] := Expand[Sum[vals[[k]] Product[(u - uPts[[l]])/(uPts[[k]] - uPts[[l]]), {l, DeleteCases[Range[7], k]}], {k, 7}]];
{tims["eigenvalue polynomials"], ttS} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[polyOf /@ lamPts[ψ], {ψ, vecS}]];
tX = Table[N[tL2[a, 19/23, Zd], prec], {a, 3}];
resEig = Table[Max[Table[Norm[tX[[a]] . vecS[[A]] - (ttS[[A, a]] /. u -> 19/23) vecS[[A]]], {a, 3}]], {A, nS}];
gapMix = Min[Table[If[Length[g] > 1, Min[Abs[Subtract @@@ Subsets[states[[g, "ev"]], {2}]]], Infinity], {g, GatherBy[Range[nS], muS[[#]] &]}]];
checks["# states, sector sizes (max), max |t_a psi - lambda_a psi| at x = 19/23"] = {nS, Max[Length /@ secList], Max[resEig]};
checks["min gap of mix eigenvalues inside sectors"] = gapMix;
Grid[{{"states, largest sector", {nS, Max[Length /@ secList]}}, {"max eigen-residual of t_a at x = 19/23", N[Max[resEig], 3]}, {"min eigenvalue gap in sectors", N[gapMix, 3]}, {"max |Im ev|", N[Max[Abs[Im[states[[All, "ev"]]]]], 3]}, {"times (s): numeric t, eigensystems, polynomials", {tims["numeric t_a at 7 points"], tims["eigensystems per sector"], tims["eigenvalue polynomials"]}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 7 (kind 2) =====
(*@a03*)
(* dual Q's: Q^j(x) = z_j^(x/h) q^j(x), monic q^j of degree mu_j - L lambda_3 solving the dual Baxter equation (null space at prec 60) *)
opDual[tt_, Q_, x_] := nuP[3][x] nuP[3][x - hN] nuP[3][x - 2 hN] Q[x + hN] - (tt[[1]] /. u -> x) nuP[3][x - hN] nuP[3][x - 2 hN] Q[x] + (tt[[2]] /. u -> x) nuP[3][x - 2 hN] Q[x - hN] - (tt[[3]] /. u -> x) Q[x - 2 hN];
(* fixed-degree solve; returns {monic polynomial in X or {}, dimension of the null space} *)
solveQdeg[tt_, zz_, d_] := Module[{cq, eqs, ns},
  eqs = CoefficientList[Chop[Expand[opDual[tt, Function[yy, zz^((yy - X)/hN) Sum[cq[j] yy^j, {j, 0, d}]], X]], 10^-40], X];
  ns = If[eqs === {}, {UnitVector[d + 1, d + 1]}, NullSpace[N[Table[Coefficient[e, cq[j]], {e, eqs}, {j, 0, d}], prec], Tolerance -> 10^-30]];
  If[Length[ns] == 0 || Abs[ns[[1, d + 1]]] < 10^-20, {{}, Length[ns]}, {Expand[Sum[ns[[1, j + 1]] X^j, {j, 0, d}]/ns[[1, d + 1]]], Length[ns]}]];
{tims["dual Q's, all states"], qRes} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[solveQdeg[ttS[[A]], zN[[j]], muS[[A, j]] - nL Λ[[3]]], {A, nS}, {j, 3}]];
qu = qRes[[All, All, 1]]; quDim = qRes[[All, All, 2]];
checks["all dual Q's found (deg q^j = mu_j - L lambda3)"] = FreeQ[qu, {}];
checks["null space dimension of every Q (all 1?)"] = Union[Flatten[quDim]];
(* values on the ladder points: VT[[state, site, m + lambda1 + 2]] = v(theta_s + m h) = (Q^1, Q^2, Q^3) *)
mPts = Range[-1 - Λ[[1]], 0]; pPts = Length[mPts];
QU[A_, j_, x_] := N[zN[[j]]^(x/hN), prec] (qu[[A, j]] /. X -> x);
{tims["Q values on ladders"], VT} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[Table[Table[Table[QU[A, j, thN[[s]] + m hN], {j, 3}], {m, mPts}], {s, nL}], {A, nS}]];
vm[A_, s_, m_] := VT[[A, s, m + Λ[[1]] + 2]];
(* Baxter residual relative to the largest term, at x = 19/23 *)
baxResB = Table[With[{x = 19/23}, Abs[opDual[ttS[[A]], Function[y, QU[A, j, y]], x]]/Max[Abs[{nuP[3][x] nuP[3][x - hN] nuP[3][x - 2 hN] QU[A, j, x + hN], (ttS[[A, 1]] /. u -> x) nuP[3][x - hN] nuP[3][x - 2 hN] QU[A, j, x], (ttS[[A, 2]] /. u -> x) nuP[3][x - 2 hN] QU[A, j, x - hN], (ttS[[A, 3]] /. u -> x) QU[A, j, x - 2 hN]}]]], {A, nS}, {j, 3}];
checks["dual Baxter residual (relative, max)"] = Max[baxResB];
Grid[{{"all 3 x nS dual Q's found, null dims", {checks["all dual Q's found (deg q^j = mu_j - L lambda3)"], checks["null space dimension of every Q (all 1?)"]}}, {"max relative Baxter residual", N[Max[baxResB], 3]}, {"degrees of q^j: min, max", {Min[Exponent[#, X] & /@ Flatten[qu]], Max[Exponent[#, X] & /@ Flatten[qu]]}}, {"times (s): Q solve, ladder values", {tims["dual Q's, all states"], tims["Q values on ladders"]}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 9 (kind 2) =====
(*@a04*)
(* physical-space Gauss factors P_phys = Pm . diag(s) . Pp (rep-independent group data), the SoV vacuum x0 = (e1 P_phys^-1) tensor 2 and the companion-frame kets (P^-1 tensor P^-1) psi *)
ρdiag[d_] := DiagonalMatrix[Table[Product[d[[k]]^J0[k][p], {k, 3}], {p, ps[{Λ}]}]];
sVec[zv_] := {zv[[1]]^2/((zv[[1]] - zv[[2]]) (zv[[1]] - zv[[3]])), -(zv[[2]]/(zv[[1]] (zv[[2]] - zv[[3]]))), 1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])};
rVec[zv_] := {-(zv[[3]]/zv[[2]]), -(zv[[3]]/zv[[1]]), (zv[[2]]^2 (zv[[3]] - zv[[1]]))/(zv[[1]]^2 (zv[[2]] - zv[[3]]))};
Pphys[zv_] := nexp[rVec[zv][[1]] Ee[3, 2]] . nexp[rVec[zv][[2]] Ee[3, 1]] . nexp[rVec[zv][[3]] Ee[2, 1]] . ρdiag[sVec[zv]] . nexp[(1/zv[[1]]) Ee[1, 2]] . nexp[-(1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) Ee[1, 3]] . nexp[((zv[[1]] + zv[[2]])/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) Ee[2, 3]];
Pph1 = Normal[Pphys[zN]]; PphInv1 = Inverse[Pph1];
kron[a_, b_] := Flatten[KroneckerProduct[a, b]];
x01 = e1 . PphInv1; x02 = kron[x01, x01]; x0C = kron[e1, e1];
x0ovl = Table[x02 . vecS[[A]], {A, nS}];
{tims["companion kets"], ketC} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[N[Flatten[PphInv1 . Partition[vecS[[A]], d1] . Transpose[PphInv1]], prec], {A, nS}]];
checks["companion kets: <x0C|ket> = diagonal-frame <x0|psi> (max rel)"] = Max[Table[Abs[x0C . ketC[[A]] - x0ovl[[A]]]/Abs[x0ovl[[A]]], {A, nS}]];
checks["<x0|Psi> nonzero (min |.|, not a zero test)"] = Min[Abs[x0ovl]];
Grid[{{"companion kets vs diagonal frame <x0|psi> (max rel dev)", N[checks["companion kets: <x0C|ket> = diagonal-frame <x0|psi> (max rel)"], 3]}, {"min |<x0|Psi>| over states", N[checks["<x0|Psi> nonzero (min |.|, not a zero test)"], 4]}, {"time (s)", tims["companion kets"]}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 11 (kind 2) =====
(*@a05*)
(* untwisted L=2 B(u) = T11(u) qmin_{12,12}(u+h) + T21(u) qmin_{12,13}(u+h) (exact, sparse); its left eigenvectors are the SoV covectors; labels from the six roots of each eigenvalue polynomial *)
Bop2[x_] := mono2[Id3][x][[1, 1]] . qmin[Id3][{1, 2}, {1, 2}][x + hN] + mono2[Id3][x][[2, 1]] . qmin[Id3][{1, 2}, {1, 3}][x + hN];
{tims["B at 7 points (exact)"], BnumX} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[Bop2[uPts[[k]]], {k, 7}]];
BnumN = N[BnumX, prec];
u0B = 3/11;
{tims["B eigensystem (dense, prec 60)"], eigB} = AbsoluteTiming[Eigensystem[Transpose[Normal[N[Bop2[u0B], prec]]]]];
{evB, vecB} = eigB;
gapB = Min[Abs[Subtract @@@ Subsets[evB, {2}]]];
rootsOf[cvec_] := Module[{k = Ordering[Abs[cvec], -1][[1]], vals, poly},
  vals = Table[(cvec . BnumN[[j]])[[k]]/cvec[[k]], {j, 7}];
  poly = Expand[Sum[vals[[j]] Product[(u - uPts[[l]])/(uPts[[j]] - uPts[[l]]), {l, DeleteCases[Range[7], j]}], {j, 7}]];
  Sort[Chop[u /. NSolve[poly == 0, u, WorkingPrecision -> 40], 10^-20]]];
{tims["B eigenvalue roots"], sovRoots} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[rootsOf[cv], {cv, vecB}]];
(* a root belongs to ladder s if (root - theta_s)/h is an integer *)
ladderOf[r_] := Module[{t1 = (r - thN[[1]])/hN, t2 = (r - thN[[2]])/hN}, Which[Abs[t1 - Round[t1]] < 10^-15, {1, Round[t1]}, Abs[t2 - Round[t2]] < 10^-15, {2, Round[t2]}, True, {0, r}]];
lab[m_] := lab[m] = {Sort[Map[Last, Select[ladderOf /@ sovRoots[[m]], First[#] == 1 &]]], Sort[Map[Last, Select[ladderOf /@ sovRoots[[m]], First[#] == 2 &]]]};
labRule = Flatten[Table[Table[{-1 - a, -b, -c}, {b, c, a}], {a, Λ[[2]], Λ[[1]]}, {c, 0, Λ[[2]]}], 2];
vacTrip = {-1 - Λ[[1]], -Λ[[1]], -Λ[[2]]};
allLabs = Table[lab[m], {m, nS}];
checks["B: all roots on the two ladders, 3 + 3"] = And @@ (Length[#[[1]]] == 3 && Length[#[[2]]] == 3 & /@ allLabs);
checks["B: rule set size = dim V; label rule: every site triple is {-1-a,-b,-c}, 15 distinct"] = {Length[labRule], Sort[Union[allLabs[[All, 1]]]] === Sort[labRule] && Sort[Union[allLabs[[All, 2]]]] === Sort[labRule]};
checks["B: (site-1, site-2) label pairs: # distinct (225 = bijection)"] = Length[Union[allLabs]];
checks["B: vacuum triple present on both sites"] = MemberQ[allLabs, {vacTrip, vacTrip}];
vacIdx = First[Flatten[Position[allLabs, {vacTrip, vacTrip}]]];
checks["x0 = e1 tensor e1 is the left eigenvector of B with the vacuum label (exact eigen-test, parallel to vecB[vac])"] = {Norm[Normal[x0C . Bop2[u0B]] - ((x0C . Bop2[u0B])[[1]]) x0C] === 0, N[1 - Abs[Conjugate[vecB[[vacIdx]]] . x0C]/Norm[x0C], 3]};
labTable = Table[{"(a|bc) = (" <> ToString[-1 - l[[1]]] <> "|" <> ToString[-l[[2]]] <> ToString[-l[[3]]] <> ")", l}, {l, labRule}];
Grid[{{"B eigensystem: gap (min), time (s)", {N[gapB, 3], tims["B eigensystem (dense, prec 60)"]}}, {"all roots on ladders 3+3", checks["B: all roots on the two ladders, 3 + 3"]}, {"|rule| = dim V; triples = rule set (both sites)", checks["B: rule set size = dim V; label rule: every site triple is {-1-a,-b,-c}, 15 distinct"]}, {"# distinct label pairs", checks["B: (site-1, site-2) label pairs: # distinct (225 = bijection)"]}, {"vacuum label pair present, index", {checks["B: vacuum triple present on both sites"], vacIdx}}, {"label (a|bc) <-> sorted triple of root offsets (theta_s + offset h)", labTable}, {"x0 vacuum eigenvector", checks["x0 = e1 tensor e1 is the left eigenvector of B with the vacuum label (exact eigen-test, parallel to vecB[vac])"]}, {"times (s): B(7 pts), roots", {tims["B at 7 points (exact)"], tims["B eigenvalue roots"]}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 13 (kind 2) =====
(*@a06*)
(* Task 1: SoV wavefunction rule. <x_m|Psi_B>/<x0|Psi_B> = N_m prod_s M^(s)_m / (vacuum), M_(a|bc) = det2[v(theta - (1+a)h), v(theta - b h)]_rows . v_k(theta - c h), N_m state independent. All 225 states, three (rows, k) conventions. *)
{tims["SoV overlaps with kets"], ovlM} = AbsoluteTiming[vecB . Transpose[ketC]];
ratioM = Table[ovlM[[m, A]]/(x0C . ketC[[A]]), {m, nS}, {A, nS}];
labIdx[l_] := First[Flatten[Position[labRule, l]]];
siteIdx = Table[{labIdx[allLabs[[m, 1]]], labIdx[allLabs[[m, 2]]]}, {m, nS}];
(* det2[v(m21), v(m1)] v_k(m22) on rows {a,b}: one value per state, site, label *)
siteMono[rows_, k_] := Table[Table[With[{m1 = l[[1]], m21 = l[[2]], m22 = l[[3]]}, (vm[A, s, m21][[rows[[1]]]] vm[A, s, m1][[rows[[2]]]] - vm[A, s, m21][[rows[[2]]]] vm[A, s, m1][[rows[[1]]]]) vm[A, s, m22][[k]]], {l, labRule}], {A, nS}, {s, nL}];
wfTest[rows_, k_] := Module[{sm = siteMono[rows, k], pred, rr, mask, worst, nExcl, bestA, Nrow},
  pred = Table[Product[sm[[A, s, siteIdx[[m, s]]]], {s, nL}], {m, nS}, {A, nS}];
  pred = Table[pred[[m, A]]/pred[[vacIdx, A]], {m, nS}, {A, nS}];
  rr = ratioM/pred;
  bestA = Table[Ordering[Abs[pred[[m]]], -1][[1]], {m, nS}];
  Nrow = Table[rr[[m, bestA[[m]]]], {m, nS}];
  worst = Table[Max[Abs[rr[[m]]/Nrow[[m]] - 1]], {m, nS}];
  {Max[worst], Min[Abs[pred]], Nrow}];
{tims["wavefunction tests (3 conventions)"], wfRes} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[wfTest @@ rk, {rk, {{{1, 2}, 1}, {{2, 3}, 3}, {{1, 3}, 2}}}]];
checks["wavefunction rule M_x (rows {1,2},k=1; {2,3},3; {1,3},2): max rel dev over 225 labels x 225 states"] = wfRes[[All, 1]];
Nm = wfRes[[2, 3]];
checks["N_m the same for all three conventions (max rel diff)"] = Max[Abs[wfRes[[1, 3]]/wfRes[[2, 3]] - 1], Abs[wfRes[[3, 3]]/wfRes[[2, 3]] - 1]];
Grid[{{"max rel dev of <x_m|Psi_B>/<x0|Psi_B> vs N_m prod M (rows12 k1, rows23 k3, rows13 k2)", N[wfRes[[All, 1]], 3]}, {"min |predicted ratio| (zero-division guard)", N[wfRes[[All, 2]], 3]}, {"N_m identical across conventions (max rel diff)", N[checks["N_m the same for all three conventions (max rel diff)"], 3]}, {"N_m: min, max abs", N[{Min[Abs[Nm]], Max[Abs[Nm]]}, 4]}, {"time (s): overlaps, tests", {tims["SoV overlaps with kets"], tims["wavefunction tests (3 conventions)"]}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 15 (kind 2) =====
(*@a07*)
(* Task 0: the one-site vacuum covector. Omega_c[w] = e1 exp(E12/w1) exp(-E13/(w1 w2)) exp((w1+w2)/(w1 w2) E23) is the Gauss rotation gaussCov[{alpha, beta, gamma}] with alpha = 1/w1, beta = -1/(w1 w2), gamma = (w1+w2)/(w1 w2); with s = 1/w2 this is (alpha, -alpha s, alpha + s), polynomial in (alpha, s), and beta + alpha gamma = alpha^2 (first row (1, alpha, alpha^2)). All exact. *)
gaussCov[{al_, be_, ga_}] := e1 . nexp[al Ee[1, 2]] . nexp[be Ee[1, 3]] . nexp[ga Ee[2, 3]];
gaussParOf[w_] := {1/w[[1]], -1/(w[[1]] w[[2]]), (w[[1]] + w[[2]])/(w[[1]] w[[2]])};
Ωc[w_] := Normal[gaussCov[gaussParOf[Take[w, 2]]]];
PppW[y_] := MatrixExp[(1/y[[1]]) Normal[Ee[1, 2]]] . MatrixExp[-(1/(y[[1]] y[[2]])) Normal[Ee[1, 3]]] . MatrixExp[((y[[1]] + y[[2]])/(y[[1]] y[[2]])) Normal[Ee[2, 3]]];
wN = {5/2, 7/3, 3/5}; wP = {7/4, -2/5, 9/7};
checks["Omega_c[w] = e1 exp(E12/w1) exp(-E13/(w1 w2)) exp(...E23): exact, w, w', 2 more"] = Table[Union[Flatten[Ωc[w] - e1 . PppW[w]]] === {0}, {w, {wN, wP, {2/3, -5/7}, {-3/2, 4/5}}}];
ee3b[i_, j_] := Table[KroneckerDelta[i, ii] KroneckerDelta[j, jj], {ii, 3}, {jj, 3}];
Pp3[w1_, w2_] := MatrixExp[ee3b[1, 2]/w1] . MatrixExp[-ee3b[1, 3]/(w1 w2)] . MatrixExp[((w1 + w2)/(w1 w2)) ee3b[2, 3]];
checks["3x3 first row of the Gauss product = (1, 1/w1, 1/w1^2), symbolic"] = Simplify[Pp3[ww1, ww2][[1]] - {1, 1/ww1, 1/ww1^2}] === {0, 0, 0};
checks["beta + alpha gamma - alpha^2 = 0 on the vacuum Gauss parameters, symbolic"] = Simplify[gaussParOf[{ww1, ww2}][[2]] + gaussParOf[{ww1, ww2}][[1]] gaussParOf[{ww1, ww2}][[3]] - gaussParOf[{ww1, ww2}][[1]]^2] === 0;
(* (alpha, s) coordinates: Omega_c = gaussCov[{al, -al s, al + s}] *)
gaussVac[{al_, s_}] := gaussCov[{al, -al s, al + s}];
checks["Omega_c[w] = gaussVac[{1/w1, 1/w2}] (exact, w, w')"] = Table[Union[Flatten[Ωc[w] - Normal[gaussVac[{1/w[[1]], 1/w[[2]]}]]]] === {0}, {w, {wN, wP}}];
Grid[{{"Omega_c[w] = exact rep exponentials (4 points)", checks["Omega_c[w] = e1 exp(E12/w1) exp(-E13/(w1 w2)) exp(...E23): exact, w, w', 2 more"]}, {"first row (1, 1/w1, 1/w1^2) symbolic; beta + alpha gamma = alpha^2 symbolic", {checks["3x3 first row of the Gauss product = (1, 1/w1, 1/w1^2), symbolic"], checks["beta + alpha gamma - alpha^2 = 0 on the vacuum Gauss parameters, symbolic"]}}, {"Omega_c[w] = gaussVac[{1/w1, 1/w2}] (alpha, s) form", checks["Omega_c[w] = gaussVac[{1/w1, 1/w2}] (exact, w, w')"]}, {"w, w'", {wN, wP}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

(*@a08*)
(* Task 1: the two-site K tensor. All d2 GT covectors e_nu1 (x) e_nu2 (index (nu1-1) d1 + nu2 = kron[uv[nu1], uv[nu2]]) are expanded in the SoV covectors (left eigenvectors of B) by ONE LinearSolve with d2 unit right-hand sides, then multiplied by N_m (normalised-wavefunction units): e_nu1 (x) e_nu2 = sum_m Kt[nu, m] <x_m|/N_m. Rationalised (tolerance 1e-40) and stored as an exact SparseArray (rows nu, columns SoV state m). *)
{tims["K: expansion of all d2 covectors"], cAll} = AbsoluteTiming[LinearSolve[Transpose[vecB], IdentityMatrix[d2]]];
checks["K: expansion error of e_nu1 (x) e_nu2 in the SoV basis (max abs, all d2)"] = Max[Abs[Transpose[vecB] . cAll - IdentityMatrix[d2]]];
KfullT = cAll * Nm;   (* rows = SoV state m, columns = covector nu *)
ratz[x_] := Rationalize[Chop[x, 10^-40], 10^-40];
{tims["K: rationalisation"], KtRat} = AbsoluteTiming[Map[ratz, KfullT, {2}]];
checks["K: rationalisation error (max abs, prec 60 data)"] = Max[Abs[N[KtRat, prec] - KfullT]];
Kt = SparseArray[Transpose[KtRat]];
checks["K: max denominator digits; # nonzero entries of d2^2; max |entry|"] = {Max[IntegerLength /@ Denominator[Kt["NonzeroValues"]]], {Length[Kt["NonzeroValues"]], d2^2}, N[Max[Abs[Kt["NonzeroValues"]]], 4]};
{tims["K: exact rank"], rkKt} = AbsoluteTiming[MatrixRank[Kt]];
checks["K: exact rank of the d2 x d2 tensor (full = d2)"] = {rkKt, d2};
mOfPair = Association[Table[allLabs[[m]] -> m, {m, nS}]];
(* state index of the label pair (x1, x2), x in labRule order *)
mIdxMat = Table[mOfPair[{labRule[[x1]], labRule[[x2]]}], {x1, d1}, {x2, d1}];
checks["K: label pair <-> state bijection, d1 x d1 index matrix"] = Sort[Flatten[mIdxMat]] === Range[d2];
Grid[{{"expansion error", N[checks["K: expansion error of e_nu1 (x) e_nu2 in the SoV basis (max abs, all d2)"], 3]}, {"rationalisation error (60-digit data)", N[checks["K: rationalisation error (max abs, prec 60 data)"], 3]}, {"max denominator digits; {# nonzero, d2^2}; max |entry|", checks["K: max denominator digits; # nonzero entries of d2^2; max |entry|"]}, {"exact rank, d2", checks["K: exact rank of the d2 x d2 tensor (full = d2)"]}, {"bijection state <-> (x1, x2)", checks["K: label pair <-> state bijection, d1 x d1 index matrix"]}, {"times (s): solve, rationalise, rank", {tims["K: expansion of all d2 covectors"], tims["K: rationalisation"], tims["K: exact rank"]}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 19 (kind 2) =====
(*@a09*)
(* Tools for Tasks 2-5. (i) 2-form and wedge tools (general notebook cell 16): minors of the ladder points, tensorOf, amat, wedgeV, Pfaffians, decompForm. (ii) the two-site map phiMat: covector Phi (length d2) -> exact d1 x d1 matrix phi[x1, x2] of SoV coefficients (labels in labRule order). (iii) the (c, c') blocks A^(c,c') = nMin x nMin matrices (rows: ladder-1 minors (n1 n1'), columns: ladder-2 minors), the lower bound r = MatrixRank and the upper bound P = sum_k (rank u_k/2)(rank w_k/2) of the pivot rank decomposition (both orientations, smaller kept), the block-form overlap identity with explicit bracket products on all states. *)
mnL = Flatten[Table[{mPts[[i]], mPts[[j]]}, {j, 2, pPts}, {i, 1, j - 1}], 1];
nMin = Length[mnL];
mnIdxA = Association[Thread[mnL -> Range[nMin]]];
mnIdx[{n_, np_}] := mnIdxA[{n, np}];
slotOf = Table[mnIdx[{labRule[[x, 1]], labRule[[x, 2]]}], {x, d1}];
cOf = Table[-labRule[[x, 3]], {x, d1}];
labName[l_] := "(" <> ToString[-1 - l[[1]]] <> "|" <> ToString[-l[[2]]] <> ToString[-l[[3]]] <> ")";
(* tensorOf: vector over labels (labRule order) -> nMin x (lambda2+1) matrix, slot ((n,n'), c) *)
tensorOf[φ_] := Module[{CC = ConstantArray[0, {nMin, Λ[[2]] + 1}]}, Do[CC[[slotOf[[x]], cOf[[x]] + 1]] = φ[[x]], {x, d1}]; CC];
amat[A_] := Table[Which[i == j, 0, i < j, A[[mnIdx[{mPts[[i]], mPts[[j]]}]]], True, -A[[mnIdx[{mPts[[j]], mPts[[i]]}]]]], {i, pPts}, {j, pPts}];
wedgeV[F_, G_] := Flatten[Table[F[[mi]] G[[mj]] - F[[mj]] G[[mi]], {mj, 2, pPts}, {mi, 1, mj - 1}]];
pfaff[M_] := Module[{n = Length[M]}, If[n == 0, 1, If[OddQ[n], 0, Expand[Sum[(-1)^j M[[1, j]] pfaff[With[{r = Complement[Range[n], {1, j}]}, M[[r, r]]]], {j, 2, n}]]]]];
pf4list[A_] := With[{M = amat[A]}, Table[pfaff[M[[s, s]]], {s, Subsets[Range[pPts], {4}]}]];
decompForm[A_] := Module[{M = amat[A], res = {}, ij, F, G},
  While[Not[AllTrue[Flatten[M], # === 0 &]],
   ij = First[Select[Tuples[Range[pPts], 2], #[[1]] < #[[2]] && M[[#[[1]], #[[2]]]] =!= 0 &]];
   F = M[[All, ij[[1]]]]/M[[ij[[1]], ij[[2]]]]; G = M[[All, ij[[2]]]];
   AppendTo[res, {F, G}];
   M = M - Table[F[[i]] G[[j]] - F[[j]] G[[i]], {i, pPts}, {j, pPts}]];
  res];
halfRk[v_] := MatrixRank[amat[v]]/2;
(* brackets on ladder s *)
brS[s_][A_, j_, F_] := Sum[F[[i]] vm[A, s, mPts[[i]]][[j]], {i, pPts}];
bkS[s_][A_, F_, G_] := brS[s][A, 3, F] brS[s][A, 2, G] - brS[s][A, 2, F] brS[s][A, 3, G];
sm23 = siteMono[{2, 3}, 3]; vacL = labIdx[vacTrip];
(* tool checks *)
qS[j_, m_] := qq[j, m];
minSym[{n_, np_}] := qS[2, np] qS[3, n] - qS[3, np] qS[2, n];
brQ[j_, F_] := Sum[F[[i]] qS[j, mPts[[i]]], {i, pPts}];
Bk2sym[F_, G_] := brQ[3, F] brQ[2, G] - brQ[2, F] brQ[3, G];
Fs0 = Array[ff, pPts]; Gs0 = Array[gg, pPts];
checks["bracket identity Bk2[F,G] = sum (F^G)_{nn'} minor_{23}(n,n'), symbolic on all points"] = Expand[Bk2sym[Fs0, Gs0] - Sum[wedgeV[Fs0, Gs0][[i]] minSym[mnL[[i]]], {i, nMin}]] === 0;
SeedRandom[5];
rF = RandomInteger[{-5, 5}, pPts]; rG = RandomInteger[{-5, 5}, pPts]; rF2 = RandomInteger[{-5, 5}, pPts]; rG2 = RandomInteger[{-5, 5}, pPts];
checks["decompForm reconstructs a random rank-4 form"] = (Total[wedgeV[#[[1]], #[[2]]] & /@ decompForm[wedgeV[rF, rG] + wedgeV[rF2, rG2]]] === wedgeV[rF, rG] + wedgeV[rF2, rG2]);
(* the two-site map *)
phiMat[Φ_] := ArrayReshape[Normal[Φ . Kt][[Flatten[mIdxMat]]], {d1, d1}];
ovlExactTwo[Φ_, A_] := (Φ . ketC[[A]])/(x0C . ketC[[A]]);
idFull[Φ_] := Module[{ph = phiMat[Φ], lhs, rhs},
  lhs = Table[ovlExactTwo[Φ, A] sm23[[A, 1, vacL]] sm23[[A, 2, vacL]], {A, nS}];
  rhs = Table[sm23[[A, 1]] . ph . sm23[[A, 2]], {A, nS}];
  Max[Abs[lhs - rhs]]/Max[Abs[lhs]]];
(* blocks *)
blockOf[ph_] := Association[Table[{c1, cp} -> Module[{M = ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}]}, Do[If[cOf[[x1]] == c1, Do[If[cOf[[x2]] == cp, M[[slotOf[[x1]], slotOf[[x2]]]] = ph[[x1, x2]]], {x2, d1}]], {x1, d1}]; M], {c1, 0, Λ[[2]]}, {cp, 0, Λ[[2]]}]];
(* matrix-rank decomposition M = Bc . R (R = row-reduced basis, Bc = pivot columns): count sum_k (rank/2)(rank/2); both orientations, smaller kept *)
blockCountOne[M_] := Module[{R = DeleteCases[RowReduce[M], {0 ..}], piv, Bc}, If[R === {}, Return[{0, 0, {}, M, {}}]]; piv = Table[First[Flatten[Position[R[[k]], x_ /; x =!= 0, {1}, Heads -> False]]], {k, Length[R]}]; Bc = M[[All, piv]]; {Length[R], Sum[halfRk[Bc[[All, k]]] halfRk[R[[k]]], {k, Length[R]}], Table[{halfRk[Bc[[All, k]]], halfRk[R[[k]]]}, {k, Length[R]}], Bc, R}];
blockCount[M_] := With[{a = blockCountOne[M], b = blockCountOne[Transpose[M]]}, If[a[[2]] <= b[[2]], Append[a, "rows"], {b[[1]], b[[2]], Reverse /@ b[[3]], Transpose[b[[5]]], Transpose[b[[4]]], "cols"}]];
(* table row per block: {slots, r, P, {(rank u/2, rank w/2) per rank-1 term}, lower, upper both orientations} *)
blockInfo[M_] := Module[{a = blockCountOne[M], b = blockCountOne[Transpose[M]], bc = blockCount[M]}, <|"slots" -> Count[Flatten[M], x_ /; x =!= 0], "r" -> MatrixRank[M], "P" -> bc[[2]], "factors" -> bc[[3]], "Prows" -> a[[2]], "Pcols" -> b[[2]]|>];
(* the block-form identity with explicit bracket products: sum_{c,c'} v3(theta1 - c h) v3(theta2 - c' h) sum_k [sum wedges Bk2^(1)][sum wedges Bk2^(2)] = <Phi|Psi>/<x0|Psi> M1vac M2vac, all states *)
blockTerms[bg_] := Flatten[Table[Module[{bc = blockCount[bg[{c1, cp}]]}, If[bc[[2]] == 0, {}, Flatten[Table[Flatten[Table[{c1, cp, u, w}, {u, decompForm[bc[[4, All, k]]]}, {w, decompForm[bc[[5, k]]]}], 1], {k, Length[bc[[5]]]}], 1]]], {c1, 0, Λ[[2]]}, {cp, 0, Λ[[2]]}], 2];
idBlock[Φ_, bg_] := Module[{lhs, rhs, terms = blockTerms[bg]},
  lhs = Table[ovlExactTwo[Φ, A] sm23[[A, 1, vacL]] sm23[[A, 2, vacL]], {A, nS}];
  rhs = Table[Sum[vm[A, 1, -t[[1]]][[3]] vm[A, 2, -t[[2]]][[3]] bkS[1][A, t[[3, 1]], t[[3, 2]]] bkS[2][A, t[[4, 1]], t[[4, 2]]], {t, terms}], {A, nS}];
  {Length[terms], Max[Abs[lhs - rhs]]/Max[Abs[lhs]]}];
(* one-site column counts (rank/2 summed over the (group, column) 2-forms): site-1 tensors per site-2 label x2 and site-2 tensors per site-1 label x1 of a coefficient matrix phi *)
perLabelCount[ph_, site_] := Total[Flatten[Table[Table[MatrixRank[amat[tensorOf[If[site == 1, ph[[All, x]], ph[[x]]]][[All, c + 1]]]]/2, {c, 0, Λ[[2]]}], {x, d1}]]];
gen0 = {2/3, -3/7, 5/4}; gen1 = {-1/2, 4/9, 3/5};
covGen = Normal[gaussCov[gen0]]; covGen1 = Normal[gaussCov[gen1]];
SeedRandom[11]; covRnd = RandomInteger[{-9, 9}, d1]; covRnd2 = RandomInteger[{-9, 9}, d1];
phTest = phiMat[kron[covGen, covGen1]];
bgTest = blockOf[phTest];
{tims["tools: one block count + identity (generic pair)"], idTest} = AbsoluteTiming[{idFull[kron[covGen, covGen1]], idBlock[kron[covGen, covGen1], bgTest]}];
Grid[{{"bracket identity Bk2 = sum (F^G) minor (symbolic, all points)", checks["bracket identity Bk2[F,G] = sum (F^G)_{nn'} minor_{23}(n,n'), symbolic on all points"]}, {"decompForm reconstructs a random rank-4 form", checks["decompForm reconstructs a random rank-4 form"]}, {"ladder points, nMin (block size), # blocks", {mPts, nMin, (Λ[[2]] + 1)^2}}, {"generic pair (g1, g2) = ((2/3,-3/7,5/4), (-1/2,4/9,3/5)): phi-form identity (all states), block-form identity {# products, rel dev}", {N[idTest[[1]], 3], {idTest[[2, 1]], N[idTest[[2, 2]], 3]}}}, {"time (s)", tims["tools: one block count + identity (generic pair)"]}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 22 (kind 2) =====
(*@a10*)
(* Task 0: the (inf,inf) family Phi(a, a') = gaussCov[{a,0,a}] (x) gaussCov[{a',0,a'}] = Omega_c[(1/a, inf)] (x) Omega_c[(1/a', inf)]. phiSym builds the d1 x d1 matrix of SoV coefficients phi[x1, x2] = sum Kt[nu1 nu2, m] c1_nu1 c2_nu2 for SYMBOLIC covectors (entries are polynomials in a, a'); blockOf cuts it into the four (c, c') blocks A^(cc') (rows: ladder-1 minors (n n'), columns: ladder-2 minors). Reproduces Stage B: ranks 3,3,2,2, pivot-count P = 4,4,2,2 (sum 12), identity on all 64 states with explicit bracket products at three (a, a') points. *)
nzPos = Kt["NonzeroPositions"]; nzVal = Kt["NonzeroValues"];
phiSym[c1_, c2_] := Module[{v = ConstantArray[0, d2], nu, m, n1, n2}, Do[nu = nzPos[[k, 1]]; m = nzPos[[k, 2]]; n1 = Quotient[nu - 1, d1] + 1; n2 = Mod[nu - 1, d1] + 1; v[[m]] += nzVal[[k]] c1[[n1]] c2[[n2]], {k, Length[nzPos]}]; ArrayReshape[Map[Expand, v][[Flatten[mIdxMat]]], {d1, d1}]];
cInf[a_] := Normal[gaussCov[{a, 0, a}]];
cS1 = Expand /@ cInf[aS]; cS2 = Expand /@ cInf[bS];
{tims["symbolic phi (a, a')"], phPl} = AbsoluteTiming[phiSym[cS1, cS2]];
bgPl = blockOf[phPl];
checks["limit: Omega_c[{w1, w2}] -> gaussCov[{1/w1,0,1/w1}] as w2 -> infinity (symbolic, s = 0 of gaussVac)"] = (Expand /@ Normal[gaussVac[{aa, ss}]] /. ss -> 0) === Expand /@ cInf[aa];
checks["symbolic phi at a rational pair equals the numeric phi (exact)"] = (phPl /. {aS -> 2/3, bS -> -5/7}) === phiMat[kron[cInf[2/3], cInf[-5/7]]];
checks["entries of the blocks: max total degree in (a, a'); # nonzero slots per block"] = {Max[Exponent[#, {aS, bS}] & /@ Flatten[phPl]] // Max, Table[Count[Flatten[bgPl[k]], x_ /; x =!= 0], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]};
SeedRandom[71];
rr[] := RandomChoice[{-3, -2, -3/2, -1, -1/2, 1/2, 1, 3/2, 2, 3, 5/2, 4}];
rkPl = Max /@ Transpose[Table[Table[MatrixRank[bgPl[k] /. {aS -> rr[], bS -> rr[]}], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], {3}]];
pts0 = {{2/3, -5/7}, {3/2, 1/4}, {-4/5, 7/3}};
infoAt[pt_] := Association[Table[k -> blockInfo[bgPl[k] /. {aS -> pt[[1]], bS -> pt[[2]]}], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]];
{tims["block counts at 3 points"], infos0} = AbsoluteTiming[infoAt /@ pts0];
idAt[pt_] := Module[{Φ = kron[cInf[pt[[1]]], cInf[pt[[2]]]], bg = Association[Table[k -> bgPl[k] /. {aS -> pt[[1]], bS -> pt[[2]]}, {k, Keys[bgPl]}]]}, {idFull[Φ], idBlock[Φ, bg]}];
ptsId = {{2/3, -5/7}, {5/7, -4/9}, {-3/5, 7/4}};   (* the first point is the Stage B point (a = 1/z3 makes 22 states orthogonal to the covector: w1 = z_k is a degenerate vacuum); the other two are generic *)
nzCount[pt_] := Module[{Φ = kron[cInf[pt[[1]]], cInf[pt[[2]]]]}, Count[Abs[Table[ovlExactTwo[Φ, A], {A, nS}]], x_ /; x > 10^-30]];
{tims["identities (64 states)"], idRes0} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[Append[idAt[pt], nzCount[pt]], {pt, ptsId}]];
checks["T0 ranks per block (generic over the plane), P per block and sum at the 3 points, identity {# products, dev}"] = {rkPl, Table[Total[#["P"] & /@ Values[i]], {i, infos0}], idRes0[[All, 2, 1]], Max[idRes0[[All, 2, 2]]], idRes0[[All, 3]]};
Grid[{{"limit Omega_c -> gaussCov[{a,0,a}] (symbolic)", checks["limit: Omega_c[{w1, w2}] -> gaussCov[{1/w1,0,1/w1}] as w2 -> infinity (symbolic, s = 0 of gaussVac)"]}, {"symbolic phi at (2/3, -5/7) = numeric phi", checks["symbolic phi at a rational pair equals the numeric phi (exact)"]}, {"max total degree in (a, a'); # nonzero slots per block (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)", checks["entries of the blocks: max total degree in (a, a'); # nonzero slots per block"]}, {"generic ranks r per block (max of 3 random points)", rkPl}, Sequence @@ Table[{"point (a, a') = " <> ToString[pts0[[i]], InputForm] <> ": r, P, P rows/cols per block", {Table[infos0[[i]][k]["r"], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], Table[infos0[[i]][k]["P"], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], {Table[infos0[[i]][k]["Prows"], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], Table[infos0[[i]][k]["Pcols"], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]}, "sum r = " <> ToString[Total[#["r"] & /@ Values[infos0[[i]]]]] <> ", sum P = " <> ToString[Total[#["P"] & /@ Values[infos0[[i]]]]]}}, {i, 3}], {"overlap identity on all 64 states at (a, a') = (2/3,-5/7), (5/7,-4/9), (-3/5,7/4): {phi-form rel dev, {# products, block-form rel dev}, # states with nonzero overlap}", N[idRes0, 3]}, {"time (s)", {tims["symbolic phi (a, a')"], tims["block counts at 3 points"], tims["identities (64 states)"]}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 25 (kind 1) =====
## Task 1: minimal number of bracket products and the closed-form wedge decomposition in (a, a')

Cells: 26 (how few products: scan of all pivot choices), 28 (scaling law, kernels kappa(s), closed forms), 30 (numeric identity on 64 states, structure). Notation: a block A = sum_k u_k (x) w_k (u_k = ladder-1 2-forms = columns, w_k = ladder-2 2-forms = rows); each rank-2 (decomposable) 2-form is ONE wedge F ^ G, each rank-4 one TWO wedges, so a term costs (rank u_k / 2)(rank w_k / 2) wedge products; the lower bound is sum_cc' rank = 10.

===== cell 26 (kind 2) =====
(*@a11*)
(* Task 1a: how few wedge products can the (inf,inf) blocks be written with? A block A = sum_k u_k (x) w_k (u_k ladder-1 2-forms, w_k ladder-2 2-forms) costs (rank u_k / 2)(rank w_k / 2) wedge products per term (rank 2 = decomposable = 1 wedge, rank 4 = 2 wedges; rank 4 <=> Pfaffian nonzero). The 'pivot' decompositions are M = U R with U = r independent columns of M and R = (the r x r submatrix of M on those columns and some independent rows)^-1 (those rows of M): the 'rows' orientation takes pivot columns of M (u_k = raw columns, w_k = normalised rows of R), the 'cols' orientation does the same for M^T (w_k raw, u_k normalised). Scan ALL independent pivot sets in both orientations at a generic rational point and report the product count; the lower bound is rank M (all terms decomposable on both sides). *)
ptG = {aS -> 7/5, bS -> -4/3};
(* slot order of mnL: (12),(13),(23),(14),(24),(34) on the ladder points; Pf = x12 x34 - x13 x24 + x14 x23 *)
pfOf[v_] := v[[1]] v[[6]] - v[[2]] v[[5]] + v[[4]] v[[3]];
checks["Pf slot formula: pfOf = Pfaffian of amat on 4 points (random vector)"] = With[{vv = RandomInteger[{-9, 9}, 6]}, pfOf[vv] === pfaff[amat[vv]]];
rank2[v_] := If[Together[pfOf[v]] === 0, 1, 2];
pivSets[M_] := Select[Subsets[Range[Dimensions[M][[2]]], {MatrixRank[M]}], MatrixRank[M[[All, #]]] == MatrixRank[M] &];
rowSetFor[M_, piv_] := First[Select[Subsets[Range[Dimensions[M][[1]]], {MatrixRank[M]}], MatrixRank[M[[#, piv]]] == MatrixRank[M] &]];
(* decomposition of M by pivot columns piv: list of {raw column, normalised row} *)
pivDecomp[M_, piv_] := Module[{prow = rowSetFor[M, piv], R},
  R = Map[Together, Inverse[M[[prow, piv]]] . M[[prow, All]], {2}];
  Table[{M[[All, piv[[k]]]], R[[k]]}, {k, Length[piv]}]];
(* orientation "rows": u = raw column, w = normalised row; "cols": decompose M^T, u = normalised, w = raw *)
terms2[M_, orient_, piv_] := If[orient === "rows", pivDecomp[M, piv], Reverse /@ pivDecomp[Transpose[M], piv]];
countTerms[tt_] := Total[Table[rank2[t[[1]]] rank2[t[[2]]], {t, tt}]];
scanBlock[k_] := Module[{M = bgPl[k] /. ptG, a, b},
  a = Table[{piv, countTerms[terms2[M, "rows", piv]]}, {piv, pivSets[M]}];
  b = Table[{piv, countTerms[terms2[M, "cols", piv]]}, {piv, pivSets[Transpose[M]]}];
  <|"rows" -> {Min[a[[All, 2]]], Max[a[[All, 2]]], Length[a]}, "cols" -> {Min[b[[All, 2]]], Max[b[[All, 2]]], Length[b]}, "bestRows" -> Select[a, #[[2]] == Min[a[[All, 2]]] &][[All, 1]], "bestCols" -> Select[b, #[[2]] == Min[b[[All, 2]]] &][[All, 1]], "all" -> {a, b}|>];
{tims["pivot scan"], scanRes} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> scanBlock[k], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]]];
(* the overall minimum per block over both orientations, and the winners *)
bestOf[k_] := With[{r = scanRes[k]}, If[r["rows"][[1]] <= r["cols"][[1]], {r["rows"][[1]], "rows", r["bestRows"]}, {r["cols"][[1]], "cols", r["bestCols"]}]];
bestAll = Association[Table[k -> bestOf[k], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]];
checks["T1 minimal product count per block (both orientations, all pivot sets) and total; lower bound sum r"] = {Table[bestAll[k][[1]], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], Total[bestAll[#][[1]] & /@ Keys[bgPl]], Total[MatrixRank[bgPl[#] /. ptG] & /@ Keys[bgPl]]};
Grid[{{"Pf slot formula check", checks["Pf slot formula: pfOf = Pfaffian of amat on 4 points (random vector)"]}, {"generic point (a, a')", ptG}, Sequence @@ Table[{"block " <> ToString[k] <> ": rank; rows orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-column sets}; cols orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-row sets}", {MatrixRank[bgPl[k] /. ptG], scanRes[k]["rows"], scanRes[k]["cols"]}}, {k, Keys[bgPl]}], {"winner per block {P, orientation, # winning pivot sets}", Table[k -> {bestAll[k][[1]], bestAll[k][[2]], Length[bestAll[k][[3]]]}, {k, Keys[bgPl]}]}, {"sum P (best), sum r (lower bound)", checks["T1 minimal product count per block (both orientations, all pivot sets) and total; lower bound sum r"][[2 ;; 3]]}, {"distribution of P over the pivot sets of the cols orientation, blocks (0,0) and (0,1)", {Tally[scanRes[{0, 0}]["all"][[2, All, 2]]], Tally[scanRes[{0, 1}]["all"][[2, All, 2]]]}}, {"time (s)", tims["pivot scan"]}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 27 (kind 1) =====
*Printed output of the code cell `a11_t1_search` (evaluated in the shared kernel K1 from exactly the saved source; not a native notebook output). Rows of the printed `Grid` as InputForm; first entry = cell id, second = time in s. In this text `a` = aS, `a'` = bS.*

```
{"a11_t1_search", 0.16, {{"Pf slot formula check", True}, {"generic point (a, a')", {a -> 7/5, a' -> -4/3}}, 
  {"block {0, 0}: rank; rows orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-column sets}; cols orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-row sets}", {3, {4, 4, 1}, {3, 5, 7}}}, 
  {"block {0, 1}: rank; rows orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-column sets}; cols orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-row sets}", {3, {4, 4, 1}, {3, 5, 7}}}, 
  {"block {1, 0}: rank; rows orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-column sets}; cols orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-row sets}", {2, {2, 2, 3}, {2, 2, 3}}}, 
  {"block {1, 1}: rank; rows orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-column sets}; cols orientation {min P, max P, # pivot-row sets}", {2, {2, 2, 3}, {2, 2, 3}}}, 
  {"winner per block {P, orientation, # winning pivot sets}", {{0, 0} -> {3, "cols", 2}, {0, 1} -> {3, "cols", 2}, {1, 0} -> {2, "rows", 3}, {1, 1} -> {2, "rows", 3}}}, 
  {"sum P (best), sum r (lower bound)", {10, 10}}, {"distribution of P over the pivot sets of the cols orientation, blocks (0,0) and (0,1)", {{{5, 1}, {4, 4}, {3, 2}}, {{5, 1}, {4, 4}, {3, 2}}}}, 
  {"time (s)", 0.136836}}}
```

===== cell 28 (kind 2) =====
(*@a12*)
(* Task 1b: closed forms in (a, a') (aS = a, bS = a'). STEP 1, the scaling law: every block entry is homogeneous in (a, a') and A^(cc')_{(m m'),(n n')}(a, a') = a^(m+m'+e) a'^(n+n'+e') kappa^(cc')_{(m m'),(n n')}(s), s = a'/a, kappa a POLYNOMIAL in s (the exponents e + e' = E fixed by homogeneity, e' = lowest power of s over the block so that kappa has no negative powers). STEP 2: the minimal-P pivot decompositions of Task 1a redone SYMBOLICALLY on the kernels kappa(s) (the pivot sets are generic); candidates ranked by LeafCount of the factored wedge vectors, every 2-form checked to be decomposable (Pfaffian identically zero) and split into a wedge F ^ G in the gauge F_i = 0, F_j = -1, G_j = 0 (pivot (i, j) a nonzero slot, F = M[All, i]/M_ij, G = M[All, j]). STEP 3: back to the raw vectors, F_m = a^m f_m(s), G_m = a^(m+e) g_m(s), F'_n = a'^n f'_n(s), G'_n = a'^(n+e') g'_n(s). *)
tog[x_] := Together[x];
slotSum[{n_, np_}] := n + np;
totDeg[p_] := Exponent[Expand[p] /. {aS -> lam aS, bS -> lam bS}, lam];
kernelOf[k_] := Module[{A = bgPl[k], E0, ent, k0, kap, homog},
  ent = Flatten[Table[With[{e = tog[A[[i, j]]]}, If[e === 0, Nothing, {i, j, e}]], {i, nMin}, {j, nMin}], 1];
  E0 = Union[Table[totDeg[x[[3]]] - slotSum[mnL[[x[[1]]]]] - slotSum[mnL[[x[[2]]]]], {x, ent}]];
  homog = AllTrue[ent, (Exponent[Expand[#[[3]]] /. {aS -> lam aS, bS -> lam bS}, lam, Min] == totDeg[#[[3]]]) &];
  kap = Table[If[A[[i, j]] === 0, 0, tog[(A[[i, j]] /. bS -> sS aS)/(aS^(slotSum[mnL[[i]]] + slotSum[mnL[[j]]] + First[E0]) sS^slotSum[mnL[[j]]])]], {i, nMin}, {j, nMin}];
  k0 = Min[Table[If[x === 0, Infinity, Exponent[Numerator[x], sS, Min] - Exponent[Denominator[x], sS, Min]], {x, Flatten[kap]}]];
  <|"E" -> First[E0], "Euniq" -> Length[E0] == 1, "homog" -> homog, "e'" -> k0, "e" -> First[E0] - k0, "kappa" -> Map[tog, kap/sS^k0, {2}]|>];
kern = Association[Table[k -> kernelOf[k], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]];
(* the law checked exactly: reconstruct each block from kappa *)
lawOK = Table[k -> (Map[tog, Table[aS^(slotSum[mnL[[i]]] + kern[k]["e"]) bS^(slotSum[mnL[[j]]] + kern[k]["e'"]) kern[k]["kappa"][[i, j]] /. sS -> bS/aS, {i, nMin}, {j, nMin}] - bgPl[k], {2}] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}]), {k, Keys[bgPl]}];
kapPoly = Table[k -> AllTrue[Flatten[kern[k]["kappa"]], PolynomialQ[#, sS] &], {k, Keys[bgPl]}];
(* kernel pivot scan at a generic s (same machinery as Task 1a) *)
bgSave = bgPl; bgPl = Association[Table[k -> kern[k]["kappa"], {k, Keys[bgSave]}]];
ptG = {sS -> 5/3};
{tims["kernel pivot scan"], scanK} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> scanBlock[k], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]]];
scanRes = scanK; bestK = Association[Table[k -> bestOf[k], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]];
bestAll = If[AssociationQ[forcedBest], forcedBest, bestK];
(* a wedge in the gauge, symbolic: minimal LeafCount over the admissible pivots *)
wedgeSym[v_] := Module[{M = Map[tog, amat[v], {2}], cands},
  cands = Table[With[{Fv = tog /@ (M[[All, p[[1]]]]/M[[p[[1]], p[[2]]]]), Gv = M[[All, p[[2]]]]}, {Fv, Gv}], {p, Select[Subsets[Range[pPts], {2}], M[[#[[1]], #[[2]]]] =!= 0 &]}];
  First[SortBy[cands, LeafCount[Map[Factor, #, {2}]] &]]];
candInfo[k_, orient_, piv_] := Module[{tt = Map[tog, terms2[bgPl[k], orient, piv], {3}], dec, cost},
  If[! AllTrue[Flatten[Table[{tog[pfOf[t[[1]]]], tog[pfOf[t[[2]]]]}, {t, tt}]], # === 0 &], Return[$Failed]];
  dec = Table[{wedgeSym[t[[1]]], wedgeSym[t[[2]]]}, {t, tt}];
  <|"orient" -> orient, "piv" -> piv, "dec" -> dec, "cost" -> LeafCount[Map[Factor, dec, {4}]]|>];
cfCand = Association[Table[k -> DeleteCases[Table[candInfo[k, bestAll[k][[2]], piv], {piv, bestAll[k][[3]]}], $Failed], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]];
{tims["closed-form candidates"], cfBest} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> First[SortBy[cfCand[k], #["cost"] &]], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]]];
reconBlock[k_] := Total[Table[Outer[Times, wedgeV[t[[1, 1]], t[[1, 2]]], wedgeV[t[[2, 1]], t[[2, 2]]]], {t, cfBest[k]["dec"]}]];
cfResid = Table[k -> (Map[tog, reconBlock[k] - bgPl[k], {2}] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}]), {k, Keys[bgPl]}];
(* tilde-frame products {c, c', f, g, f', g'} (functions of s) *)
prodTilde = Flatten[Table[Map[{k[[1]], k[[2]], #[[1, 1]], #[[1, 2]], #[[2, 1]], #[[2, 2]]} &, cfBest[k]["dec"]], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], 1];
(* raw vectors: F_m = a^m f_m, G_m = a^(m+e) g_m (m = -3..0), F'_n = a'^n f'_n, G'_n = a'^(n+e') g'_n, s = a'/a *)
rawOf[p_] := Module[{k = {p[[1]], p[[2]]}, e = kern[{p[[1]], p[[2]]}]["e"], ep = kern[{p[[1]], p[[2]]}]["e'"]},
  {p[[1]], p[[2]], tog /@ (Table[aS^mPts[[i]], {i, pPts}] p[[3]] /. sS -> bS/aS), tog /@ (Table[aS^(mPts[[i]] + e), {i, pPts}] p[[4]] /. sS -> bS/aS), tog /@ (Table[bS^mPts[[i]], {i, pPts}] p[[5]] /. sS -> bS/aS), tog /@ (Table[bS^(mPts[[i]] + ep), {i, pPts}] p[[6]] /. sS -> bS/aS)}];
prodList = rawOf /@ prodTilde;
bgPl = bgSave;
cfResidRaw = Table[k -> (Map[tog, Total[Table[Outer[Times, wedgeV[p[[3]], p[[4]]], wedgeV[p[[5]], p[[6]]]], {p, Select[prodList, {#[[1]], #[[2]]} === k &]}]] - bgPl[k], {2}] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}]), {k, Keys[bgPl]}];
fmtV[v_] := Map[Factor, v];
Grid[Join[{{"homogeneous entries, E = e + e' unique per block", Table[k -> {kern[k]["homog"], kern[k]["Euniq"]}, {k, Keys[bgPl]}]}, {"(e, e') per block, E", Table[k -> {kern[k]["e"], kern[k]["e'"], kern[k]["E"]}, {k, Keys[bgPl]}]}, {"scaling law A = a^(m+m'+e) a'^(n+n'+e') kappa(a'/a) reproduces every block exactly", lawOK}, {"kappa polynomial in s", kapPoly}, {"minimal P per block on the kernels (same as on the blocks)", Table[k -> bestK[k][[1 ;; 2]], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]}, {"closed forms in the s-frame: every block reconstructed exactly from its wedge products (kappa), and the raw products reconstruct the (a, a') blocks", {cfResid, cfResidRaw}}, {"orientation and pivot set per block", Table[k -> {cfBest[k]["orient"], cfBest[k]["piv"]}, {k, Keys[bgPl]}]}, {"# products", Length[prodList]}, {"times (s)", {tims["kernel pivot scan"], tims["closed-form candidates"]}}, {"s-frame products {f, g, f', g'} over the ladder points m = -3..0 resp. n = -3..0; F_m = a^m f_m, G_m = a^(m+e) g_m, F'_n = a'^n f'_n, G'_n = a'^(n+e') g'_n; sS = a'/a", ""}}, Table[With[{p = prodTilde[[i]]}, {"(" <> ToString[p[[1]]] <> "," <> ToString[p[[2]]] <> ") #" <> ToString[i], {"f" -> fmtV[p[[3]]], "g" -> fmtV[p[[4]]], "f'" -> fmtV[p[[5]]], "g'" -> fmtV[p[[6]]]}}], {i, Length[prodTilde]}]], Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 30 (kind 2) =====
(*@a13*)
(* Task 1c: verification and structure of the closed forms. (i) the 10 products (raw vectors F, G, F', G' in (a, a')) reproduce the overlap <Phi|Psi>/<x0|Psi> M1_vac M2_vac on ALL 64 states at three (a, a') points (relative deviation, explicit bracket products: sum_{cc'} Q^3(theta1 - c h) Q^3(theta2 - c' h) Bk2^(1)[F, G] Bk2^(2)[F', G']); (ii) structure: for every vector, which entries vanish, which are monomials in (a, a') (const x a^i a'^j), which have non-monomial s-dependence and their factors; the a-/a'-power laws F_m = a^m f_m(s) are exact by construction; (iii) the rank-drop loci of the kernels kappa(s): gcd of all r x r minors (zeros in s), and the exact ranks and product counts on the lines a = a' (s = 1), a' = 0 (s = 0), a = 0 (s = infinity: read from the (a, a') blocks). *)
evP[pt_] := Map[N[# /. {aS -> pt[[1]], bS -> pt[[2]]}, prec] &, prodList, {2}];
idProd[pt_] := Module[{Φ = kron[cInf[pt[[1]]], cInf[pt[[2]]]], terms = evP[pt], lhs, rhs},
  lhs = Table[ovlExactTwo[Φ, A] sm23[[A, 1, vacL]] sm23[[A, 2, vacL]], {A, nS}];
  rhs = Table[Sum[vm[A, 1, -t[[1]]][[3]] vm[A, 2, -t[[2]]][[3]] bkS[1][A, t[[3]], t[[4]]] bkS[2][A, t[[5]], t[[6]]], {t, terms}], {A, nS}];
  {Length[terms], Max[Abs[lhs - rhs]]/Max[Abs[lhs]], Count[Abs[lhs], x_ /; x > 10^-30 Max[Abs[lhs]]], Min[Select[Abs[lhs], # > 10^-30 Max[Abs[lhs]] &]]/Max[Abs[lhs]]}];
{tims["closed-form identity, 3 points"], idCF} = AbsoluteTiming[idProd /@ {{5/7, -4/9}, {-3/5, 7/4}, {9/5, 2/7}}];
(* entry classification in the s-frame *)
classOf[x_] := Which[x === 0, "0", FreeQ[x, sS], "const", MatchQ[Together[x/sS^Exponent[Numerator[Together[x]], sS, Min]], _?NumberQ] || (Head[Numerator[Together[x]]] =!= Plus && Head[Denominator[Together[x]]] =!= Plus), "monomial in s", True, "non-monomial"];
classTab = Table[With[{p = prodTilde[[i]]}, {i, {p[[1]], p[[2]]}, Table[Tally[classOf /@ p[[j]]], {j, 3, 6}]}], {i, Length[prodTilde]}];
classTotals = Tally[Flatten[Table[classOf /@ p[[j]], {p, prodTilde}, {j, 3, 6}]]];
rawFmt = Table[{p[[1]], p[[2]], Factor /@ p[[3]], Factor /@ p[[4]], Factor /@ p[[5]], Factor /@ p[[6]]}, {p, prodList}];
nonMono = Flatten[Table[With[{p = prodTilde[[i]]}, Table[If[classOf[p[[j, m]]] === "non-monomial", {i, {"f", "g", "f'", "g'"}[[j - 2]], mPts[[m]], FactorList[Numerator[Together[p[[j, m]]]]][[All, 1]] // DeleteCases[#, _?NumberQ] &, FactorList[Denominator[Together[p[[j, m]]]]][[All, 1]] // DeleteCases[#, _?NumberQ] &}, Nothing], {j, 3, 6}, {m, pPts}]], {i, Length[prodTilde]}], 2];
(* the kernels kappa(s): determinantal rank-drop loci *)
kapOf[k_] := kern[k]["kappa"];
dropLoc[k_] := Module[{r = MatrixRank[kapOf[k] /. sS -> 5/3], mins}, mins = DeleteCases[Flatten[Minors[kapOf[k], r]], 0]; {r, Factor[PolynomialGCD @@ Take[mins, UpTo[400]]]}];
{tims["rank-drop loci"], dropK} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[k -> dropLoc[k], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]];
lineCount[pt_] := Module[{inf = Association[Table[k -> blockInfo[bgPl[k] /. pt], {k, Keys[bgPl]}]]}, {Table[inf[k]["r"], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], Table[inf[k]["P"], {k, Keys[bgPl]}], Total[#["r"] & /@ Values[inf]], Total[#["P"] & /@ Values[inf]]}];
lines = {"a = a' (s = 1)" -> lineCount[{aS -> 5/7, bS -> 5/7}], "a' = 0 (s = 0)" -> lineCount[{aS -> 5/7, bS -> 0}], "a = 0 (s = inf)" -> lineCount[{aS -> 0, bS -> 5/7}], "generic" -> lineCount[{aS -> 5/7, bS -> -3/4}]};
Grid[{{"identity on all 64 states at (a, a') = (5/7,-4/9), (-3/5,7/4), (9/5,2/7): {# products, max rel dev, # states with nonzero overlap (of 64; the others vanish identically), smallest nonzero |lhs|/max |lhs|}", N[idCF, 3]}, {"time (s) identity", tims["closed-form identity, 3 points"]}, {"entry classes of the s-frame vectors per product {(c,c'), tallies for f, g, f', g'}", classTab}, {"tally of the 160 vector entries of the ten products by class (s-frame)", classTotals}, {"non-monomial entries {product #, vector, m, numerator factors, denominator factors}", nonMono}, {"rank-drop loci of the kernels: {generic rank, gcd of all r x r minors, factored} per block (zeros in s)", dropK}, {"ranks r per block, P per block, sum r, sum P, on the special lines (blocks (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1))", lines}, {"RAW closed forms: products {c, c', F, G (ladder 1, m = -3..0), F', G' (ladder 2, n = -3..0)} as rational functions of (a, a') = (aS, bS)", rawFmt}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 32 (kind 1) =====
### Task 1 results

**Count resolved: 10 products, not 12.** The 12 of Stage B (P = 4, 4, 2, 2) is the count of the DEFAULT pivot choice of `RowReduce`: in blocks (0,0) and (0,1) one of the three terms has a ladder-1 factor of rank 4 (two wedges). The "3 + 3 + 2 + 2 = 10" is the number of rank-1 TERMS u_k (x) w_k, which is the lower bound Sigma r. Scanning ALL independent pivot sets in both orientations (cell 26, generic point (a, a') = (7/5, -4/3)): rows orientation (pivot columns of the 6 x 6 block) has only one admissible set for the rank-3 blocks (P = 4); columns orientation (pivot rows) has 7 sets with P = 3 (twice), 4 (four times), 5 (once); for the rank-2 blocks every one of the 3 pivot sets gives P = 2 in both orientations. So the minimum is 3, 3, 2, 2: **ten products (= Sigma r), attained exactly**: for the rank-3 blocks every one of the 3 + 3 two-forms is decomposable. The decomposability is verified symbolically: the Pfaffians of all 20 ladder 2-forms vanish identically in (a, a') (cell 28 returns $Failed otherwise, and the blocks are reconstructed exactly from the products). Winning choices: blocks (0,0), (0,1): columns orientation, pivot rows {2,3,4} (slots (13), (23), (14)); (1,0): rows orientation, pivot columns {1,3}; (1,1): rows, {2,3}. Overlap identity of the ten products on all 64 states at three generic points (5/7,-4/9), (-3/5,7/4), (9/5,2/7): < 1e-45, all 64 overlaps nonzero (cell 30).

**Scaling law (exact; the a-/a'-power laws and the only coupling variable).** Every block entry is a homogeneous polynomial of (a, a'), and
A^(cc')_{(m m'),(n n')}(a, a') = a^(m+m'+e) a'^(n+n'+e') kappa^(cc')_{(m m'),(n n')}(s), s = a'/a, kappa a polynomial in s,
with (e, e') = (7,5), (7,4), (6,5), (6,4) for (c,c') = (0,0), (0,1), (1,0), (1,1) (e + e' = 12, 11, 11, 10). So on ladder 1 every wedge vector is F_m = a^m f_m(s), G_m = a^(m+e) g_m(s) (the bra twist a^((u-theta1)/h)) and on ladder 2 F'_n = a'^n f'_n(s), G'_n = a'^(n+e') g'_n(s): **the two ladders couple only through the single variable s = a'/a**; the whole (a, a') plane is a one-parameter family of kernels. (This is the torus covariance: Phi(a) = e1 rho(exp(aE12) exp(aE23)) is Phi(1) conjugated by the diagonal torus, which rescales the SoV covectors by monomials.)

**Closed forms.** The ten products {c, c', f, g, f', g'} in the s-frame at t = 62/35, with F_m = a^m f_m etc., are printed in cell 29; the RAW rational functions of (a, a') (F, G over m = -3..0, F', G' over n = -3..0; e.g. block (0,0) product 1 has G = (a^4, 0, 0, 0), F = (0, (169136 a - 349071 a')(a - a')/(9 a^2 (8184 a - 11579 a') a'), -1/a, 0)) are in the last row of cell 31; the general-t version, exact in (s, t), is in cell 62. Reconstruction of the (a, a') blocks from the raw products is exact (`cfResidRaw` in cell 28). Gauge: pivot (i, j) = a nonzero slot of the antisymmetric matrix, F_i = 0, F_j = -1, G_j = 0. Structure (cell 31): of the 160 vector entries of the ten products, 92 vanish identically, 33 are constants and 31 are monomials c s^k (i.e. monomials in a and a' jointly: F_m ~ a^(m-k) a'^k); only FOUR are non-monomial in s (f'_(-2) of product 4, f'_(-3) of product 5, g_(-2) of product 6, f_(-2) of product 8). Many vectors are delta functions (g = (1,0,0,0), f = (0,-1,0,0), ...). The factor (a - a') = a(1 - s) appears in many entries; the pole 1/(s-1) occurs once (product 7, f'_(-2) = 132/(97 (s-1)) at t = 62/35); the other poles in s sit at roots of pivot minors (cell 62).

**Rank-drop loci.** gcd of all r x r minors of the kernels: (0,0): (s-1) s^2, (0,1): (s-1) s^3, (1,0), (1,1): s. On the lines: a = a' (s = 1): ranks (2,2,2,2), P = 8 (global rotation, Stage B: 8); a' = 0 (s = 0): ranks (1,1,1,1), P = 4; a = 0 (s = infinity): ranks (2,2,2,2), P = 8; generic: ranks (3,3,2,2), P = 10 with the optimal pivots.

===== cell 34 (kind 2) =====
(* Part B (small-eps, 200 digits): the group-theory and branch machinery of the two-vacua untwist notebook, reused verbatim. Same conventions as the finite-twist part above (identical definitions are idempotent); new here: pr = 200, hN, thN, zN as above. *)
(* GT basis and gl(3) generators: plain, non-Hermitian, exact rationals, verbatim from L2-310-untwist-CG.wb *)
ps[rep_] := ps[rep] = Block[{res = (Prepend[rep, #1] &) /@ Tuples[Table[Range[rep[[1, i + 1]], rep[[1, i]]], {i, 1, Length[rep[[1]]] - 1}]]}, If[Length[res[[1, 1]]] == 1, Reverse[Sort[res]], Reverse[Sort[Flatten[ps /@ res, 1]]]]];
dim[rep_] := dim[rep] = Length[ps[{rep}]];
J0[k_][pat_] := J0[k][pat] = Sum[pat[[k, i]], {i, 1, k}] - Sum[pat[[k - 1, i]], {i, 1, k - 1}];
Jp[k_][pat_] := Jp[k][pat] = -Sum[(Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k + 1, j]] + j, {j, Range[k + 1]}]/Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k, j]] + j, {j, Complement[Range[k], {i}]}])*v[ReplacePart[pat, {k, i} -> pat[[k, i]] + 1]], {i, 1, k}];
Jm[k_][pat_] := Jm[k][pat] = Sum[(Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k - 1, j]] + j, {j, Range[k - 1]}]/Product[pat[[k, i]] - i - pat[[k, j]] + j, {j, Complement[Range[k], {i}]}])*v[ReplacePart[pat, {k, i} -> pat[[k, i]] - 1]], {i, 1, k}];
JJ[k_, l_, rep_] := JJ[k, l, rep] = Which[
  k == l, DiagonalMatrix[J0[k] /@ ps[{rep}]],
  l == k + 1, Transpose[Table[(Coefficient[Jp[k][ps[{rep}][[i]]], v[#1]] &) /@ ps[{rep}], {i, 1, Length[ps[{rep}]]}]],
  l == k - 1, Transpose[Table[(Coefficient[Jm[l][ps[{rep}][[i]]], v[#1]] &) /@ ps[{rep}], {i, 1, Length[ps[{rep}]]}]],
  l > k + 1, JJ[k, k + 1, rep] . JJ[k + 1, l, rep] - JJ[k + 1, l, rep] . JJ[k, k + 1, rep],
  l < k - 1, JJ[k, k - 1, rep] . JJ[k - 1, l, rep] - JJ[k - 1, l, rep] . JJ[k, k - 1, rep]];
(* size of a residual: a numerically exact zero (tracked-precision zero) is reported by its accuracy bound 10^-Accuracy, never as an exact 0 (Clean-CG-combinations trap) *)
sz[x_] := If[x == 0, 10.^-Accuracy[x], N[Abs[x], 3]];
(* THE ONLY REP SWITCH is the next line *)
pr = 200; Λ = {2, 1, 0}; nL = 2; d1 = dim[Λ]; d2 = d1^nL; nilp = Λ[[1]] - Λ[[3]];
hN = 1/2; thN = {2/7, -3/5}; zN = {3/2, 5/4, 2/3};
tPar = (thN[[1]] - thN[[2]])/hN; th12 = thN[[1]] - thN[[2]]; t12 = tPar; y0 = (th12 - hN)/(th12 - 2 hN);
id1 = IdentityMatrix[d1, SparseArray]; id2 = IdentityMatrix[d2, SparseArray];
Ee[i_, j_] := Ee[i, j] = SparseArray[JJ[i, j, Λ]];
E1[i_, j_] := E1[i, j] = KroneckerProduct[Ee[i, j], id1];
E2[i_, j_] := E2[i, j] = KroneckerProduct[id1, Ee[i, j]];
(* total gl(3) weight of each product basis state, in KroneckerProduct order *)
wtL1 = Table[Table[J0[k][p], {k, 3}], {p, ps[{Λ}]}];
wtBasis = Flatten[Table[wtL1[[i]] + wtL1[[j]], {i, d1}, {j, d1}], 1];
secOf[mu_] := secOf[mu] = Flatten[Position[wtBasis, mu]];
uv[n_] := UnitVector[d1, n]; e1 = uv[1]; u12e1 = e1;
kron[a_, b_] := Flatten[KroneckerProduct[a, b]];
(* gl(3) commutation relations of JJ (exact) *)
comm = Flatten[Table[Normal[Ee[i, j] . Ee[k, l] - Ee[k, l] . Ee[i, j] - (KroneckerDelta[j, k] Ee[i, l] - KroneckerDelta[l, i] Ee[k, j])], {i, 3}, {j, 3}, {k, 3}, {l, 3}]];
checks["JJ gl(3) relations"] = Union[comm] === {0};
(* invariant diagonal metric G (G[[1]] = 1): Ee[i,j]^T G = G Ee[j,i]. JJ is not Hermitian, so projectors must be G-orthogonal (= orthogonal in the Hermitian EH frame) *)
gv = Array[gg, d1];
gsol = Solve[Flatten[Append[Table[Transpose[Normal[Ee[i, j]]] . DiagonalMatrix[gv] - DiagonalMatrix[gv] . Normal[Ee[j, i]], {i, 3}, {j, i + 1, 3}], {gv[[1]] - 1}]] == 0, gv];
gMet = DiagonalMatrix[gv /. First[gsol]];
checks["G unique, positive, invariant for all Ee[i,j]"] = Length[gsol] === 1 && And @@ Positive[Diagonal[gMet]] && And @@ Flatten[Table[Transpose[Normal[Ee[i, j]]] . gMet === gMet . Normal[Ee[j, i]], {i, 3}, {j, 3}]];
checks["nilpotency: Ee[i,j]^(nilp+1) = 0 (i != j), Ee[1,3]^nilp != 0"] = And @@ Flatten[Table[If[i == j, True, Max[Abs[Normal[MatrixPower[Ee[i, j], nilp + 1]]]] == 0], {i, 3}, {j, 3}]] && Max[Abs[Normal[MatrixPower[Ee[1, 3], nilp]]]] != 0;
Grid[Join[{{"Λ, d1, d2, nilp", ToString[{Λ, d1, d2, nilp}, InputForm]}, {"distinct total weights, distinct one-site weights", {Length[Union[wtBasis]], Length[Union[wtL1]]}}, {"diag G", ToString[Diagonal[gMet], InputForm]}, {"pr, h, θ, z, t = θ12/h", ToString[{pr, hN, thN, zN, tPar}, InputForm]}}, List @@@ Normal[checks]], Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 36 (kind 2) =====
(* monodromy T(v) = L^(1)(v-θ1).L^(2)(v-θ2) (exact, sparse) with the new Lax L_ij(u) = u δ_ij + h E_ij; principal quantum minors t_a (rows antisymmetrized, k-th factor at v-(k-1)h). For a diagonal twist the (I,I) minor is (prod z_I) times the untwisted minor, so the exact basis polynomials tBasisC[a, I] (coefficient matrices of u^j, from exact Lagrange interpolation on the seven points uS) are computed once; any twist costs one N[..] (memoized) and a linear combination. *)
Tm[v_] := Tm[v] = Table[Sum[(KroneckerDelta[i, k] (v - thN[[1]]) id2 + hN E1[i, k]) . (KroneckerDelta[k, j] (v - thN[[2]]) id2 + hN E2[k, j]), {k, 3}], {i, 3}, {j, 3}];
uS = 1/7 + Range[0, 6]/3;
minorAt[I_, v_] := Sum[Signature[σ] Dot @@ Table[Tm[v - (k - 1) hN][[σ[[k]], I[[k]]]], {k, Length[I]}], {σ, Permutations[I]}];
vinv = Inverse[Table[uS[[k]]^j, {k, 7}, {j, 0, 6}]];
tBasisC[a_, I_] := tBasisC[a, I] = With[{ms = Table[minorAt[I, uS[[k]]], {k, 7}]}, Table[Sum[vinv[[j, k]] ms[[k]], {k, 7}], {j, 7}]];
tBasisN[a_, I_] := tBasisN[a, I] = N[tBasisC[a, I], pr];
(* t_a(u) as a list of seven numeric coefficient matrices (u^0..u^6) for the diagonal twist zz *)
tZof[zz_] := tZof[zz] = Table[Sum[(Times @@ zz[[I]]) tBasisN[a, I], {I, Subsets[Range[3], {a}]}], {a, 3}];
nuP[n_][x_] := Product[x - thN[[s]] + hN Λ[[n]], {s, nL}];
auditNu[hh_, ths_, n_, x_] := Product[x - ths[[s]] + hh Λ[[n]], {s, Length[ths]}];
tEval[tc_, a_, u0_] := Sum[u0^(j - 1) tc[[a, j]], {j, 7}];
{tTime, tzTest} = AbsoluteTiming[tZof[N[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, pr]]];
(* quantum determinant: t3(u) = (z1 z2 z3) nu1(u) nu2(u-h) nu3(u-2h) times the identity, at two points *)
checks["qdet: t3 = chi3 nu1(u)nu2(u-h)nu3(u-2h) (max abs dev, 2 points)"] = Max[Table[Max[Abs[Normal[tEval[tzTest, 3, u0] - (3/2 5/4 2/3) auditNu[hN, thN, 1, u0] auditNu[hN, thN, 2, u0 - hN] auditNu[hN, thN, 3, u0 - 2 hN] id2]]], {u0, {1/5, -3/7}}]];
checks["t_a commute (t1(1/5) t2(2/3) - t2 t1: max abs)"] = Max[Abs[Normal[tEval[tzTest, 1, 1/5] . tEval[tzTest, 2, 2/3] - tEval[tzTest, 2, 2/3] . tEval[tzTest, 1, 1/5]]]];
checks["exact degree check: coefficient matrices u^(2a+1..6) of t_a vanish (a=1,2), max abs"] = Max[Table[Max[Abs[Normal[tzTest[[a, j]]]]], {a, 2}, {j, 2 a + 2, 7}]];
(* exact sparse t_a for an ARBITRARY 3x3 twist M (used only for the bra-twist eigen-covector check) *)
lax1x[s_, x_] := Table[KroneckerDelta[i, j] (x - thN[[s]]) id1 + hN Ee[i, j], {i, 3}, {j, 3}];
lax2x[x_] := lax2x[x] = With[{L1 = lax1x[1, x], L2 = lax1x[2, x]}, Table[Sum[KroneckerProduct[L1[[i, k]], L2[[k, j]]], {k, 3}], {i, 3}, {j, 3}]];
mono2x[M_][x_] := mono2x[M][x] = Table[Sum[lax2x[x][[i, l]] M[[l, j]], {l, 3}], {i, 3}, {j, 3}];
qminx[M_][rI_, cJ_][x_] := Sum[Signature[σ] Dot @@ Table[mono2x[M][x - (k - 1) hN][[σ[[k]], cJ[[k]]]], {k, Length[cJ]}], {σ, Permutations[rI]}];
tLg[a_, x_, M_] := Sum[qminx[M][rows, rows][x], {rows, Subsets[Range[3], {a}]}];
Grid[{{"time (s): all 7 minor families + one twist", tTime}, {"qdet dev", ScientificForm[sz[checks["qdet: t3 = chi3 nu1(u)nu2(u-h)nu3(u-2h) (max abs dev, 2 points)"]], 3]}, {"[t1,t2] dev", ScientificForm[sz[checks["t_a commute (t1(1/5) t2(2/3) - t2 t1: max abs)"]], 3]}, {"degree check", ScientificForm[sz[checks["exact degree check: coefficient matrices u^(2a+1..6) of t_a vanish (a=1,2), max abs"]], 3]}, {"Precision of t-coeffs", Precision[tzTest[[2, 3]]]}}, Frame -> All]

===== cell 38 (kind 2) =====
(* Group data: expN (exact on the nilpotent root generators), the SoV vacuum x0 with the FACTORIZED inverse (never Inverse[Pph1] at small eps), rho(diag), the one-site vacuum rotation OmegaD[z,w] = e1 rho(n(z,w)) with the Gauss parameters r_k(z,w) of the diagonal frame (w2 = Infinity allowed: the limit), rho of a general 3x3 upper unipotent matrix, the undone unipotent n' = n(z,w) n(z,w')^-1, the physical-frame Gauss factors P_phys and the companion-frame vacuum OmegaC[w]. A row vector times rho(exp(c E_ij)) is applied through the precomputed powers v.E^k (so symbolic or exact scalars are cheap). *)
zs = {z[1], z[2], z[3]};
rsSol = {s[1] -> z[1]^2/((z[1] - z[2]) (z[1] - z[3])), s[2] -> -(z[2]/(z[1] (z[2] - z[3]))), s[3] -> 1/(z[1] z[2]), r[1] -> -(z[3]/z[2]), r[2] -> -(z[3]/z[1]), r[3] -> (z[2]^2 (-z[1] + z[3]))/(z[1]^2 (z[2] - z[3]))};
expN[a_] := With[{A = Normal[a]}, IdentityMatrix[Length[A]] + Sum[MatrixPower[A, k]/k!, {k, 1, nilp}]];
checks["expN = MatrixExp on root generators (exact, 3 test values)"] = And @@ Flatten[Table[If[i == j, True, expN[c Ee[i, j]] === MatrixExp[c Normal[Ee[i, j]]]], {i, 3}, {j, 3}, {c, {3/7, -5/2, 2}}]];
x0Of[zz_] := Module[{rs, ppinv, pminv, dinv, x},
  rs = rsSol /. Thread[zs -> zz];
  ppinv = expN[-((zz[[1]] + zz[[2]])/(zz[[1]] zz[[2]])) Ee[2, 3]] . expN[(1/(zz[[1]] zz[[2]])) Ee[1, 3]] . expN[-(1/zz[[1]]) Ee[1, 2]];
  pminv = expN[-(r[3] /. rs) Ee[2, 1]] . expN[-(r[2] /. rs) Ee[3, 1]] . expN[-(r[1] /. rs) Ee[3, 2]];
  dinv = DiagonalMatrix[Table[Product[(s[k] /. rs)^(-J0[k][p]), {k, 3}], {p, ps[{Λ}]}]];
  x = e1 . ppinv . dinv . pminv; Flatten[KroneckerProduct[x, x]]];
x0Control[zz_] := Module[{rs = rsSol /. Thread[zs -> zz], Ppp, Pmp, Pdp, x},
  Ppp = MatrixExp[(1/zz[[1]]) Normal[Ee[1, 2]]] . MatrixExp[-(1/(zz[[1]] zz[[2]])) Normal[Ee[1, 3]]] . MatrixExp[((zz[[1]] + zz[[2]])/(zz[[1]] zz[[2]])) Normal[Ee[2, 3]]];
  Pmp = MatrixExp[(r[1] /. rs) Normal[Ee[3, 2]]] . MatrixExp[(r[2] /. rs) Normal[Ee[3, 1]]] . MatrixExp[(r[3] /. rs) Normal[Ee[2, 1]]];
  Pdp = DiagonalMatrix[Table[Product[(s[k] /. rs)^J0[k][p], {k, 3}], {p, ps[{Λ}]}]];
  x = First[{e1} . Inverse[Pmp . Pdp . Ppp]]; Flatten[KroneckerProduct[x, x]]];
checks["x0Of (factorized inverse) = x0 from Inverse[Pph1] (exact, z = (3/2,5/4,2/3))"] = x0Of[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}] === x0Control[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}];
ρdiag[d_] := DiagonalMatrix[Table[Product[d[[k]]^J0[k][p], {k, 3}], {p, ps[{Λ}]}]];
(* row vector times rho(exp(c E_ij)) *)
Apow[i_, j_][k_] := Apow[i, j][k] = If[k == 0, IdentityMatrix[d1], MatrixPower[Normal[Ee[i, j]], k]];
rowExp[vv_, c_, {i_, j_}] := vv + Sum[c^k/k! (vv . Apow[i, j][k]), {k, 1, nilp}];
(* e1 rho(exp(p1 E12) exp(p2 E13) exp(p3 E23)) for Gauss parameters (p1, p2, p3) *)
coverG[{p1_, p2_, p3_}] := rowExp[rowExp[rowExp[e1, p1, {1, 2}], p2, {1, 3}], p3, {2, 3}];
(* a 3x3 upper unipotent n = [[1,a,b],[0,1,c],[0,0,1]] = exp(a e12) exp((b-ac) e13) exp(c e23): Gauss parameters (a, b - a c, c) *)
gaussOfN[n_] := {n[[1, 2]], n[[1, 3]] - n[[1, 2]] n[[2, 3]], n[[2, 3]]};
coverU[n_] := coverG[gaussOfN[n]];
rhoU[n_] := With[{g = gaussOfN[n]}, expN[g[[1]] Ee[1, 2]] . expN[g[[2]] Ee[1, 3]] . expN[g[[3]] Ee[2, 3]]];
nInv3[n_] := {{1, -n[[1, 2]], n[[1, 2]] n[[2, 3]] - n[[1, 3]]}, {0, 1, -n[[2, 3]]}, {0, 0, 1}};
(* Gauss parameters of the one-site vacuum n(z,w) (depends on w1, w2 only); w2 = Infinity is the limit *)
rGauss[zz_, w1_, w2_] := If[w2 === Infinity,
  {(w1 - zz[[1]])/(w1 - zz[[2]]), -((w1 - zz[[1]]) (zz[[2]] - zz[[3]]))/((zz[[1]] - zz[[2]]) (w1 - zz[[3]])), ((w1 - zz[[2]]) (zz[[1]] - zz[[3]]))/((zz[[1]] - zz[[2]]) (w1 - zz[[3]]))},
  {(w1 - zz[[1]])/(w1 - zz[[2]]), ((w1 - zz[[1]]) (zz[[1]] - w2) (zz[[2]] - zz[[3]]))/((zz[[1]] - zz[[2]]) (w1 - zz[[3]]) (w2 - zz[[3]])), ((w1 - zz[[2]]) (zz[[2]] - w2) (zz[[1]] - zz[[3]]))/((zz[[1]] - zz[[2]]) (w1 - zz[[3]]) (zz[[3]] - w2))}];
nMat3[zz_, w1_, w2_] := With[{r = rGauss[zz, w1, w2]}, {{1, r[[1]], r[[2]] + r[[1]] r[[3]]}, {0, 1, r[[3]]}, {0, 0, 1}}];
nOf[zz_, w_] := nMat3[zz, w[[1]], w[[2]]];
(* the undone unipotent of the pair (w, w'): <e1 rho(n(w)) (x) e1 rho(n(w'))|h> = <e1 rho(n(w) n(w')^-1) (x) e1|h> for every HW ket h *)
nPrime[zz_, w_, wp_] := nOf[zz, w] . nInv3[nOf[zz, wp]];
ΩD[zz_, w_] := coverU[nOf[zz, w]];
phiUof[zz_, w_, wp_] := coverU[nPrime[zz, w, wp]];
(* the 3x3 matrices of the defining rep: check the Gauss parameters give the 3x3 unipotent matrix, and that rhoU is multiplicative (exact, 3 random points) *)
e3[i_, j_] := Table[KroneckerDelta[i, a] KroneckerDelta[j, b], {a, 3}, {b, 3}];
n3exp[{p1_, p2_, p3_}] := MatrixExp[p1 e3[1, 2]] . MatrixExp[p2 e3[1, 3]] . MatrixExp[p3 e3[2, 3]];
checks["nMat3 = exp(r1 e12) exp(r2 e13) exp(r3 e23) (3x3, exact)"] = nMat3[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, 5/2, 7/3] === n3exp[rGauss[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, 5/2, 7/3]];
checks["OmegaD = e1 expN(r1 E12) expN(r2 E13) expN(r3 E23) (full matrices, exact, 2 points)"] = Table[With[{r = rGauss[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, w[[1]], w[[2]]]}, ΩD[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, w] === e1 . expN[r[[1]] Ee[1, 2]] . expN[r[[2]] Ee[1, 3]] . expN[r[[3]] Ee[2, 3]]], {w, {{5/2, 7/3}, {-3/2, 4/5}}}];
checks["rho multiplicative: e1 rho(n n'^-1) = (e1 rho(n)) rho(n'^-1) (exact)"] = Module[{zz = {3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, n1 = nOf[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {5/2, 7/3}], n2 = nOf[{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {7/4, -2/5}]}, phiUof[zz, {5/2, 7/3}, {7/4, -2/5}] === ΩD[zz, {5/2, 7/3}] . rhoU[nInv3[n2]]];
(* physical-frame Gauss factors P_phys = Pm diag(s) Pp (rep-independent group data) and the companion-frame vacuum OmegaC[w] = e1 rho(exp(E12/w1) exp(-E13/(w1 w2)) exp((w1+w2)/(w1 w2) E23)) *)
sVec[zv_] := {zv[[1]]^2/((zv[[1]] - zv[[2]]) (zv[[1]] - zv[[3]])), -(zv[[2]]/(zv[[1]] (zv[[2]] - zv[[3]]))), 1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])};
rVec[zv_] := {-(zv[[3]]/zv[[2]]), -(zv[[3]]/zv[[1]]), (zv[[2]]^2 (zv[[3]] - zv[[1]]))/(zv[[1]]^2 (zv[[2]] - zv[[3]]))};
Pphys[zv_] := expN[rVec[zv][[1]] Ee[3, 2]] . expN[rVec[zv][[2]] Ee[3, 1]] . expN[rVec[zv][[3]] Ee[2, 1]] . ρdiag[sVec[zv]] . expN[(1/zv[[1]]) Ee[1, 2]] . expN[-(1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) Ee[1, 3]] . expN[((zv[[1]] + zv[[2]])/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) Ee[2, 3]];
gaussParC[w_] := If[w[[2]] === Infinity, {1/w[[1]], 0, 1/w[[1]]}, {1/w[[1]], -1/(w[[1]] w[[2]]), (w[[1]] + w[[2]])/(w[[1]] w[[2]])}];
ΩC[w_] := coverG[gaussParC[w]];
(* the regime: z = 1 + eps phi, w = 1 + eps omega, w' = 1 + eps omega' (w3 = 1 + eps 5/13, w3' = 1 - eps 4/13 drop out of the vacuum covector but enter the vacuum Q's) *)
reg2[eps_, ph_, om_, omp_] := <|"eps" -> eps, "z" -> 1 + eps ph, "w" -> {1 + eps om[[1]], 1 + eps om[[2]]}, "wp" -> {1 + eps omp[[1]], 1 + eps omp[[2]]}, "w3" -> {1 + eps om[[1]], 1 + eps om[[2]], 1 + eps 5/13}, "w3p" -> {1 + eps omp[[1]], 1 + eps omp[[2]], 1 - eps 4/13}|>;
phiP = {1/3, -2/7, 5/11}; phiC = {-2/5, 3/8, 7/11};
(* the (inf, inf) family: w = (1 + eps om1, Infinity), w' = (1 + eps om1', Infinity); direction grid (om1, om1') *)
gridQ = Tuples[{{3/4, -5/3}, {-12/5, 7/2}}];
epsL = {10^-6, 2 10^-6, 4 10^-6};
(* PPhys-frame tie and undo, exact, at rational points *)
Grid[{{"expN, x0Of", ToString[{checks["expN = MatrixExp on root generators (exact, 3 test values)"], checks["x0Of (factorized inverse) = x0 from Inverse[Pph1] (exact, z = (3/2,5/4,2/3))"]}]}, {"nMat3, OmegaD (full matrices), rho multiplicative", ToString[{checks["nMat3 = exp(r1 e12) exp(r2 e13) exp(r3 e23) (3x3, exact)"], checks["OmegaD = e1 expN(r1 E12) expN(r2 E13) expN(r3 E23) (full matrices, exact, 2 points)"], checks["rho multiplicative: e1 rho(n n'^-1) = (e1 rho(n)) rho(n'^-1) (exact)"]}]}, {"grid", ToString[

===== cell 40 (kind 2) =====
(* exact HW kets of V(x)V (common null space of the total raising ops per total-weight sector), their sectors (computed, not typed in), G-orthogonal projectors per HW sector. G2 = G(x)G is the invariant inner product (JJ is not Hermitian), so P_HW = H (H^T G2 H)^-1 H^T G2 is the projector orthogonal in the Hermitian EH frame. u12wts/u12idx = one-site weights and the GT indices per weight; the CG slice of an HW state of weight mu sits at the one-site weight nu = mu - Lambda (site 2 = e1). *)
u12raiseM = Join[E1[1, 2] + E2[1, 2], E1[1, 3] + E2[1, 3], E1[2, 3] + E2[2, 3]];
hwKets[mu_] := hwKets[mu] = With[{sec = secOf[mu]}, Table[Normal[SparseArray[Thread[sec -> v], {d2}]], {v, NullSpace[Normal[u12raiseM[[All, sec]]]]}]];
hwSecs = Reverse[Sort[Select[Union[wtBasis], Length[hwKets[#]] > 0 &]]];
u12HW = Flatten[hwKets /@ hwSecs, 1];
gd2 = Flatten[KroneckerProduct[Diagonal[gMet], Diagonal[gMet]]];
hwH[mu_] := hwH[mu] = Transpose[hwKets[mu]];
hwMinv[mu_] := hwMinv[mu] = Inverse[Transpose[hwH[mu]] . (gd2 hwH[mu])];
PHWc[mu_][psi_] := hwH[mu] . (hwMinv[mu] . (Transpose[hwH[mu]] . (gd2 psi)));
gNorm[psi_] := Sqrt[Total[gd2 psi^2]];
u12wt1 = wtL1; u12wts = Union[u12wt1]; u12idx = Table[Flatten[Position[u12wt1, m]], {m, u12wts}]; u12dims = Length /@ u12idx;
idxOf[wt_] := u12idx[[Position[u12wts, wt][[1, 1]]]];
hwList = Flatten[Table[Table[{mu, c}, {c, Length[hwKets[mu]]}], {mu, hwSecs}], 1];
checks["HW: number of kets, number of sectors"] = {Length[u12HW], Length[hwSecs]};
checks["HW kets annihilated by all total raising ops"] = And @@ (Normal[u12raiseM . #] === ConstantArray[0, 3 d2] & /@ u12HW);
checks["HW sectors, multiplicities, sector sizes"] = {hwSecs, Length[hwKets[#]] & /@ hwSecs, Length[secOf[#]] & /@ hwSecs};
checks["G2-projector: idempotent, fixes HW kets, G2-orthogonal residual (exact)"] = With[{vv = Table[(-1)^k k/7, {k, d2}]}, And @@ Table[PHWc[mu][PHWc[mu][vv]] === PHWc[mu][vv] && (And @@ (PHWc[mu][#] === # & /@ hwKets[mu])) && (And @@ (Total[gd2 # (vv - PHWc[mu][vv])] === 0 & /@ hwKets[mu])), {mu, hwSecs}]];
checks["slice weights nu = mu - Lambda, their dims (the site-1 weight space the CG slice lives on)"] = Table[{mu - Λ, Length[idxOf[mu - Λ]]}, {mu, hwSecs}];
Grid[Join[{{"# HW kets, # HW sectors", ToString[checks["HW: number of kets, number of sectors"], InputForm]}, {"annihilated by raising ops", checks["HW kets annihilated by all total raising ops"]}, {"sectors; multiplicities; sector sizes", ToString[checks["HW sectors, multiplicities, sector sizes"], InputForm]}, {"G2-projector exact", checks["G2-projector: idempotent, fixes HW kets, G2-orthogonal residual (exact)"]}, {"slice weights nu = mu - Lambda and dims", ToString[checks["slice weights nu = mu - Lambda, their dims (the site-1 weight space the CG slice lives on)"], InputForm]}, {"one-site weights, dims", ToString[{u12wts, u12dims}, InputForm]}}], Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 42 (kind 2) =====
(* Task 0 checks, all exact at rational points. (a) frame tie: OmegaC[w] = OmegaD[z,w] . P_phys(z); (b) exact undo on all HW kets: <OmegaD[w] (x) OmegaD[w']|h> = <e1 rho(n(w) n(w')^-1) (x) e1|h> (with a control showing the test is not vacuous); (c) OmegaD[w] (x) OmegaD[w] is the exact left eigen-covector of t_a(u) with the BRA twist G'(w) = P_aux G(w) P_aux^-1 (G(w) the companion matrix with eigenvalues w; the diagonal-frame image of the companion twist of eigenvalues w; NOT the diagonal matrix diag(w), shown as a control). *)
tiePts = {{{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {5/2, Infinity}}, {{2, 5/3, -1/2}, {-2, Infinity}}, {{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {5/2, 7/3}}, {{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {-3/2, 4/5}}, {{2, 5/3, -1/2}, {3, -1/3}}, {reg2[10^-6, phiP, {3/4, -1/5}, {-12/5, 1/8}]["z"], reg2[10^-6, phiP, {3/4, -1/5}, {-12/5, 1/8}]["w"]}};
(* the tie holds up to the scalar kappa(z,w) = <e1|rho(n P)|e1> = Prod_k Delta_k(n P3)^(lambda_k - lambda_{k+1}) (Delta_k leading principal minors of the 3x3 matrix n(z,w) P3(z), P3 = the defining-rep image of P_phys): OmegaC is normalised to first component 1, OmegaD . P_phys is not *)
Pphys3[zv_] := MatrixExp[rVec[zv][[1]] e3[3, 2]] . MatrixExp[rVec[zv][[2]] e3[3, 1]] . MatrixExp[rVec[zv][[3]] e3[2, 1]] . DiagonalMatrix[sVec[zv]] . MatrixExp[(1/zv[[1]]) e3[1, 2]] . MatrixExp[-(1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) e3[1, 3]] . MatrixExp[((zv[[1]] + zv[[2]])/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) e3[2, 3]];
kappaTie[zv_, w_] := With[{g = nOf[zv, w] . Pphys3[zv]}, Det[g[[1 ;; 1, 1 ;; 1]]]^(Λ[[1]] - Λ[[2]]) Det[g[[1 ;; 2, 1 ;; 2]]]^(Λ[[2]] - Λ[[3]]) Det[g]^Λ[[3]]];
tieTest[p_] := With[{a = ΩC[p[[2]]], b = ΩD[p[[1]], p[[2]]] . Pphys[p[[1]]]}, {MatrixRank[{a, b}] == 1, b === kappaTie[p[[1]], p[[2]]] a}];
checks["(a) frame tie OmegaC[w] = OmegaD[z,w] . P_phys(z)/kappa, exact: {parallel, scalar = kappa}, 2 points with w2 = Infinity + 3 finite points + regime point eps = 1e-6"] = tieTest /@ tiePts;
checkInfC = Module[{zz = {3/2, 5/4, 2/3}}, Table[N[Max[Abs[ΩD[zz, {5/2, W}] - ΩD[zz, {5/2, Infinity}]]], 5], {W, {10^10, 10^30}}]];
undoPts = {{{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {5/2, 7/3}, {7/4, -2/5}}, {{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {-3/2, 4/5}, {3, -5/7}}, {{2, 5/3, -1/2}, {3, -1/3}, {-2, 9/5}}};
undoPts = Join[undoPts, {{{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}, {5/2, Infinity}, {7/4, Infinity}}, {{2, 5/3, -1/2}, {3, 7/3}, {-2, Infinity}},With[{R = reg2[10^-6, phiP, {3/4, -1/5}, {-12/5, 1/8}]}, {R["z"], R["w"], R["wp"]}], With[{R = reg2[10^-6, phiC, {-5/3, 5/3}, {7/2, -3/4}]}, {R["z"], R["w"], R["wp"]}]}];
undoTest[p_] := Module[{zz = p[[1]], w = p[[2]], wp = p[[3]], lhs, rhs, wrong},
  lhs = kron[ΩD[zz, w], ΩD[zz, wp]]; rhs = kron[phiUof[zz, w, wp], e1]; wrong = kron[ΩD[zz, w], e1];
  {And @@ (lhs . # === rhs . # & /@ u12HW), Or @@ (lhs . # =!= wrong . # & /@ u12HW)}];
{tims["undo exact"], undoRes} = AbsoluteTiming[undoTest /@ undoPts];
checks["(b) exact undo on all HW kets (7 points: 2 with w2 = Infinity, 2 regime points); control: the naive e1 (x) e1 pairing differs"] = undoRes;
(* (c) bra-twist eigen-covector *)
ee3 = e3;
Gc[tw_] := {{Total[tw], -(tw[[1]] tw[[2]] + tw[[1]] tw[[3]] + tw[[2]] tw[[3]]), Times @@ tw}, {1, 0, 0}, {0, 1, 0}};
rsSolP[zv_] := {s1 -> zv[[1]]^2/((zv[[1]] - zv[[2]]) (zv[[1]] - zv[[3]])), s2 -> -(zv[[2]]/(zv[[1]] (zv[[2]] - zv[[3]]))), s3 -> 1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]]), rh1 -> -(zv[[3]]/zv[[2]]), rh2 -> -(zv[[3]]/zv[[1]]), rh3 -> (zv[[2]]^2 (zv[[3]] - zv[[1]]))/(zv[[1]]^2 (zv[[2]] - zv[[3]]))};
Paux[zv_] := (MatrixExp[-rh1 ee3[2, 3]] . MatrixExp[-rh2 ee3[1, 3]] . MatrixExp[-rh3 ee3[1, 2]] . DiagonalMatrix[1/{s1, s2, s3}] . MatrixExp[-(1/zv[[1]]) ee3[2, 1]] . MatrixExp[(1/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) ee3[3, 1]] . MatrixExp[-((zv[[1]] + zv[[2]])/(zv[[1]] zv[[2]])) ee3[3, 2]]) /. rsSolP[zv];
eigTest[zz_, wv_, M_] := With[{O2 = kron[ΩD[zz, wv[[1 ;; 2]]], ΩD[zz, wv[[1 ;; 2]]]]}, Table[With[{c = Normal[O2 . tLg[a, 2/9, M]]}, Union[Flatten[c - (c[[1]]/O2[[1]]) O2]] === {0}], {a, 3}]];
zE = {3/2, 5/4, 2/3}; wE = {5/2, 7/3, 3/5};
checks["(c0) Paux diagonalizes the ket companion matrix G(z) to diag(z)"] = Paux[zE] . Gc[zE] . Inverse[Paux[zE]] === DiagonalMatrix[zE];
GP = Paux[zE] . Gc[wE] . Inverse[Paux[zE]];
{tims["eigen-covector"], eigRes} = AbsoluteTiming[{eigTest[zE, wE, GP], eigTest[zE, wE, DiagonalMatrix[wE]]}];
checks["(c) OmegaD[w] (x) OmegaD[w] left eigen-covector of t_a(2/9), a = 1,2,3: bra twist G'(w); control diag(w)"] = eigRes;
Grid[{{"continuity: max |OmegaD(w2 = 1e10, 1e30) - OmegaD(w2 = Infinity)|", ToString[checkInfC, InputForm]}, {"(a) frame tie OmegaC[w] = OmegaD . P_phys / kappa, 6 points (first two: w2 = Infinity): {parallel, scalar = kappa}", ToString[checks["(a) frame tie OmegaC[w] = OmegaD[z,w] . P_phys(z)/kappa, exact: {parallel, scalar = kappa}, 2 points with w2 = Infinity + 3 finite points + regime point eps = 1e-6"]]}, {"(b) undo exact on all HW kets, 7 points (2 with w2 = Infinity): {all equal, naive control differs}", ToString[checks["(b) exact undo on all HW kets (7 points: 2 with w2 = Infinity, 2 regime points); control: the naive e1 (x) e1 pairing differs"]]}, {"(c0) Paux diagonalizes G(z)", checks["(c0) Paux diagonalizes the ket companion matrix G(z) to diag(z)"]}, {"(c) eigen-covector of t_a, a = 1,2,3: twist G'(w); control twist diag(w)", ToString[checks["(c) OmegaD[w] (x) OmegaD[w] left eigen-covector of t_a(2/9), a = 1,2,3: bra twist G'(w); control diag(w)"]]}, {"times (s): undo, eigen", ToString[{tims["undo exact"], tims["eigen-covector"]}]}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]
