===== cell 44 (kind 2) =====
(* Q machinery (used by the HW-limit branches and by Task 5) and the eigenstates. A state is an association st = <|"qu" -> {q1,q2,q3} (monic dual polynomials in X, degrees mu_j), "z" -> ket twist|>; Q^j(x) = z_j^(x/h) q^j(x). Vacuum direct Q_i^vac(x) = w_i^(-x/h) q_i^vac(x), degrees vacDeg, given as the function F[i, x]. det6 poles {s, n}, n = lambda3..lambda1 on each ladder; Psi_Q with the vacuum triple (lambda1+1, lambda1, lambda2). *)
poles = Flatten[Table[{s, n}, {s, nL}, {n, Λ[[3]], Λ[[1]]}], 1];
xP[{s_, n_}] := thN[[s]] - n hN;
wgt[p_] := wgt[p] = 1/Product[xP[p] - xP[q], {q, DeleteCases[poles, p]}];
ins[1][x_] := 1; ins[2][x_] := Exp[2 Pi I x/hN];
prsL1 = {{1, 3}, {1, 2}, {2, 3}};
QUf[st_, j_, x_] := st["z"][[j]]^(x/hN) (st["qu"][[j]] /. X -> x);
rowG[st_, F_, {i_, j_}, k_] := Sum[N[wgt[p] ins[k][xP[p]], pr] With[{x = xP[p]}, {-QUf[st, j, x] F[i, x], -x QUf[st, j, x] F[i, x], QUf[st, j, x] F[i, x + hN], x QUf[st, j, x] F[i, x + hN], QUf[st, j, x - hN] F[i, x], x QUf[st, j, x - hN] F[i, x]}], {p, poles}];
det6[st_, F_] := Det[Flatten[Table[rowG[st, F, ij, k], {k, 2}, {ij, prsL1}], 1]];
psiQ[st_] := Product[With[{t = thN[[s]]}, (QUf[st, 2, t - Λ[[1]] hN] QUf[st, 3, t - (Λ[[1]] + 1) hN] - QUf[st, 3, t - Λ[[1]] hN] QUf[st, 2, t - (Λ[[1]] + 1) hN]) QUf[st, 3, t - Λ[[2]] hN]], {s, nL}];
makeFvac[ww_, qv_] := Function[{i, x}, ww[[i]]^(-x/hN) (qv[[i]] /. X -> x)];
(* fixed-degree monic Baxter solver (overdetermined least squares, no Chop), verbatim from L2-diagonal-frame-overlap-EH.wb cell 95 *)
auditVac[zz_, hh_, ths_] := Table[Sum[Times @@ Table[zz[[ii[[k]]]] auditNu[hh, ths, ii[[k]], u - (k - 1) hh], {k, a}], {ii, Subsets[Range[3], {a}]}], {a, 3}];
auditQdet[zz_, hh_, ths_, x_] := (Times @@ zz) auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x] auditNu[hh, ths, 2, x - hh] auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x - 2 hh];
auditOp[tt_, q_, zz_, sgn_, hh_, ths_, x_] := If[sgn == 1,
  auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x] auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x - hh] auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x - 2 hh] zz (q /. X -> x + hh)
  - (tt[[1]] /. u -> x) auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x - hh] auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x - 2 hh] (q /. X -> x)
  + (tt[[2]] /. u -> x) auditNu[hh, ths, 3, x - 2 hh] (q /. X -> x - hh)/zz
  - (tt[[3]] /. u -> x) (q /. X -> x - 2 hh)/zz^2,
  auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x] auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x + hh] auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x + 2 hh] zz (q /. X -> x - hh)
  - (tt[[1]] /. u -> x) auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x + hh] auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x + 2 hh] (q /. X -> x)
  + (tt[[2]] /. u -> x + hh) auditNu[hh, ths, 1, x + 2 hh] (q /. X -> x + hh)/zz
  - (tt[[3]] /. u -> x + 2 hh) (q /. X -> x + 2 hh)/zz^2];
auditMonicQ[tt_, zz_, sgn_, deg_, hh_, ths_, prc_] := Module[{vars, eq, ar, sol},
  If[deg == 0, Return[1]]; vars = Array[aq, deg]; eq = CoefficientList[Expand[auditOp[tt, X^deg + Sum[vars[[k + 1]] X^k, {k, 0, deg - 1}], zz, sgn, hh, ths, X]], X];
  ar = CoefficientArrays[eq, vars]; sol = LeastSquares[N[Normal[ar[[2]]], prc], -N[Normal[ar[[1]]], prc]];
  Expand[X^deg + Sum[sol[[k + 1]] X^k, {k, 0, deg - 1}]]];
(* Baxter residual in exact arithmetic on the stored numbers (the naive norm of tracked approximate zeros prints an exact 0 with Precision Infinity, Clean-CG-combinations trap) *)
baxRes[tt_, qq_, zz_, sgn_] := Module[{t1 = SetPrecision[tt, Infinity], q1 = SetPrecision[qq, Infinity], z1 = SetPrecision[zz, Infinity]}, N[Max[Table[Norm[CoefficientList[Expand[auditOp[t1, q1[[j]], z1[[j]], sgn, hN, thN, X]], X]]/Max[1, Norm[CoefficientList[q1[[j]], X]]], {j, 3}]], 6]];
vacDeg = {0, 2 (Λ[[1]] - Λ[[2]]), 2 (Λ[[1]] - Λ[[3]])};
vacQs[ww_] := Module[{wn = N[ww, pr], tt, qv}, tt = auditVac[wn, hN, thN]; qv = Table[auditMonicQ[tt, wn[[j]], -1, vacDeg[[j]], hN, thN, pr], {j, 3}]; <|"tt" -> tt, "q" -> qv, "F" -> makeFvac[wn, qv], "res" -> baxRes[tt, qv, wn, -1], "w" -> wn|>];
(* eigenstates of one total-weight sector mu at the ket twist zz (exact rational list in, numeric pr inside), normalized <x0|Psi> = 1; each state carries its transfer eigenvalue polynomials t1,t2 (read off at the largest component; coefficients above the true degrees 2a are dropped and the dropped size is reported), t3 = quantum determinant, its dual Q's (degrees mu_j), the exact-arithmetic Baxter residual, the raising residual rr = ||(E12+E12, E23+E23) Psi||/||Psi|| (rr ~ 0 marks the HW-limit states), and for the HW sectors ||P_HW Psi||/||Psi|| in the invariant G-norm. Memoized on (zz, mu): purge with literal symbols only. *)
raiseSec[mu_] := raiseSec[mu] = With[{sec = secOf[mu]}, Normal[Join[(E1[1, 2] + E2[1, 2])[[All, sec]], (E1[2, 3] + E2[2, 3])[[All, sec]]]]];
statesCore[zz_, mu_] := statesCore[zz, mu] = Module[{zn = N[zz, pr], tc, x0, sec = secOf[mu], mix, es, rr, isHW = MemberQ[hwSecs, mu], out = {}},
  tc = tZof[zn]; x0 = x0Of[zn];
  mix = Normal[(tEval[tc, 1, 3/7] + N[Pi, pr] tEval[tc, 2, 3/7])[[sec, sec]]];
  es = Eigensystem[mix];
  rr = Table[Norm[raiseSec[mu] . v]/Norm[v], {v, es[[2]]}];
  Do[Module[{psiS, psi, km, tt, qq, st, dropped},
    psiS = es[[2, k]]/(x0[[sec]] . es[[2, k]]);
    psi = Normal[SparseArray[Thread[sec -> psiS], {d2}]];
    km = First@Ordering[Abs[psiS], -1];
    tt = Table[Sum[u^(j - 1) ((tc[[a, j]][[sec, sec]] . psiS)[[km]]/psiS[[km]]), {j, 1 + 2 a}], {a, 2}];
    dropped = Max[Table[Abs[(tc[[a, j]][[sec, sec]] . psiS)[[km]]/psiS[[km]]], {a, 2}, {j, 2 a + 2, 7}]];
    tt = Append[tt, auditQdet[zn, hN, thN, u]];
    qq = Table[auditMonicQ[tt, zn[[j]], 1, mu[[j]], hN, thN, pr], {j, 3}];
    st = <|"qu" -> qq, "z" -> zn|>;
    AppendTo[out, <|"mu" -> mu, "k" -> k, "psi" -> psi, "ev" -> es[[1, k]], "rr" -> rr[[k]], "tt" -> tt, "qu" -> qq, "st" -> st, "bax" -> baxRes[tt, qq, zn, 1], "dropped" -> dropped, "x0ov" -> (x0[[sec]] . es[[2, k]]), "hwFrac" -> If[isHW, gNorm[PHWc[mu][psi]]/gNorm[psi], Indeterminate]|>]], {k, Length[sec]}];
  out];
(* all states of the HW sectors at zz; the HW-limit branches = the dim(HW_mu) states of each sector with the smallest rr, copies ordered by Re of the eigenvalue of t1 + Pi t2 at u = 3/7 *)
branchesAt[zz_] := branchesAt[zz] = Module[{out = {}},
  Do[Module[{ss = statesCore[zz, mu], nHW = Length[hwKets[mu]], pick},
    pick = SortBy[Ordering[ss[[All, "rr"]], nHW], Re[ss[[#, "ev"]]] &];
    Do[AppendTo[out, Append[ss[[k]], <|"branch" -> MemberQ[pick, k], "copy" -> If[MemberQ[pick, k], Position[pick, k][[1, 1]], 0]|>]], {k, Length[ss]}]], {mu, hwSecs}];
  <|"states" -> out, "zz" -> zz|>];
brOf[B_, mu_, copy_] := First@Select[B["states"], #["mu"] === mu && #["copy"] === copy &];
brList[B_] := brOf[B, #[[1]], #[[2]]] & /@ hwList;
(* purge of every memoized point-dependent definition, LITERAL symbols only (DownValues is HoldAll: a Do over a symbol list purges nothing) *)
purge[] := (DownValues[Tm] = Select[DownValues[Tm], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[tBasisC] = Select[DownValues[tBasisC], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[tBasisN] = Select[DownValues[tBasisN], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[tZof] = Select[DownValues[tZof], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[wgt] = Select[DownValues[wgt], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[statesCore] = Select[DownValues[statesCore], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[branchesAt] = Select[DownValues[branchesAt], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &]; DownValues[raiseSec] = Select[DownValues[raiseSec], ! FreeQ[#[[1]], Pattern] &];);
(* one eps: all states of all HW sectors, the HW-limit branches *)
zTest = reg2[10^-6, phiP, {3/4, -1/5}, {-12/5, 1/8}]["z"];
{tBr, B0} = AbsoluteTiming[branchesAt[zTest]];
checks["branches: # states, # HW-limit branches (= # HW kets)"] = {Length[B0["states"]], Count[B0["states"], s_ /; s["branch"]]};
checks["branches: top branch = e1 (x) e1 (rel dev)"] = With[{p = brOf[B0, hwSecs[[1]], 1]["psi"]}, N[Norm[p/p[[1]] - kron[e1, e1]], 5]];
checks["rr separation per sector (max branch rr / min other rr)"] = Table[With[{s = Select[B0["states"], #["mu"] === mu &]}, {mu, N[Max[Select[s, #["branch"] &][[All, "rr"]]], 3], If[Length[Select[s, ! #["branch"] &]] == 0, "-", N[Min[Select[s, ! #["branch"] &][[All, "rr"]]], 3]]}], {mu, hwSecs}];
Grid[{{"time (s), all HW-sector states at eps = 1e-6", tBr}, {"#states, #branches", ToString[checks["branches: # states, # HW-limit branches (= # HW kets)"], InputForm]}, {"top = e1 (x) e1, rel dev", checks["branches: top branch = e1 (x) e1 (rel dev)"]}, {"dropped high coeffs of t1,t2 (max abs)", ScientificForm[sz[Max[B0["states"][[All, "dropped"]]]], 3]}, {"max exact Baxter residual (dual Q's)", ScientificForm[Max[B0["states"][[All, "bax"]]], 3]}, {"hwFrac of the branches (min)", ScientificForm[N[Min[Select[B0["states"], #["branch"] &][[All, "hwFrac"]]], 4]]}, {"rr separation {mu, max branch, min other}", ToString[checks["rr separation per sector (max branch rr / min other rr)"], InputForm]}, {"Precision of psi (min over states)", Min[Precision /@ B0["states"][[All, "psi"]]]}}, Frame -> All]

===== cell 46 (kind 2) =====
(* Task 1. The undone unipotent n' = n(z,w) n(z,w')^-1 (3x3, exact, symbolic in eps and in all directions). n' = [[1,a',b'],[0,1,c'],[0,0,1]] = exp(a' e12) exp((b'-a'c') e13) exp(c' e23); the three Gauss parameters are (a', c', g' = b' - a'c'), g' the commutator direction that degenerated in the old det6 x det2 family. Regime: z = 1+eps phi, w = 1+eps omega, w' = 1+eps omega'. Orders = exponent of the lowest power of eps (exact, Together); leading coefficient = the eps -> 0 value after dividing out the leading power. *)
ordEps[f_] := With[{t = Together[f]}, If[t === 0, Infinity, Exponent[Numerator[t], ep, Min] - Exponent[Denominator[t], ep, Min]]];
lead0[f_] := With[{t = Together[f]}, If[t === 0, 0, Factor[Together[t ep^(-ordEps[t])] /. ep -> 0]]];
symZ = 1 + ep {f1, f2, f3};
symAt[wS_, wpS_] := Module[{np = Map[Together, nPrime[symZ, wS, wpS], {2}]}, {np[[1, 2]], np[[2, 3]], Together[np[[1, 3]] - np[[1, 2]] np[[2, 3]]]}];
wMain = {1 + ep o1, 1 + ep o2}; wpMain = {1 + ep p1, 1 + ep p2};
{tims["symbolic n'"], parMain} = AbsoluteTiming[symAt[wMain, wpMain]];
ordsMain = ordEps /@ parMain;
leadMain = lead0 /@ parMain;
{a0S, c0S, g0S} = leadMain;
(* structure of the closed forms (exact): n(w) = [[1, a, b],[0, 1, c],[0, 0, 1]] with a = r1, b = r2 + r1 r3 = (w1 - z1)/(w1 - z3) (independent of w2) and c = r3; hence for n' = n(w) n(w')^-1: a' = a - a'w, c' = c - c'w and g' = b' - a'c' = (b - bw') - (a - aw') c, with c the E23 parameter of w only *)
rW = rGauss[symZ, 1 + ep o1, 1 + ep o2]; rWp = rGauss[symZ, 1 + ep p1, 1 + ep p2];
bOf[r_] := r[[2]] + r[[1]] r[[3]];
checks["T1 b = r2 + r1 r3 = (w1 - z1)/(w1 - z3) is independent of w2 (symbolic)"] = Simplify[bOf[rW] - (1 + ep o1 - symZ[[1]])/(1 + ep o1 - symZ[[3]])] === 0;
checks["T1 a' = a - a_w', c' = c - c_w' (exact, symbolic)"] = Simplify[parMain[[1]] - (rW[[1]] - rWp[[1]])] === 0 && Simplify[parMain[[2]] - (rW[[3]] - rWp[[3]])] === 0;
checks["T1 g' = (b - b_w') - (a - a_w') c (exact, symbolic)"] = Simplify[parMain[[3]] - ((bOf[rW] - bOf[rWp]) - (rW[[1]] - rWp[[1]]) rW[[3]])] === 0;
r0W = lead0 /@ rW; r0Wp = lead0 /@ rWp;
checks["T1 a'0 = r1_0(om) - r1_0(om'), c'0 = r3_0(om) - r3_0(om') (leading Gauss parameters of the two vacua)"] = Simplify[a0S - (r0W[[1]] - r0Wp[[1]])] === 0 && Simplify[c0S - (r0W[[3]] - r0Wp[[3]])] === 0;
checks["T1 g'0 independent of om2' ; a'0 independent of om2, om2' ; c'0 depends on all four"] = {D[g0S, p2] === 0, D[a0S, o2] === 0 && D[a0S, p2] === 0, And @@ (D[c0S, #] =!= 0 & /@ {o1, o2, p1, p2})};
(* rank of (o1, o2, p1, p2) -> (a'0, c'0, g'0): Jacobian at generic rational points *)
jac = D[{a0S, c0S, g0S}, {{o1, o2, p1, p2}}];
rankPts = {{f1 -> 1/3, f2 -> -2/7, f3 -> 5/11, o1 -> 3/4, o2 -> -1/5, p1 -> -12/5, p2 -> 1/8}, {f1 -> 1/3, f2 -> -2/7, f3 -> 5/11, o1 -> -5/3, o2 -> 9/7, p1 -> 7/2, p2 -> -3/4}, {f1 -> -2/5, f2 -> 3/8, f3 -> 7/11, o1 -> 5/7, o2 -> -11/3, p1 -> 2/9, p2 -> 13/4}};
ranksJ = Table[MatrixRank[jac /. pt], {pt, rankPts}];
(* is the map (o1, o2, p1, p2) -> (a'0, c'0, g'0) onto an open set with a one-dimensional fibre?  Check by solving for (o2, p1, p2) at fixed o1 given the target values at a sample point *)
checks["T1 orders of (a', c', g') in the regime (all O(1) expected)"] = ordsMain;
checks["T1 rank of the Jacobian of (om1, om2, om1', om2') -> (a'0, c'0, g'0) at 3 generic points"] = ranksJ;
(* controls *)
ctl[name_, wS_, wpS_] := Module[{pa = symAt[wS, wpS]}, {name, ordEps /@ pa, If[AllTrue[pa, # === 0 &], "n' = 1", ""], lead0 /@ pa}];
y0val = (tPar - 1)/(tPar - 2);
ctlRows = {
  ctl["(i) w' = w exactly", wMain, wMain],
  ctl["(ii-a) old regime: w2' pinned at y0 (1 + eps om2'), y0 = (t-1)/(t-2), w1' = 1 + eps om1'", {1 + ep o1, 1 + ep o2}, {1 + ep p1, y0val (1 + ep p2)}],
  ctl["(ii-b) w2' = 7/4 fixed, w1' = 1 + eps om1'", wMain, {1 + ep p1, 7/4}],
  ctl["(ii-c) w' = (7/4, -2/5) fixed (no w' untwisting)", wMain, {7/4, -2/5}],
  ctl["(iii) common w1' = w1, w2' = 1 + eps om2'", wMain, {1 + ep o1, 1 + ep p2}],
  ctl["(iv) common w2' = w2, w1' = 1 + eps om1'", wMain, {1 + ep p1, 1 + ep o2}]};
(* (iii) exactly: only the E23 direction survives, n' = exp(c' E23) with c' = r3(w2) - r3(w2') *)
checks["T1 (iii) common w1': a' = 0 and g' = 0 exactly (only c' survives)"] = With[{pa = symAt[wMain, {1 + ep o1, 1 + ep p2}]}, {pa[[1]] === 0, pa[[3]] === 0, pa[[2]] =!= 0}];
Grid[Join[{{"a'0 = lim a' (orders of a', c', g')", ToString[ordsMain, InputForm], ToString[a0S, InputForm]}, {"c'0", "", ToString[c0S, InputForm]}, {"g'0 = (b' - a'c')_0", "", ToString[g0S, InputForm]}, {"rank of Jacobian (3 points)", ToString[ranksJ, InputForm], ""}, {"b w2-independent; a' = a - a_w', c' = c - c_w'; g' = (b - b_w') - (a - a_w') c; a'0, c'0 = differences of the vacua leading parameters; dependence structure", ToString[{checks["T1 b = r2 + r1 r3 = (w1 - z1)/(w1 - z3) is independent of w2 (symbolic)"], checks["T1 a' = a - a_w', c' = c - c_w' (exact, symbolic)"], checks["T1 g' = (b - b_w') - (a - a_w') c (exact, symbolic)"], checks["T1 a'0 = r1_0(om) - r1_0(om'), c'0 = r3_0(om) - r3_0(om') (leading Gauss parameters of the two vacua)"], checks["T1 g'0 independent of om2' ; a'0 independent of om2, om2' ; c'0 depends on all four"]}, InputForm], ""}, {"(iii) common w1': {a' = 0, g' = 0, c' != 0}", ToString[checks["T1 (iii) common w1': a' = 0 and g' = 0 exactly (only c' survives)"], InputForm], ""}, {"controls: name, orders (a', c', g'), note, leading coefficients", "", ""}}, Table[{r[[1]], ToString[r[[2]], InputForm] <> " " <> r[[3]], ToString[r[[4]], InputForm]}, {r, ctlRows}], {{"time (s)", tims["symbolic n'"], ""}}], Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 48 (kind 1) =====
### Part B setup results (cells 34-46; same numbers as the Stage D notebook)

Exact checks (rational points): frame tie Omega_D[z,w] P_phys(z) = kappa(z,w) Omega_c[w] with kappa = prod_k Delta_k(n P3)^(lambda_k - lambda_(k+1)) at six points (two with w2 = infinity, one in the eps regime); undo <Omega_D[w] (x) Omega_D[w']|h> = <e1 rho(n(w) n(w')^-1) (x) e1|h> on all 6 HW kets at seven points (control: the naive pairing differs); 6 HW kets in 5 sectors (4,2,0), (4,1,1), (3,3,0), (3,2,1) (multiplicity 2), (2,2,2); slice weights nu = mu - Lambda of dimension 1,1,1,2,1. HW-limit branches at eps = 1e-6 (200 digits): 21 states of the HW sectors, 6 branches (smallest raising residual 2e-7 against >= 1.3 for the other states), exact dual-Baxter residual 1.1e-147, minimum precision of psi 162 digits, HW fraction >= 1 - 2e-14. Undone unipotent (cell 46, Stage D Task 1): a'0, c'0, g'0 are O(1), rank of (om1, om2, om1', om2') -> (a'0, c'0, g'0) is 3; common w1' = w1 gives a' = g' = 0 exactly.

===== cell 49 (kind 2) =====
(* Task 3(i): the bracket Q-number on the HW-limit branches at small eps, and the companion <-> diagonal dictionary. Companion frame: Phi_c = Omega_c[(1/a, inf)] (x) Omega_c[(1/a', inf)] with a = 1/w1 = 1/(1 + eps om1), a' = 1/(1 + eps om1'); the closed-form products of Task 1 are exact rational functions of (a, a') hence of eps. Diagonal frame (Stage D): Phi_d = Omega_D[z,w] (x) Omega_D[z,w'], w = (1 + eps om1, Infinity). Dictionary: Omega_D . P_phys = kappa Omega_c (exact, b05 (a)), the companion ket is Psi_c = (P^-1 (x) P^-1) psi_d, <x0_c|Psi_c> = <x0_d|psi_d> = 1, hence <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> = <Phi_d|psi_d>/(kappa kappa') (kappa = kappaTie(z, w), kappa' = kappaTie(z, w')). Qnum(B) := sum_{cc'} Q3(theta1 - c h) Q3(theta2 - c' h) sum_k Bk2^(1)[F,G] Bk2^(2)[F',G'] / (M1_vac M2_vac), M_vac = minor_s(-3,-2) Q3(theta_s - h), with the dual Q's of the Stage D branch (monic q^j times z_j^(x/h), 200 digits). Verified on ALL 21 states of the HW sectors. *)
nb = Length[hwList];
prodFun = Function[{aa, bb}, prodList /. {aS -> aa, bS -> bb}];
prodNum[a_, b_] := Map[Join[#[[1 ;; 2]], N[#[[3 ;;]], pr]] &, prodFun[a, b]];
ladderVals[st_] := Table[Table[QUf[st, j, thN[[s]] + m hN], {j, 3}], {s, 2}, {m, mPts}];   (* [[s, m-index, j]] *)
brk[LV_, s_, j_, F_] := F . LV[[s, All, j]];
bk2[LV_, s_, F_, G_] := brk[LV, s, 3, F] brk[LV, s, 2, G] - brk[LV, s, 2, F] brk[LV, s, 3, G];
q3at[LV_, s_, c_] := LV[[s, First[First[Position[mPts, -c]]], 3]];
mVac[LV_, s_] := (LV[[s, 2, 2]] LV[[s, 1, 3]] - LV[[s, 2, 3]] LV[[s, 1, 2]]) LV[[s, 3, 3]];
(* per-product record: brackets, Bk2's and the full product term (already divided by M1 M2) *)
termsOf[LV_, pn_] := Table[<|"cc" -> {p[[1]], p[[2]]}, "br1" -> {brk[LV, 1, 2, p[[3]]], brk[LV, 1, 3, p[[3]]], brk[LV, 1, 2, p[[4]]], brk[LV, 1, 3, p[[4]]]}, "br2" -> {brk[LV, 2, 2, p[[5]]], brk[LV, 2, 3, p[[5]]], brk[LV, 2, 2, p[[6]]], brk[LV, 2, 3, p[[6]]]}, "bk1" -> bk2[LV, 1, p[[3]], p[[4]]], "bk2" -> bk2[LV, 2, p[[5]], p[[6]]], "q3" -> q3at[LV, 1, p[[1]]] q3at[LV, 2, p[[2]]], "den" -> mVac[LV, 1] mVac[LV, 2]|>, {p, pn}];
termVal[t_] := t["q3"] t["bk1"] t["bk2"]/t["den"];
qnumOf[LV_, pn_] := Total[termVal /@ termsOf[LV, pn]];
kappaPair[zz_, w_, wp_] := kappaTie[zz, w] kappaTie[zz, wp];
(* chain-side overlap R_C(B) = <Phi_d|psi_B> (<x0|psi> = 1) *)
phiD[zz_, w_, wp_] := kron[N[ΩD[zz, w], pr], N[ΩD[zz, wp], pr]];
famWs[eps_, o1_, o1p_] := {{1 + eps o1, Infinity}, {1 + eps o1p, Infinity}};
aOf[eps_, o1_] := 1/(1 + eps o1);
(* (1) dictionary at one point, all 21 states: companion pairing with Psi_c = (P^-1 (x) P^-1) psi vs the diagonal overlap / (kappa kappa') *)
ptA = {3/4, -12/5}; epsA = epsL[[1]];
zA = 1 + epsA phiP; wsA = famWs[epsA, ptA[[1]], ptA[[2]]];
BA = branchesAt[zA]; statesA = BA["states"];
PinvA = N[Inverse[Pphys[zA]], pr];
cInfF[a_] := coverG[{a, 0, a}];
PhiCc = kron[cInfF[aOf[epsA, ptA[[1]]]], cInfF[aOf[epsA, ptA[[2]]]]];
{tims["dictionary + Qnum, 21 states"], dictRes} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[Module[{psi = s["psi"], Psic, lhsC, rhsD, qn, LV},
   Psic = Flatten[PinvA . Partition[psi, d1] . Transpose[PinvA]];
   lhsC = (PhiCc . Psic)/(x0C . Psic);
   rhsD = (phiD[zA, wsA[[1]], wsA[[2]]] . psi)/kappaPair[zA, wsA[[1]], wsA[[2]]];
   LV = ladderVals[s["st"]]; qn = qnumOf[LV, prodNum[aOf[epsA, ptA[[1]]], aOf[epsA, ptA[[2]]]]];
   {Abs[lhsC/rhsD - 1], Abs[qn/lhsC - 1], Abs[qn], s["mu"], s["branch"]}], {s, statesA}]];
checks["T3 dictionary: companion pairing = diagonal overlap/(kappa kappa'), 21 states (max rel dev)"] = Max[dictRes[[All, 1]]];
checks["T3 Qnum = <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> on the 21 states (max rel dev)"] = Max[dictRes[[All, 2]]];
Grid[{{"point (om1, om1'), eps", {ptA, N[epsA]}}, {"# states, # branches", {Length[statesA], Count[statesA, s_ /; s["branch"]]}}, {"kappa, kappa'", N[{kappaTie[zA, wsA[[1]]], kappaTie[zA, wsA[[2]]]}, 12]}, {"dictionary: max rel dev of <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> vs <Phi_d|psi_d>/(kappa kappa') over 21 states", ScientificForm[sz[checks["T3 dictionary: companion pairing = diagonal overlap/(kappa kappa'), 21 states (max rel dev)"]], 3]}, {"Qnum vs <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c>: max rel dev over 21 states", ScientificForm[sz[checks["T3 Qnum = <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> on the 21 states (max rel dev)"]], 3]}, {"|Qnum| min / max over the states (non-vacuity), and per-state deviations", {ScientificForm[N[Min[dictRes[[All, 3]]], 3]], ScientificForm[N[Max[dictRes[[All, 3]]], 3]], ScientificForm[sz[#], 2] & /@ dictRes[[All, 2]]}}, {"time (s)", tims["dictionary + Qnum, 21 states"]}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 51 (kind 2) =====
(* Task 3(ii): epsilon-exponents along the Q-side chain, per branch, at the direction point (om1, om1') = (3/4, -12/5): the entries of F, G, F', G' (exact orders in eps: s = a'/a = (1 + eps om1)/(1 + eps om1'), the factors a^m, a'^n are units), then numerically (200 digits, eps = 1, 2, 4 x 10^-6, exponent = log2 |x(2 eps)/x(eps)|, integrality = distance to the nearest integer) the brackets <Q^j F>_s, the Bk2's, the product terms Q3 Q3 Bk2 Bk2/(M1 M2), the four block sums (c, c') and the total Qnum. A cancellation shows as a block sum / total whose exponent is higher than the lowest exponent among the terms it sums. *)
P0 = {3/4, -12/5};
sAt[ep_] := (1 + ep P0[[1]])/(1 + ep P0[[2]]);
entOrd = Table[With[{p = prodTilde[[i]]}, Table[Module[{os = Table[ordEps[p[[j, m]] /. sS -> sAt[ep]], {m, pPts}]}, {Min[os], Max[Select[os, # =!= Infinity &]]}], {j, 3, 6}]], {i, Length[prodTilde]}];
entMinMax = {Min[Flatten[entOrd]], Max[Flatten[entOrd]]};
blkKeys = {{0, 0}, {0, 1}, {1, 0}, {1, 1}};
itemsOf[ib_, ie_] := Module[{eps = epsL[[ie]], st, LV, pn, tt},
  st = brOf[branchesAt[1 + eps phiP], hwList[[ib, 1]], hwList[[ib, 2]]]; LV = ladderVals[st["st"]];
  pn = prodNum[aOf[eps, P0[[1]]], aOf[eps, P0[[2]]]]; tt = termsOf[LV, pn];
  <|"ent" -> Flatten[pn[[All, 3 ;;]]], "br" -> Flatten[Table[Join[t["br1"], t["br2"]], {t, tt}]], "bk" -> Flatten[Table[{t["bk1"], t["bk2"]}, {t, tt}]], "prod" -> (termVal /@ tt), "blk" -> Table[Total[termVal /@ Select[tt, #["cc"] === k &]], {k, blkKeys}], "tot" -> {Total[termVal /@ tt]}, "bkmag" -> Flatten[Table[{Max[Abs[brk[LV, 1, 3, p[[3]]] brk[LV, 1, 2, p[[4]]]], Abs[brk[LV, 1, 2, p[[3]]] brk[LV, 1, 3, p[[4]]]]], Max[Abs[brk[LV, 2, 3, p[[5]]] brk[LV, 2, 2, p[[6]]]], Abs[brk[LV, 2, 2, p[[5]]] brk[LV, 2, 3, p[[6]]]]]}, {p, pn}]], "blkmag" -> {Max[Abs[termVal /@ tt]]}|>];
{tims["items, 6 branches x 3 eps"], itemsAll} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[Table[itemsOf[ib, ie], {ie, 3}], {ib, nb}]];
exps[ib_, key_, ie_] := Module[{x1 = itemsAll[[ib, ie, key]], x2 = itemsAll[[ib, ie + 1, key]]}, Table[If[x1[[i]] == 0 || x2[[i]] == 0, Nothing, Log[Abs[x2[[i]]/x1[[i]]]]/Log[2]], {i, Length[x1]}]];
mm[l_] := If[l === {}, {"-", "-"}, N[Round[MinMax[l], 1/100]]];
digLost[ib_, ie_] := Module[{it = itemsAll[[ib, ie]]}, {N[Round[Max[Log10[it["bkmag"]/Abs[it["bk"]]]], 0.1]], N[Round[Log10[it["blkmag"][[1]]/Abs[it["tot"][[1]]]], 0.1]]}];
nonInt[l_] := If[l === {}, 0, Max[Abs[l - Round[l]]]];
rows = Table[Module[{ee = Table[exps[ib, k, 1], {k, {"ent", "br", "bk", "prod", "blk", "tot"}}]},
   {hwList[[ib, 1]], hwList[[ib, 2]], mm[ee[[2]]], mm[ee[[3]]], mm[ee[[4]]], N[Round[ee[[5]], 1/100]], N[Round[ee[[6, 1]], 1/100]], N[Round[Min[ee[[4]]] - ee[[6, 1]], 1/100]], digLost[ib, 1], ScientificForm[N[Max[Table[nonInt[exps[ib, k, ie]], {k, {"br", "bk", "prod", "blk", "tot"}}, {ie, 2}]], 2]]}], {ib, nb}];
(* exponents per product (term) and per block for the branch with the largest spread, printed for all branches *)
rowsProd = Table[{hwList[[ib, 1]], hwList[[ib, 2]], N[Round[exps[ib, "prod", 1], 1/100]], N[Round[exps[ib, "blk", 1], 1/100]], N[Round[exps[ib, "tot", 1][[1]], 1/100]]}, {ib, nb}];
checks["T3 exponent integrality (max distance to an integer over all brackets/Bk2/products/blocks/totals, both eps pairs)"] = Max[Table[nonInt[exps[ib, k, ie]], {ib, nb}, {k, {"br", "bk", "prod", "blk", "tot"}}, {ie, 2}]];
Grid[Join[{{"entries of F,G,F',G' (exact eps-orders; per product {F, G, F', G'} {min, max} over the nonzero entries)", entOrd}, {"entry order range over all products", entMinMax}, {"mu", "copy", "brackets <Q^j F>_s: exponent range", "Bk2's", "product terms", "block sums (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)", "total Qnum", "cancellation depth (orders): lowest product exponent - total exponent", "digits lost at eps = 1e-6: {inside the worst Bk2, in the sum of the products}", "max distance to an integer"}}, rows, {{"per branch: exponents of the 10 product terms, the 4 block sums, the total", "", "", "", ""}}, rowsProd, {{"time (s)", tims["items, 6 branches x 3 eps"], "", "", ""}}], Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 53 (kind 2) =====
(* Task 3(iii): all six CG data from the bracket Q-number ALONE. For the (inf, inf) family (a, a' free through om1, om1') and each HW branch B: R_Q(B; eps, point) := kappa kappa' Qnum(B) is the diagonal-frame overlap <Omega_D[w] (x) Omega_D[w']|psi_B> (<x0|psi_B> = 1); with the exact undo <Phi|P_HW psi> = <phi_u (x) e1|P_HW psi> and K/S = O(eps), C := R_Q/phi_u(0)[slice] -> the CG slice of B (1-dim slices directly, the octet 2 x 2 system from two grid points with non-parallel phi_u(0)[slice]); the chain slice (Stage D) is used ONLY as the comparison. Grid om1 in {3/4,-5/3}, om1' in {-12/5, 7/2}; eps = 1, 2, 4 x 10^-6; z = 1 + eps phi, phi = (1/3,-2/7,5/11); 200 digits. phi_u(0): exact eps^0 coefficient vector of e1 rho(n(z,w) n(z,w')^-1) in the (inf, inf) family, checked against the closed forms of Stage D Task 1 in the limit om2, om2' -> infinity and against the direct Gauss decomposition. *)
ordEps2[f_] := With[{t = Together[f]}, If[t === 0, Infinity, Exponent[Numerator[t], ep, Min] - Exponent[Denominator[t], ep, Min]]];
famII = <|"name" -> "(inf, inf) family", "pts" -> Table[{{g[[1]], 0}, {g[[2]], 0}}, {g, gridQ}], "wOf" -> Function[{ee, om, omp}, {{1 + ee om[[1]], Infinity}, {1 + ee omp[[1]], Infinity}}]|>;
phiSeries[fam_, ph_, pt_] := Module[{zS = 1 + ep ph, ws = fam["wOf"][ep, pt[[1]], pt[[2]]], np, par, comps},
  np = Map[Together, nPrime[zS, ws[[1]], ws[[2]]], {2}];
  par = {np[[1, 2]], np[[1, 3]] - np[[1, 2]] np[[2, 3]], np[[2, 3]]};
  comps = Together /@ coverG[par];
  <|"ord" -> (ordEps2 /@ comps), "c0" -> (SeriesCoefficient[#, {ep, 0, 0}] & /@ comps), "c1" -> (SeriesCoefficient[#, {ep, 0, 1}] & /@ comps), "par" -> par|>];
{tims["phi_u series"], serII} = AbsoluteTiming[phiSeries[famII, phiP, #] & /@ famII["pts"]];
phi0 = serII[[All, "c0"]];
(* limit of the generic closed forms (a'0, c'0, g'0 of Stage D Task 1) as om2, om2' -> infinity, against the direct (inf, inf) leading parameters *)
limCF = Map[Limit[Limit[#, o2 -> Infinity], p2 -> Infinity] &, {a0S, c0S, g0S}];
dirLead = lead0 /@ symAt[{1 + ep o1, Infinity}, {1 + ep p1, Infinity}];
checks["T3 limit om2, om2' -> inf of the closed forms (a'0, c'0, g'0) = leading Gauss parameters of n(w)n(w')^-1 at w2 = w2' = infinity (exact, symbolic)"] = Simplify[limCF - dirLead] === {0, 0, 0};
checks["T3 phi_u(0) (series c0) = e1 rho(exp(a'0 E12) exp(g'0 E13) exp(c'0 E23)) with the limit forms, 4 grid points"] = And @@ Table[With[{rules = Thread[{f1, f2, f3, o1, p1} -> Join[phiP, {pt[[1, 1]], pt[[2, 1]]}]]}, phiSeries[famII, phiP, pt]["c0"] === coverG[limCF[[{1, 3, 2}]] /. rules]], {pt, famII["pts"]}];
checks["T3 orders of phi_u components over the grid (min over points, per one-site weight); all slice weights at order 0?"] = Table[{u12wts[[m]], u12dims[[m]], Min[Table[Min[s["ord"][[u12idx[[m]]]]], {s, serII}]], MatrixRank[Table[s["c0"][[u12idx[[m]]]], {s, serII}]]}, {m, Length[u12wts]}];
(* chain data and overlaps *)
chainData[ie_, ph_] := chainData[ie, ph] = Module[{zz = 1 + epsL[[ie]] ph, B}, B = branchesAt[zz];
  Table[With[{b = brOf[B, hwList[[ib, 1]], hwList[[ib, 2]]]}, <|"psi" -> b["psi"], "ph" -> PHWc[b["mu"]][b["psi"]], "st" -> b["st"]|>], {ib, nb}]];
{tims["Q-side and chain overlaps"], RR} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[Table[Table[Module[{eps = epsL[[ie]], zz = 1 + epsL[[ie]] phiP, pt = famII["pts"][[ip]], cd, ws, LVb, RQ, RC},
      cd = chainData[ie, phiP][[ib]]; ws = famII["wOf"][eps, pt[[1]], pt[[2]]];
      LVb = ladderVals[cd["st"]];
      RQ = kappaPair[zz, ws[[1]], ws[[2]]] qnumOf[LVb, prodNum[aOf[eps, pt[[1, 1]]], aOf[eps, pt[[2, 1]]]]];
      RC = phiD[zz, ws[[1]], ws[[2]]] . cd["psi"];
      {RQ, RC}], {ib, nb}], {ip, Length[serII]}], {ie, 3}]];
devQC = Table[Max[Table[Abs[RR[[ie, ip, ib, 1]]/RR[[ie, ip, ib, 2]] - 1], {ip, Length[serII]}]], {ie, 3}, {ib, nb}];
checks["T3 R_Q = chain overlap (max rel dev over 3 eps x 4 grid points x 6 branches)"] = Max[devQC];
(* CG extraction from R_Q only *)
fmtE[x_] := If[x == 0, "0 (acc " <> ToString[Round[Accuracy[x]]] <> ")", With[{e = Floor[Log10[Abs[x]]]}, ToString[Round[x/10^e, 0.01]] <> "e" <> ToString[e]]];
rich3[f_] := Module[{r1 = 2 f[[1]] - f[[2]], r2 = 2 f[[2]] - f[[3]]}, (4 r1 - r2)/3];
refNums = <|{{4, 1, 1}, 1} -> 46.67453891, {{3, 3, 0}, 1} -> 10.92853892, {{2, 2, 2}, 1} -> -5118.538314, {{3, 2, 1}, 1} -> 184.3305612, {{3, 2, 1}, 2} -> -238.5587523|>;
funcOf = <|{{3, 2, 1}, 1} -> {1, 3}, {{3, 2, 1}, 2} -> {1, 3}|>;
np = Length[serII]; cdAll = Table[chainData[ie, phiP], {ie, 3}];
cgQ[ib_] := Module[{mu = hwList[[ib, 1]], nu, ix, dm, slC, RQ, p, pairs, cs, res},
  nu = mu - Λ; ix = idxOf[nu]; dm = Length[ix];
  slC = Table[cdAll[[ie, ib, "ph"]][[(ix - 1) d1 + 1]], {ie, 3}];
  RQ = Table[RR[[ie, ip, ib, 1]], {ie, 3}, {ip, np}];
  p = -Round[Log[Norm[slC[[2]]]/Norm[slC[[1]]]]/Log[2]];
  If[dm == 1,
   cs = Table[RQ[[ie, ip]]/phi0[[ip, ix[[1]]]], {ie, 3}, {ip, np}];
   <|"ib" -> ib, "dim" -> 1, "p" -> p, "spread" -> Table[Max[Abs[cs[[ie]]/Mean[cs[[ie]]] - 1]], {ie, 3}], "err" -> Table[Max[Abs[cs[[ie]]/slC[[ie, 1]] - 1]], {ie, 3}], "fchain" -> Table[slC[[ie, 1]] epsL[[ie]]^p, {ie, 3}], "fq" -> Table[Mean[cs[[ie]]] epsL[[ie]]^p, {ie, 3}], "fq1" -> Table[cs[[ie, 1]] epsL[[ie]]^p, {ie, 3}]|>,
   pairs = Select[{{1, 3}, {2, 4}}, Abs[Det[phi0[[#, ix]]]] > 10^-3 Max[Abs[phi0[[#, ix]]]]^2 &];
   cs = Table[Table[LinearSolve[phi0[[pr, ix]], RQ[[ie, pr]]], {pr, pairs}], {ie, 3}];
   <|"ib" -> ib, "dim" -> 2, "p" -> p, "npairs" -> Length[pairs], "spread" -> Table[Max[Abs[cs[[ie]]/ConstantArray[Mean[cs[[ie]]], Length[pairs]] - 1]], {ie, 3}], "err" -> Table[Max[Abs[cs[[ie]]/ConstantArray[slC[[ie]], Length[pairs]] - 1]], {ie, 3}], "fchain" -> Table[slC[[ie]] epsL[[ie]]^p, {ie, 3}], "fq" -> Table[Mean[cs[[ie]]] epsL[[ie]]^p, {ie, 3}], "fq1" -> Table[cs[[ie, 1]] epsL[[ie]]^p, {ie, 3}], "cond" -> Max[Table[Norm[phi0[[pr, ix]]] Norm[Inverse[phi0[[pr, ix]]]], {pr, pairs}]]|>]];
cgRes = cgQ /@ Range[nb];
rowQ[r_] := Module[{ib = r["ib"], lab, rchain, rq, refv, fv},
  lab = hwList[[ib]]; rchain = rich3[r["fchain"]]; rq = rich3[r["fq1"]];
  refv = Lookup[refNums, Key[lab], None]; fv = Lookup[funcOf, Key[lab], None];
  {lab[[1]], lab[[2]], r["dim"], -r["p"], StringRiffle[fmtE /@ r["spread"][[{1, 3}]], ", "], StringRiffle[fmtE /@ r["err"], ", "], If[r["err"][[1]] == 0, "-", ToString[N[r["err"][[3]]/r["err"][[1]], 3]]], ToString[N[If[fv === None, rq, fv . rq], 12], InputForm], fmtE[Max[Abs[rq/rchain - 1]]], If[refv === None, "", ToString[N[If[fv === None, rchain, fv . rchain], 10]] <> " vs " <> ToString[refv]]}];

===== cell 57 (kind 1) =====
### Task 3 results

**Dictionary (cell 49).** <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> = <Phi_d|psi_d>/(kappa kappa'), kappa = kappaTie(z, w), kappa' = kappaTie(z, w'), with Psi_c = (P^-1 (x) P^-1) psi_d and <x0_c|Psi_c> = <x0_d|psi_d> = 1. Verified numerically at (om1, om1', eps) = (3/4, -12/5, 1e-6) on all 21 states of the HW sectors: max relative deviation 4.9e-123 (kappa = -1.79e25, kappa' = 9.38e22, i.e. ~ eps^-4 each).

**(i) Q-number verification.** Qnum(B) = sum_{cc'} Q3(theta1 - c h) Q3(theta2 - c' h) sum_k Bk2^(1)[F,G] Bk2^(2)[F',G'] / (M1_vac M2_vac) with the ten Task 1 products evaluated at the exact rationals a = 1/(1 + eps om1), a' = 1/(1 + eps om1') and the 200-digit dual Q's of the branch: **Qnum(B) = <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> on all 21 states with max relative deviation 7.9e-111** (non-vacuity: |Qnum| between 1.3e-36 and 4.7e-10; the deviation is limited by the ~160-digit Q's and the cancellation of ~37 digits at eps = 1e-6, see below). At every one of the four grid points, three eps and six branches (72 checks, cell 53) the full R_Q = kappa kappa' Qnum equals the chain overlap <Omega_D[w] (x) Omega_D[w']|psi_B>: max relative deviation 1.6e-113.

**(ii) eps-exponent table (cell 51, point (om1, om1') = (3/4, -12/5); exponent e means x ~ eps^e; entries are exact, the rest are log2 ratios between eps = 1e-6 and 2e-6, integer to <= 8e-4, confirmed by the pair 2e-6 -> 4e-6).**

| level | exponent range, all branches |
|---|---|
| entries of F, G, F', G' (exact; Stage 1 closed forms) | -1 ... 1 (only f'_(-2) of product 7 has eps^-1, from 1/(s-1); eps^1 entries from (s-1)) |
| brackets <Q^j F>_s | branch (4,2,0): -3...1; (4,1,1): -1...1; (3,3,0): -4...1; (3,2,1) both copies: -2...1; (2,2,2): -1...1 |
| Bk2's (2 x 2 bracket determinants) | 0 ... 2 for every branch |
| product terms Q3 Q3 Bk2 Bk2/(M1 M2) | 0 or 1 for every branch (products #3 and #6 carry eps^1, the other eight eps^0) |
| the four block sums (c, c') | 0, 0, 0, 0 for every branch |
| **total Qnum** | **+6 (4,2,0), +5 (4,1,1), +5 (3,3,0), +4 (3,2,1) x 2, +2 (2,2,2)** |

**Where the cancellations sit.** (1) Inside the Bk2's: the brackets are as large as eps^-3 ... eps^-4 (the dual Q's of the HW-limit branches are themselves large at the near-degenerate twist) but the 2 x 2 determinants are O(1) ... O(eps^2): the cancellation loses up to 19.9 / 6.7 / 26.2 / 13.2 / 14.2 / 6.8 digits (branches in the order (4,2,0), (4,1,1), (3,3,0), (3,2,1)#1, #2, (2,2,2)) at eps = 1e-6. (2) In the final SUM over the ten products (equivalently over the four (c,c') blocks): every product and every block sum is O(eps^0), the total is O(eps^6 ... eps^2): the sum cancels 6, 5, 5, 4, 4, 2 orders = 36.7, 29.5, 30.9, 23.3, 23.4, 10.7 digits at eps = 1e-6. The total exponent equals the exponent of the CG slice of the branch (chain slice ~ eps^p): R_Q = kappa kappa' Qnum ~ eps^(-8+6) = eps^-2 for the top, eps^-3, eps^-3, eps^-4, eps^-4 and eps^-6 (the singlet): exactly the slice exponents of the Stage D Task 5 table (mixed overlap exponent = CG-slice exponent). The 200-digit precision of the Q's is what makes the formula usable at eps = 1e-6; the brackets themselves are NOT small, the whole eps-dependence of the CG datum is produced by cancellations between O(1) terms, and the leading coefficient is read off at the end.

**(iii) Six CG data from R_Q alone (cell 53; 4 grid points, eps = 1,2,4 x 10^-6; the chain slice is used only as comparison).** phi_u(0) = e1 rho(exp(a'0 E12) exp(g'0 E13) exp(c'0 E23)) with the w2, w2' -> infinity limits of the Stage D closed forms (a'0 = (phi1 - phi2)(om1 - om1')/((om1 - phi2)(om1' - phi2)), g'0 = (phi1 - phi3)(phi2 - phi3)(om1 - om1')/((phi3 - om1)(phi2 - om1')(phi3 - om1')), c'0 long): equal to the direct leading Gauss parameters of n(w) n(w')^-1 at w2 = w2' = infinity (exact, symbolic), and equal to the eps^0 series coefficient at all four points; every one-site weight is at order eps^0 with rank = multiplicity (2 on (1,1,1)).

All errors and spreads scale like eps (ratio 4.00 between 1e-6 and 4e-6), the second-order Richardson limits of the Q-side data agree with the chain Richardson limits to <= 1.2e-18 (exactly, to the working precision, for the 1-dim slices of the first irreps), and the reference numbers 46.67453891, 10.92853892, -5118.538314 and the octet functionals 184.3305612 / -238.5587523 are reproduced; the normalisation is the same as in Stage D (<x0|psi> = 1), so no rescaling is needed. **Octet mixing (cell 55)**: R_c = u.C/((u+u').C) (u = e1 E13, u' = e1 E23 E12 on the (1,1,1) slice; (u+u') = (1,3)) = -1.694366610178479773 and 1.095564214968898936 from the Q side, equal to the chain values to 5e-19 / 4e-20 and to the earlier notebooks' -1.694 / 1.096. The top-irrep slice 2.609467455621301775 has no earlier reference number.

**Cost statement (cell 56).** Ten products = twenty 2 x 2 bracket determinants Bk2, four block sums (c, c'); the ladder vectors have 18 (ladder 1) + 15 (ladder 2) distinct projective classes among the 2 x 20 vectors, so 2 x 33 = 66 brackets <Q^2 F>, <Q^3 F> (each a 4-term sum; 80 without sharing); the Q-values needed are Q^2 and Q^3 on the four points of each ladder, 16 numbers (the Q^3(theta_s - c h) and M_vac use the same points). Comparison: det6 = 36 bracket entries (each an 8-pole sum) and one 6 x 6 determinant; direct sum over states dim V^2 = 64. The count of the bracket formula is fixed by the ladder length and the block ranks, not by dim V; it is NOT a determinant, and for the adjoint it is of the same order as the det6 (66 four-term sums plus 20 two-by-two determinants against 36 eight-term sums plus one 6 x 6 determinant); its content is a closed-form (a, a') dependence of a CG-complete family. For [3,1,0] (Task 4) it is not cheaper than the det6.

===== cell 58 (kind 1) =====
## Task 2: t-dependence and the u-form (drivers WIPE `Global``; run after Part B)

Cell 59 re-runs the finite-twist cells for 15 parameter points (the base point, the same t at (2h, 2 theta) and at another z, and 12 other t values, all distinct from the integers at which the two ladders overlap) with the pivots of the base point forced; cell 61 fits the t-dependence, assembles the kernels and the closed forms exactly in (s, t); cell 63 tests the SoV-measure classes.

===== cell 59 (kind 2) =====
(*@c20*)
(* Task 2 driver. RUN AFTER THE OTHER TASKS: it WIPES Global` (it re-runs the finite-twist cells a01-a10 (setup, K tensor, tools, symbolic blocks) and a11-a12 (kernels and closed forms) of THIS notebook, read from its own file, with other (h, theta, z) lines); everything it stores lives in the context T2` (not wiped; NO Module/With locals are used across the wipe, they would live in Global`). The pivots/orientation of the base point are FORCED at every other point so that the wedge vectors are compared in the same gauge. Points: base (t = 62/35); the same t at (2h, 2 theta) and at another z (K tensor homogeneity and z-independence); 12 further t values (h = 1/2 unless noted; the t values avoid the integers at which ladder points coincide). *)
T2`nbFile = "C:/Users/pryan/git-projects/gaudin-cg/Paul/Mathematica/new-conventions/L2-210-two-vacua-brackets.wb";
T2`cells := T2`cells = Select[Import[T2`nbFile, "RawJSON"]["cells"], #["kind"] == 2 &][[All, "value"]];
T2`pick[tag_] := First[Select[T2`cells, StringContainsQ[#, "(*@" <> tag <> "*)"] &]];
T2`tmp = FileNameJoin[{$TemporaryDirectory, "t2_driver_cell.wl"}];
T2`runCell[tag_] := (Export[T2`tmp, StringReplace[T2`pick[tag], T2`subs], "Text", CharacterEncoding -> "UTF-8"]; Get[T2`tmp, CharacterEncoding -> "UTF-8"]; Null);
T2`seqA = {"a01", "a02", "a03", "a04", "a05", "a06", "a07", "a08", "a09", "a10"};
T2`run[lam_String, hs_String, ths_String, zs_String, forced_, keepK_] := (
  T2`t0 = AbsoluteTime[];
  T2`subs = {"Λ = {2, 1, 0}; nL = 2;" -> "Λ = " <> lam <> "; nL = 2;", "hN = 1/2; thN = {2/7, -3/5}; zN = {3/2, 5/4, 2/3};" -> "hN = " <> hs <> "; thN = " <> ths <> "; zN = " <> zs <> ";"};
  Scan[T2`runCell, T2`seqA];
  If[forced =!= None, ToExpression["Global`forcedBest = " <> ToString[forced, InputForm]]];
  T2`runCell["a11"]; T2`runCell["a12"];
  <|"t" -> ToExpression["Global`tPar"], "h" -> ToExpression["Global`hN"], "th" -> ToExpression["Global`thN"], "z" -> ToExpression["Global`zN"], "kern" -> ToExpression["Global`kern"], "dec" -> ToExpression["Global`cfBest"], "best" -> ToExpression["Global`bestAll"], "resid" -> ToExpression["Global`cfResid"], "law" -> ToExpression["Global`lawOK"], "Kt" -> If[keepK, ToExpression["Global`Kt[[All, Flatten[Global`mIdxMat]]]"], None], "time" -> AbsoluteTime[] - T2`t0|>);
T2`pts = {{"base", "1/2", "{2/7, -3/5}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"2h, 2 theta (same t)", "1", "{4/7, -6/5}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"other z (same t)", "1/2", "{2/7, -3/5}", "{5/3, -3/7, 4/5}"}, {"h = 1/3, th = (1/5, -4/9)", "1/3", "{1/5, -4/9}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = 1/5", "1/2", "{2/7, 1/5}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -26/105", "1/2", "{2/7, -26/105}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -27/28", "1/2", "{2/7, -27/28}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -37/42", "1/2", "{2/7, -37/42}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -1/70", "1/2", "{2/7, -1/70}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -3/7", "1/2", "{2/7, -3/7}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -5/3", "1/2", "{2/7, -5/3}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = 1/3", "1/2", "{2/7, 1/3}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = 2/3", "1/2", "{2/7, 2/3}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -7/4", "1/2", "{2/7, -7/4}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}, {"th2 = -1/3", "1/2", "{2/7, -1/3}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}"}};
T2`base = T2`run["{2, 1, 0}", "1/2", "{2/7, -3/5}", "{3/2, 5/4, 2/3}", None, True];
T2`forced = Association[Table[k -> {T2`base["best"][k][[1]], T2`base["best"][k][[2]], {T2`base["dec"][k]["piv"]}}, {k, Keys[T2`base["best"]]}]];
T2`safe[i_] := With[{r = TimeConstrained[T2`run["{2, 1, 0}", T2`pts[[i, 2]], T2`pts[[i, 3]], T2`pts[[i, 4]], T2`forced, i <= 3], 200, $Failed]}, If[r === $Failed, Nothing, T2`pts[[i, 1]] -> r]];
{T2`tDriver, T2`res} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Prepend[Table[T2`safe[i], {i, 2, Length[T2`pts]}], "base" -> T2`base]]];
Grid[Prepend[Table[{k, T2`res[k]["t"], T2`res[k]["h"], T2`res[k]["th"], T2`res[k]["z"], Union[Values[T2`res[k]["resid"]]], Union[Values[T2`res[k]["law"]]], N[T2`res[k]["time"], 3]}, {k, Keys[T2`res]}], {"point", "t = theta12/h", "h", "theta", "z", "closed-form residual (blocks reconstructed from the products: all True)", "scaling law A = a^(m+m'+e) a'^(n+n'+e') kappa(a'/a) exact", "time (s)"}], Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 61 (kind 2) =====
(*@c21*)
(* Task 2 analysis (uses only the T2` data of the driver; works in a wiped Global`). (i) the blocks depend on (h, theta, z) only through t = theta12/h: the kernels kappa(s) at the three points with t = 62/35 (base, (2h, 2 theta), other z) are IDENTICAL; the K tensor is z-independent (exact equality at the other z) and homogeneous in (h, theta) (ratio of every nonzero entry at (2h, 2 theta) to the base entry: tallied). (ii) every coefficient of every kernel entry kappa^{cc'}_{ij}(s, t) = sum_q c_q(t) s^q is fitted as a rational function of t from the sampled points (lowest total degree through ALL fit points, one null vector), 2 points held out and used as validation; the kernels are then assembled exactly in (s, t) and checked against every stored kernel. (iii) closed forms in (s, t): the forced-pivot decomposition of Task 1 redone SYMBOLICALLY on the fitted kernels, checked against the stored per-t decompositions (as 2-forms, i.e. gauge-free), pole positions in t. *)
tN = T2`res[#]["t"] & /@ Keys[T2`res];
labs = Keys[T2`res];
kern0 = T2`res["base"]["kern"];
sameT = {"2h, 2 theta (same t)", "other z (same t)"};
checks["T2 kernels identical at the same t (base, (2h, 2 theta), other z)"] = And @@ Flatten[Table[Table[T2`res[l]["kern"][k]["kappa"] === kern0[k]["kappa"] && T2`res[l]["kern"][k]["e"] === kern0[k]["e"] && T2`res[l]["kern"][k]["e'"] === kern0[k]["e'"], {k, Keys[kern0]}], {l, sameT}]];
Kb = T2`res["base"]["Kt"]; Kh2 = T2`res["2h, 2 theta (same t)"]["Kt"]; Kz = T2`res["other z (same t)"]["Kt"];
checks["T2 K tensor z-independent: exact equality at the other z"] = Normal[Kb] === Normal[Kz];
(* the K tensor columns in the CANONICAL order of the SoV label pairs (the eigenvector order of B changes with the parameters): per column the ratio entry(2h, 2 theta)/entry(h, theta) of the nonzero entries must be a single value lambda^d with lambda = 2 *)
Ab = Normal[Kb]; Ah = Normal[Kh2];
colRat = Table[Union[Table[If[Ab[[r, c]] === 0 && Ah[[r, c]] === 0, Nothing, If[Ab[[r, c]] === 0 || Ah[[r, c]] === 0, "pattern differs", Ah[[r, c]]/Ab[[r, c]]]], {r, d2}]], {c, d2}];
checks["T2 K tensor at (2h, 2 theta): same nonzero pattern; single ratio per column; tally of log2(ratio) over columns"] = {Unitize[Ab] === Unitize[Ah], And @@ (Length[#] == 1 & /@ colRat), Tally[Flatten[colRat /. r_Rational | r_Integer :> Log2[r]]]};
(* rational fit in t *)
tvar = Symbol["tt"];
heldOut = {57/14, 26/21};
fitPts = Select[Range[Length[labs]], ! MemberQ[heldOut, tN[[#]]] &];
fitIdx = First /@ GatherBy[fitPts, tN[[#]] &];      (* distinct t values used for the fit *)
tsFit = tN[[fitIdx]];
ratFit[vals_] := Module[{res = {}, ns},
  If[AllTrue[vals, # === 0 &], Return[0]];
  If[Length[Union[vals]] == 1, Return[First[vals]]];
  Do[If[res === {} && m + n + 2 <= Length[tsFit] - 1, ns = NullSpace[Table[Join[Table[tsFit[[i]]^k, {k, 0, m}], Table[-vals[[i]] tsFit[[i]]^k, {k, 0, n}]], {i, Length[tsFit]}]];
    If[Length[ns] == 1, res = Factor[Sum[ns[[1, k + 1]] tvar^k, {k, 0, m}]/Sum[ns[[1, m + 2 + k]] tvar^k, {k, 0, n}]]]], {tot, 0, 9}, {m, 0, tot}, {n, 0, tot - m}];
  If[res === {}, $Failed, res]];
bKeys = {{0, 0}, {0, 1}, {1, 0}, {1, 1}};
sdeg = 4;
kapAt[l_, k_] := T2`res[l]["kern"][k]["kappa"];
{T2`tFit, kapFit} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> Table[Sum[sS^q ratFit[Table[Coefficient[kapAt[labs[[i]], k][[ii, jj]], sS, q], {i, fitIdx}]], {q, 0, sdeg}], {ii, nMin}, {jj, nMin}], {k, bKeys}]]];
fitFail = Count[Flatten[Table[ratFit[Table[Coefficient[kapAt[labs[[i]], k][[ii, jj]], sS, q], {i, fitIdx}]], {k, bKeys}, {ii, nMin}, {jj, nMin}, {q, 0, sdeg}]], $Failed];
kapFitAt[k_, tv_] := Map[Together[# /. tvar -> tv] &, kapFit[k], {2}];
checks["T2 fitted kernels reproduce the stored kernels at ALL sampled points incl. the 2 held-out (exact)"] = Table[And @@ Table[Together[kapFitAt[k, tN[[i]]] - kapAt[labs[[i]], k]] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}], {k, bKeys}], {i, Length[labs]}];
heldOK = Table[And @@ Table[Together[kapFitAt[k, th] - kapAt[labs[[First[Flatten[Position[tN, th]]]]], k]] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}], {k, bKeys}], {th, heldOut}];
(* the laws, entry by entry: kappa^{cc'}_{(m m'),(n n')}(s, t) *)
mnL3 = {{-3, -2}, {-3, -1}, {-2, -1}, {-3, 0}, {-2, 0}, {-1, 0}};
kapLaws = Flatten[Table[If[kapFit[k][[ii, jj]] === 0, Nothing, {k, mnL3[[ii]], mnL3[[jj]], Collect[kapFit[k][[ii, jj]], sS, Factor]}], {k, bKeys}, {ii, nMin}, {jj, nMin}], 2];
poleSet[expr_] := Union[Flatten[Table[tvar /. Quiet[Solve[Denominator[Together[c]] == 0, tvar]], {c, DeleteCases[Flatten[CoefficientList[Numerator[Together[expr]], sS] /. {} -> {0}], 0] /. 0 -> Nothing}]]];
polesKap = DeleteCases[Union[Flatten[Table[Quiet[tvar /. Solve[Denominator[Together[Coefficient[kapFit[k][[ii, jj]], sS, q]]] == 0, tvar]], {k, bKeys}, {ii, nMin}, {jj, nMin}, {q, 0, sdeg}]]], tvar];
zerosKap = DeleteCases[Union[Flatten[Table[Quiet[tvar /. Solve[Numerator[Together[Coefficient[kapFit[k][[ii, jj]], sS, q]]] == 0, tvar]], {k, bKeys}, {ii, nMin}, {jj, nMin}, {q, 0, sdeg}]]], tvar];
(* --- symbolic closed forms in (s, t) with the base orientation / pivot sets --- *)
bestB = T2`res["base"]["best"];
amK[v_] := Table[Which[i == j, 0, i < j, v[[Position[{{1, 2}, {1, 3}, {2, 3}, {1, 4}, {2, 4}, {3, 4}}, {i, j}][[1, 1]]]], True, -v[[Position[{{1, 2}, {1, 3}, {2, 3}, {1, 4}, {2, 4}, {3, 4}}, {j, i}][[1, 1]]]]], {i, 4}, {j, 4}];
wedgeVK[F_, G_] := Flatten[Table[F[[a]] G[[b]] - F[[b]] G[[a]], {b, 2, 4}, {a, 1, b - 1}]];
togt[x_] := Together[x];
rowSetForK[M_, piv_] := First[Select[Subsets[Range[Dimensions[M][[1]]], {MatrixRank[M /. {sS -> 5/3, tvar -> 7/3}]}], MatrixRank[M[[#, piv]] /. {sS -> 5/3, tvar -> 7/3}] == MatrixRank[M /. {sS -> 5/3, tvar -> 7/3}] &]];
pivDecompK[M_, piv_] := Module[{prow = rowSetForK[M, piv], R}, R = Map[togt, Inverse[M[[prow, piv]]] . M[[prow, All]], {2}]; Table[{M[[All, piv[[k]]]], R[[k]]}, {k, Length[piv]}]];
terms2K[M_, orient_, piv_] := If[orient === "rows", pivDecompK[M, piv], Reverse /@ pivDecompK[Transpose[M], piv]];
wedgeSymK[v_] := Module[{M = Map[togt, amK[v], {2}], cands}, cands = Table[{togt /@ (M[[All, p[[1]]]]/M[[p[[1]], p[[2]]]]), M[[All, p[[2]]]]}, {p, Select[Subsets[Range[4], {2}], M[[#[[1]], #[[2]]]] =!= 0 &]}]; First[SortBy[cands, LeafCount[Map[Factor, #, {2}]] &]]];
{T2`tSym, symDec} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> Module[{M = Map[togt, kapFit[k], {2}], tt2}, tt2 = Map[togt, terms2K[M, T2`res["base"]["dec"][k]["orient"], T2`res["base"]["dec"][k]["piv"]], {3}]; Table[{wedgeSymK[t[[1]]], wedgeSymK[t[[2]]]}, {t, tt2}]], {k, bKeys}]]];
symRecon = Table[k -> (Map[togt, Total[Table[Outer[Times, wedgeVK[t[[1, 1]], t[[1, 2]]], wedgeVK[t[[2, 1]], t[[2, 2]]]], {t, symDec[k]}]] - kapFit[k], {2}] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}]), {k, bKeys}];
(* compare with the stored per-t decompositions as 2-forms (gauge-free) *)
decAt[l_, k_] := T2`res[l]["dec"][k]["dec"];
termForm[t_] := Outer[Times, wedgeVK[t[[1, 1]], t[[1, 2]]], wedgeVK[t[[2, 1]], t[[2, 2]]]];
cmpDec = Table[And @@ Table[Length[symDec[k]] == Length[decAt[labs[[i]], k]] && And @@ Table[Map[Together, termForm[symDec[k][[j]] /. tvar -> tN[[i]]] - termForm[decAt[labs[[i]], k][[j]]], {2}] === ConstantArray[0, {nMin, nMin}], {j, Length[symDec[k]]}], {k, bKeys}], {i, Length[labs]}];
cfProducts = Flatten[Table[Map[{k[[1]], k[[2]], Factor /@ #[[1, 1]], Factor /@ #[[1, 2]], Factor /@ #[[2, 1]], Factor /@ #[[2, 2]]} &, symDec[k]], {k, bKeys}], 1];
denFacs = Union[Flatten[Table[Map[First, Join[FactorList[Denominator[Together[x]]]][[2 ;;]]], {p, cfProducts}, {x, Flatten[p[[3 ;;]]]}]]];
facOnlyT = Select[denFacs, FreeQ[#, sS] &]; facOnlyS = Select[denFacs, FreeQ[#, tvar] &]; facMixed = Complement[denFacs, facOnlyT, facOnlyS];
polesCF = {Union[Flatten[Table[tvar /. Solve[f == 0, tvar], {f, DeleteCases[facOnlyT, _?NumberQ]}]]], facOnlyS, facMixed};
T2`kapFit = kapFit; T2`symDec = symDec; T2`cfProducts = cfProducts; T2`kern0 = kern0; T2`bestB = bestB;
Grid[{{"kernels identical at the same t (base, 2h/2theta, other z), e, e' too", checks["T2 kernels identical at the same t (base, (2h, 2 theta), other z)"]}, {"K tensor z-independent (exact equality at the other z)", checks["T2 K tensor z-independent: exact equality at the other z"]}, {"K tensor at (2h, 2 theta), canonical column order: {same pattern, single ratio per column, tally of log2(ratio)}", checks["T2 K tensor at (2h, 2 theta): same nonzero pattern; single ratio per column; tally of log2(ratio) over columns"]}, {"all t values (15 points)", tN}, {"distinct t used in the fit; held out", {tsFit, heldOut}}, {"# coefficients with no fit found (should be 0)", fitFail}, {"fitted kernels reproduce ALL stored kernels exactly (15 points): all True?", Union[Flatten[checks["T2 fitted kernels reproduce the stored kernels at ALL sampled points incl. the 2 held-out (exact)"]]]}, {"held-out points reproduced", heldOK}, {"pole positions in t of the kernel coefficients; zeros of the numerators (excluding trivial)", {polesKap, zerosKap}}, {"time (s) fit", T2`tFit}, {"kernel laws {(c,c'), slot (m m'), slot (n n'), kappa(s, t)} (tt = t = theta12/h, sS = s = a'/a)", kapLaws}, {"symbolic closed forms in (s, t): blocks reconstructed exactly", symRecon}, {"symbolic closed forms vs stored per-t decompositions as 2-forms: all points, all blocks", Union[Flatten[cmpDec]]}, {"time (s) symbolic decomposition", T2`tSym}, {"denominator factors of the closed-form entries (gauge: forced pivots): t-poles (roots of t-only factors), s-only factors, mixed (s,t) factors = spurious pivot-minor curves", polesCF}, {"closed forms {(c,c'), f, g (ladder 1, m = -3..0), f', g' (ladder 2)}; F_m = a^m f_m, G_m = a^(m+e) g_m, F'_n = a'^n f'_n, G'_n = a'^(n+e') g'_n; e,e' per block = (7,5),(7,4),(6,5),(6,4)", cfProducts}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 62 (kind 1) =====
*Printed output of the code cell `c21_t2_fit` (evaluated in the shared kernel K1 from exactly the saved source; not a native notebook output). Rows of the printed `Grid` as InputForm; first entry = cell id, second = time in s. (In the closed-form tables of the c-cells `sS` = s = a'/a, `tt` = t = theta12/h; in the finite-twist cells aS = a, bS = a'.)*

```
{"c21_t2_fit", 1.44, {{"kernels identical at the same t (base, 2h/2theta, other z), e, e' too", True}, {"K tensor z-independent (exact equality at the other z)", True}, 
  {"K tensor at (2h, 2 theta), canonical column order: {same pattern, single ratio per column, tally of log2(ratio)}", {True, True, {{0, 64}}}}, 
  {"all t values (15 points)", {62/35, 62/35, 62/35, 29/15, 6/35, 16/15, 5/2, 7/3, 3/5, 10/7, 82/21, -2/21, -16/21, 57/14, 26/21}}, 
  {"distinct t used in the fit; held out", {{62/35, 29/15, 6/35, 16/15, 5/2, 7/3, 3/5, 10/7, 82/21, -2/21, -16/21}, {57/14, 26/21}}}, {"# coefficients with no fit found (should be 0)", 0},
```

===== cell 63 (kind 2) =====
(*@c22*)
(* Task 2: the u-form. Gauge-free class tests on the PLANES P = span{f, g} of the ten products (ladder 1: f, g over m = -3..0 in the frame h = 1, theta1 = 0; ladder 2: f', g' over n = -3..0 with theta2 = -t; the exponentials a^m, a'^n are the bra twists and are already stripped (s-frame), so the classes are  rho^m x [SoV measure] x [low-degree polynomial in m]  with rho = 1 here). S = support of the plane (points where the 2-form has a nonzero row). Classes restricted to S: (a) wgt_S(m) m^k, wgt_S(m) = 1/prod_{m' in S, m' != m}(m' - m); (b) the global SoV measure W6 over the six poles theta_s - n h (n = 0, 1, 2) times m^k; (c) m^k; (d) the cross-ladder poles 1/prod_{n=0..2}(m - theta_other + n) times m^k; (e) (d) x wgt_S; k = 0..d. (1) dim(P cap class(d)) at three generic rational (s, t) points (exact NullSpace/MatrixRank); (2) for |S| = 3 (P is a plane inside the 3-dim space of functions on S) the natural vector of each class is  measure x (m - mstar)  with the ROOT mstar_class(s, t) = (c.v1)/(c.v0), c = f_S x g_S the annihilator, v_k = class vector of degree k restricted to S: explicit rational functions of (s, t), printed factored; for |S| = 2 the plane is ALL functions on S (F, G = two delta functions at the two points, Bk2 = a Q-minor); |S| = 4 would need the Moebius-type condition (none occurs). *)
ptsU = {{5/3, 7/3}, {-7/4, 11/5}, {3/7, -13/6}};   (* (s, t) *)
posOf = {-3, -2, -1, 0};
suppOfPlane[f_, g_] := Select[Range[4], f[[#]] =!= 0 || g[[#]] =!= 0 &];
wgtS[S_, m_] := 1/Product[mm - m, {mm, DeleteCases[posOf[[S]], m]}];
W6of[ladder_, tv_, m_] := If[ladder == 1, 1/(Product[m + n, {n, DeleteCases[Range[0, 2], -m]}] Product[m + tv + n, {n, 0, 2}]), 1/(Product[m + k, {k, DeleteCases[Range[0, 2], -m]}] Product[m - tv + k, {k, 0, 2}])];
crossOf[ladder_, tv_, m_] := If[ladder == 1, 1/Product[m + tv + n, {n, 0, 2}], 1/Product[m - tv + n, {n, 0, 2}]];
mpow[m_, k_] := If[k == 0, 1, m^k];
classVec[cls_, S_, ladder_, tv_, k_] := Table[If[! MemberQ[S, i], 0, With[{m = posOf[[i]]}, mpow[m, k] Switch[cls, "a", wgtS[S, m], "b", W6of[ladder, tv, m], "c", 1, "d", crossOf[ladder, tv, m], "e", crossOf[ladder, tv, m] wgtS[S, m]]]], {i, 4}];
clsList = {"a", "b", "c", "d", "e"};
interDim[f_, g_, S_, cls_, ladder_, pt_, d_] := Module[{fg = {f, g} /. {sS -> pt[[1]], tvar -> pt[[2]]}, ann, vecs},
  ann = NullSpace[fg];   (* covectors c with c.f = c.g = 0 *)
  vecs = Table[classVec[cls, S, ladder, pt[[2]], k], {k, 0, d}];
  If[ann === {}, d + 1, d + 1 - MatrixRank[Table[ann[[a]] . vecs[[k]], {a, Length[ann]}, {k, d + 1}]]]];
planeRow[i_, side_] := Module[{p = T2`cfProducts[[i]], f, g, S},
  f = If[side == 1, p[[3]], p[[5]]]; g = If[side == 1, p[[4]], p[[6]]];
  S = suppOfPlane[f /. {sS -> 5/3, tvar -> 7/3}, g /. {sS -> 5/3, tvar -> 7/3}];
  {i, {p[[1]], p[[2]]}, side, posOf[[S]], Table[Table[Max[Table[interDim[f, g, S, cls, side, pt, d], {pt, ptsU}]], {d, 0, 2}], {cls, clsList}], S, f, g}];
{T2`tU, uTab} = AbsoluteTiming[Flatten[Table[planeRow[i, side], {i, Length[T2`cfProducts]}, {side, 2}], 1]];
(* roots mstar_class(s, t) for the |S| = 3 planes: the natural vector measure x (m - mstar) *)
rootsOf[row_] := Module[{S = row[[6]], side = row[[3]], f = row[[7]], g = row[[8]], c, out},
  If[Length[S] == 2, Return["|S| = 2: plane = all functions on S; F, G = delta functions at the two points"]];
  If[Length[S] == 4, Return["|S| = 4 (not occurring)"]];
  c = Together /@ Cross[f[[S]], g[[S]]];
  Table[Module[{v0 = classVec[cls, S, side, tvar, 0][[S]], v1 = classVec[cls, S, side, tvar, 1][[S]], n0, n1},
    n0 = Together[c . v0]; n1 = Together[c . v1];
    cls -> If[n0 === 0 && n1 === 0, "plane inside the class (d = 1)", If[n0 === 0, "mstar = infinity (pure measure vector)", Factor[Together[n1/n0]]]]], {cls, clsList}]];
{T2`tRoots, rootTab} = AbsoluteTiming[Table[{row[[1]], row[[2]], row[[3]], row[[4]], rootsOf[row]}, {row, uTab}]];
deltaTab = Table[If[Length[row[[6]]] == 3, With[{c = Together /@ Cross[row[[7, row[[6]]]], row[[8, row[[6]]]]]}, {row[[1]], row[[3]], posOf[[row[[6]]]], "delta functions in the plane at m =", posOf[[row[[6]]]][[Flatten[Position[c, 0]]]]}], Nothing], {row, uTab}];
checks["T2 u-form: # planes with |S| = 2, 3, 4"] = Tally[Length /@ uTab[[All, 4]]];
checks["T2 u-form: dims of P cap class(d), d = 0,1,2, all classes, |S| = 3 planes: always (0,1,2)?"] = Union[Table[row[[5]], {row, Select[uTab, Length[#[[4]]] == 3 &]}]];
Grid[{{"# planes by support size |S|", checks["T2 u-form: # planes with |S| = 2, 3, 4"]}, {"dims of P cap class(d), d = 0,1,2 (classes a, b, c, d, e), distinct values over the |S| = 3 planes", checks["T2 u-form: dims of P cap class(d), d = 0,1,2, all classes, |S| = 3 planes: always (0,1,2)?"]}, {"time (s): dims, roots", {T2`tU, T2`tRoots}}, {"|S| = 3 planes containing a delta function (c = f_S x g_S has a zero component): {product #, side, support, ..., points}", deltaTab}, {"product #, (c,c'), side (1 = ladder 1, 2 = ladder 2), support points m, dims per class [d = 0,1,2]", ""}, Sequence @@ (uTab /. {i_, cc_, side_, S_, dims_, Sp_, f_, g_} :> {{i, cc, side, S}, dims}), {"roots mstar_class(s, t) of the natural vectors  measure(m) x (m - mstar)  (s = a'/a, t = theta12/h; ladder 2 in its own frame x_n = theta2 + n h with theta1 - theta2 = t h)", ""}, Sequence @@ ({{#[[1]], #[[2]], #[[3]], #[[4]]}, #[[5]]} & /@ rootTab)}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 64 (kind 1) =====

(In the closed-form tables of the c-cells `sS` = s = a'/a, `tt` = t = theta12/h; in the finite-twist cells aS = a, bS = a'.)*

===== cell 65 (kind 1) =====
### Task 2 results

**What the blocks depend on.** (cell 62) (1) The kernels kappa^(cc')(s) at the three points with t = 62/35 (base; (h, theta) -> (2h, 2 theta) = (1, (4/7, -6/5)); other z = (5/3, -3/7, 4/5)) are IDENTICAL, as are the exponents (e, e'). (2) The K tensor is z-independent (exact equality at the other z). (3) The K tensor, in the canonical column order of the SoV label pairs (the eigenvector order of B changes with the parameters), is invariant under (h, theta) -> (2h, 2 theta): same nonzero pattern, the ratio entry(2h, 2 theta)/entry(h, theta) is a single value per column and equals 1 for all 64 columns: **homogeneity degree 0** in the normalised-wavefunction units (monic Q's), i.e. K depends on (h, theta) only through t. (4) The scaling law of Task 1 (A = a^(m+m'+e) a'^(n+n'+e') kappa(a'/a)) holds exactly at all 15 points and the minimal-P closed forms exist at every point (every block reconstructed exactly from its ten products with the base pivots and gauge forced).

**t-laws.** The 15 points have 13 distinct t = theta12/h (62/35 three times, 29/15, 6/35, 16/15, 5/2, 7/3, 3/5, 10/7, 82/21, -2/21, -16/21, 57/14, 26/21). Every s-coefficient of every kernel entry kappa^(cc')_{ij}(s, t) = sum_q c_q(t) s^q is fitted as a rational function of t by the lowest total degree that goes exactly through the 11 fit values (one null vector), 0 failures; the 2 held-out values t = 57/14, 26/21 and the two duplicates are reproduced EXACTLY by the fitted kernels (all 15 points, exact). The forty kernel laws kappa(s, t) are printed in cell 62 (e.g. kappa^(1,1)_{(-3,-2),(-3,-2)} = s, kappa^(1,1)_{(-3,-2),(-3,-1)} = -2 s (t+2)/t, kappa^(1,1)_{(-3,-1),(-3,-1)} = 4 s (t-2)(t+2)/((t-1)(t+1)), kappa^(0,0)_{(-3,-2),(-3,-2)} = (s-1)(2s-1)/(t-1)). **Pole positions: the kernel coefficients have poles only at t = 0, 1, -1** (denominators t, t^2, t-1, t+1 and products), numerators contain (t-2), (t+2), (t-3), (t+3), (2t+1), (3t+1), (2t-3), 2t^2-2t-3, t^2-8t+6, 4t^2+t+1 (no pole at the integers 2, 3 where the ladders meet: those enter only the pivot gauge). The closed forms F, G, F', G' in (s, t) (forced pivots, the symbolic decomposition of the fitted kernels; checked against the stored per-t decompositions termwise as 2-forms at all 15 points, exact) are printed in cell 62; their denominators are t in {-2, -1, 0, 1, 2}, the s-only factors s, s-1, s-2 and four mixed (s, t) polynomials = the vanishing curves of the pivot minors (gauge artefacts: the 2-forms themselves, i.e. the kernels, have only the poles above).

**u-form (cell 64).** Exact class tests on the ladder planes P = span{f, g} of the ten products (20 planes). **Result: only the trivial u-forms exist.** Two planes have a two-point support (product 3 and product 6 on ladder 2: the plane is ALL functions on {m = -3, -2}; F, G = delta functions, Bk2 = a Q-minor at two points) and 18 planes have a three-point support. For the 18 planes the dimension of the intersection with each class (a: wgt_S(m) x polynomial, b: the global six-pole SoV measure x polynomial, c: polynomial, d: cross-ladder poles x polynomial, e: d x wgt_S; degrees 0, 1, 2) is always the generic dimension d (0, 1, 2) at three generic (s, t): the plane is never contained in a natural class (dimension 2 at degree 1 would be needed); the single exception is product 10, ladder 1, where the plane contains the constant vector on its support (class c, degree 0). Each plane contains exactly one natural vector per class, measure(m) x (m - mstar), with root mstar_class(s, t) an explicit rational function of (s, t) of high degree (printed per plane in the last rows of the cell; irrational roots do not occur, the obstruction is dimensional, not arithmetic). 8 of the 18 planes contain a delta function (G = delta in the pivot gauge). Consequence: the ladder kernels are a^((u-theta1)/h) (resp. a'^((u-theta2)/h)) times coefficients that are RATIONAL in (s, t), with delta- or two-point-supported wedge vectors and no SoV-measure x low-degree-polynomial form for the 3-point planes; there is no explicit F(u), G(u) beyond those trivial cases. (The two pivot-set winners of the rank-3 blocks give two such decompositions; the planes are not unique but the same statement holds for the base choice.) Möbius/natural-measure tests beyond these dimension counts were not run.

===== cell 66 (kind 1) =====
## Task 4: [3,1,0] note (driver WIPES `Global``; run last)

Cell 67: re-runs the finite-twist cells for Lambda = {3,1,0} (dim V = 15, d2 = 225, ladder of 5 points, blocks 10 x 10) and asks whether the lower bound sum r = 24 of the (inf, inf) family is attainable (full pivot-set scan of both orientations plus a greedy elimination with 400 random restarts).

===== cell 67 (kind 2) =====
(*@c23*)
(* Task 4, the [3,1,0] note. RUN LAST: WIPES Global` (re-runs a01-a10 of this notebook with the line Lambda = {3, 1, 0}; results live in T4`). The (inf, inf) family of [3,1,0] has blocks (c, c') of size 10 x 10 (ladder pairs of 5 points) and rank 6 (Stage B: ranks 6,6,6,6, sum 24, default-pivot products 12,12,9,12 = 45). Question: is the lower bound 24 (every 2-form factor decomposable) attainable? Two searches at one rational point (a, a') = (7/5, -4/3): (i) the full scan of pivot-set decompositions M = M[All, piv] R (both orientations; all independent sets of 6 columns/rows); (ii) a GREEDY elimination: repeatedly pick a pivot (i, j) with M_ij != 0 whose term u_j (x) w_i (u_j = column j = ladder-1 2-form, w_i = row i = ladder-2 2-form, cost = (rank u_j / 2)(rank w_i / 2) = 1 when both are decomposable) is cheapest, strip it (Schur complement, rank drops by 1), random tie-breaks, 400 restarts; the cost of a rank-4 2-form is two wedges. *)
T4`nbFile = "C:/Users/pryan/git-projects/gaudin-cg/Paul/Mathematica/new-conventions/L2-210-two-vacua-brackets.wb";
T4`cells := T4`cells = Select[Import[T4`nbFile, "RawJSON"]["cells"], #["kind"] == 2 &][[All, "value"]];
T4`pick[tag_] := First[Select[T4`cells, StringContainsQ[#, "(*@" <> tag <> "*)"] &]];
T4`tmp = FileNameJoin[{$TemporaryDirectory, "t4_driver_cell.wl"}];
T4`subs = {"Λ = {2, 1, 0}; nL = 2;" -> "Λ = {3, 1, 0}; nL = 2;"};
T4`runCell[tag_] := (Export[T4`tmp, StringReplace[T4`pick[tag], T4`subs], "Text", CharacterEncoding -> "UTF-8"]; Get[T4`tmp, CharacterEncoding -> "UTF-8"]; Null);
T4`t0 = AbsoluteTime[];
Scan[T4`runCell, {"a01", "a02", "a03", "a04", "a05", "a06", "a07", "a08", "a09", "a10"}];
T4`tSetup = AbsoluteTime[] - T4`t0;
T4`info = {ToExpression["Global`Λ"], ToExpression["Global`d2"], ToExpression["Global`nMin"], ToExpression["Global`pPts"], ToExpression["Global`mPts"], ToExpression["Global`checks[\"T0 ranks per block (generic over the plane), P per block and sum at the 3 points, identity {# products, dev}\"]"]};
T4`pt = {ToExpression["Global`aS"] -> 7/5, ToExpression["Global`bS"] -> -4/3};
T4`bg = Association[Table[k -> (ToExpression["Global`bgPl"][k] /. T4`pt), {k, Keys[ToExpression["Global`bgPl"]]}]];
T4`h2[v_] := MatrixRank[ToExpression["Global`amat"][v]]/2;
T4`ranksB = Table[k -> MatrixRank[T4`bg[k]], {k, Keys[T4`bg]}];
(* (i) pivot-set scan *)
T4`pivSets[M_] := Select[Subsets[Range[Dimensions[M][[2]]], {MatrixRank[M]}], MatrixRank[M[[All, #]]] == MatrixRank[M] &];
T4`rowSetFor[M_, piv_] := First[Select[Subsets[Range[Dimensions[M][[1]]], {MatrixRank[M]}], MatrixRank[M[[#, piv]]] == MatrixRank[M] &]];
T4`terms[M_, piv_] := Module[{prow = T4`rowSetFor[M, piv], R}, R = Inverse[M[[prow, piv]]] . M[[prow, All]]; Table[{M[[All, piv[[k]]]], R[[k]]}, {k, Length[piv]}]];
T4`cnt[tt_] := Total[Table[T4`h2[t[[1]]] T4`h2[t[[2]]], {t, tt}]];
T4`scan[M_] := Module[{a = Table[T4`cnt[T4`terms[M, piv]], {piv, T4`pivSets[M]}], b = Table[T4`cnt[Reverse /@ T4`terms[Transpose[M], piv]], {piv, T4`pivSets[Transpose[M]]}]}, {{Min[a], Max[a], Length[a]}, {Min[b], Max[b], Length[b]}}];
{T4`tScan, T4`scanRes} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> T4`scan[T4`bg[k]], {k, Keys[T4`bg]}]]];
(* (ii) greedy elimination *)
T4`greedy[M0_] := Module[{M = M0, cost = 0, steps = 0, hr, hc, cands, best, i, j, minc},
  While[! AllTrue[Flatten[M], # === 0 &],
   hc = Table[T4`h2[M[[All, jj]]], {jj, Dimensions[M][[2]]}]; hr = Table[T4`h2[M[[ii, All]]], {ii, Dimensions[M][[1]]}];
   cands = Select[Tuples[{Range[Dimensions[M][[1]]], Range[Dimensions[M][[2]]]}], M[[#[[1]], #[[2]]]] =!= 0 &];
   minc = Min[hr[[#[[1]]]] hc[[#[[2]]]] & /@ cands];
   best = RandomChoice[Select[cands, hr[[#[[1]]]] hc[[#[[2]]]] == minc &]];
   {i, j} = best; cost += minc; steps++;
   M = M - Outer[Times, M[[All, j]], M[[i, All]]]/M[[i, j]]];
  {cost, steps}];
SeedRandom[2026];
{T4`tGreedy, T4`greedyRes} = AbsoluteTiming[Association[Table[k -> Table[T4`greedy[T4`bg[k]], {400}], {k, Keys[T4`bg]}]]];
T4`greedyBest = Association[Table[k -> {Min[T4`greedyRes[k][[All, 1]]], Tally[T4`greedyRes[k][[All, 1]]] // Sort}, {k, Keys[T4`bg]}]];
T4`total = {Total[Min[T4`greedyRes[#][[All, 1]]] & /@ Keys[T4`bg]], Total[Min /@ (T4`scanRes[#][[All, 1]] & /@ Keys[T4`bg])], Total[Last /@ T4`ranksB]};
Grid[{{"rep, d2, nMin, # ladder points, ladder points", {T4`info[[1]], T4`info[[2]], T4`info[[3]], T4`info[[4]], T4`info[[5]]}}, {"[3,1,0] regression of Stage B on the symbolic blocks: {ranks, P (pivot default), # products and identity dev at 3 points}", T4`info[[6]]}, {"point (a, a')", T4`pt}, {"ranks of the four blocks at the point", T4`ranksB}, {"(i) pivot-set scan per block: {rows orientation {min P, max P, # pivot sets}, cols orientation {...}}", Normal[T4`scanRes]}, {"(ii) greedy elimination per block: {best cost found, tally of costs over 400 random restarts}", Normal[T4`greedyBest]}, {"totals: {greedy best sum, pivot-scan best sum (per orientation min), sum of ranks = lower bound}", T4`total}, {"reference (Stage B follow-up): default-pivot sum P = 45 (12,12,9,12); det6 = 40; generic = 54; lower bound = 24", ""}, {"times (s): setup, scan, greedy", {T4`tSetup, T4`tScan, T4`tGreedy}}}, Frame -> All, Alignment -> Left]

===== cell 69 (kind 1) =====
### Task 4 results

Regression of Stage B reproduced on the symbolic blocks: ranks (6, 6, 6, 6) (sum 24), default pivots give 45 products at three points with the explicit-bracket identity on all 225 states to 1e-39. (i) Full pivot-set scan at (a, a') = (7/5, -4/3): blocks (0,0), (0,1): rows orientation has a single admissible pivot set (P = 12), columns orientation 84 sets with P between 17 and 24 (best 17): best 12; block (1,0): 9 (rows, single set; columns 12); block (1,1): 12 in both orientations. Sum of the best: **45** = the Stage B default; (ii) greedy elimination (cheapest term first, 400 random restarts per block): best 12, 12, 9, 15, i.e. 48 (the greedy never beats the pivot scan: (0,0) and (0,1) are 12 in all 400 runs, (1,0) between 9 and 11, (1,1) between 15 and 21). **The lower bound 24 is not attained by any of the searched decompositions: every decomposition found needs, on average, one rank-4 (two-wedge) factor per term (P = 12 for 6 terms in the rank-6 blocks (0,0), (0,1), 9 resp. 12 in (1,0), (1,1)); the best total is 45 (det6 itself costs 40, generic 54).** The searched family is the set of pivot decompositions and of greedy Schur-complement eliminations; a general decomposition with decomposable factors (the column space meets the Grassmannian G(2,5) in a 2-dimensional family, but six independent decomposable columns with decomposable duals would be needed) was not excluded by a proof.

===== cell 70 (kind 1) =====
### adjoint (inf, inf) two-vacuum family: bracket formula and Q-side CG (2026-10-11)

Task 5 summary. Evidence tags: [exact] rational/symbolic, [numeric] 60- or 200-digit chain/Q data.

**1. The bracket formula and its count [exact].** <Phi|Psi>/<x0|Psi> M1_vac M2_vac = sum_{c,c' in {0,1}} Q3(theta1 - c h) Q3(theta2 - c' h) sum_k Bk2^(1)[F_k, G_k] Bk2^(2)[F'_k, G'_k] with **ten products** (blocks (0,0), (0,1), (1,0), (1,1) with 3, 3, 2, 2 terms; every one of the 20 ladder 2-forms is decomposable: Pfaffians identically zero in (a, a')). The "12" of Stage B was the count of the default pivot choice (two rank-4 factors = 4 wedges); the scan of all pivot sets reaches the lower bound sum r = 10 (cells 26, 28). Overlap identity on all 64 states at three generic (a, a') points < 1e-44 (30); symbolic reconstruction of the blocks exact.

**2. Closed forms and their structure [exact].** F, G, F', G' per wedge: cell 29 (s-frame, t = 62/35), cell 31 (RAW rational functions of (a, a'), last row), cell 62 (exact in (s, t)). Scaling law (exact, all t): A^(cc')_{(m m'),(n n')} = a^(m+m'+e) a'^(n+n'+e') kappa^(cc')(s), s = a'/a, kappa polynomial in s, (e, e') = (7,5), (7,4), (6,5), (6,4): the exponentials a^((u-theta1)/h), a'^((u-theta2)/h) are exact power laws and **the two vacua couple only through s = a'/a**; most wedge entries are monomials in (a, a') (of the 160 vector entries 92 vanish, 33 are constants, 31 are monomials c s^k and only 4 are non-monomial in s), many vectors are delta functions; a - a' = a(1 - s) enters through (s-1) factors (rank drop of the rank-3 blocks to 2 at a = a', the global rotation: sum r = 8; a' = 0: sum r = 4; a = 0: 8).

**3. t-laws and poles [exact].** The blocks depend on (h, theta, z) only through t = theta12/h: kernels identical at the same t (also at another z and at (2h, 2 theta)), K tensor z-independent and of homogeneity degree 0 in (h, theta); each kernel coefficient is a rational function of t, fitted on 11 values, validated exactly on 2 held-out values and at all 15 points; kernel poles only at t = 0, 1, -1; closed-form entries (pivot gauge) additionally at t = +-2 and on four pivot-minor curves (cell 62).

**4. u-form [exact].** Only trivial u-forms: 2 two-point planes (delta functions) and 18 three-point planes none of which lies in a natural class (SoV measure x polynomial, global six-pole measure x polynomial, polynomial, cross-ladder poles x polynomial); one natural vector per class per plane with an explicit rational root mstar(s, t); obstruction = dimension, not irrationality (cell 64).

**5. Q-number and eps-exponents [numeric, 200 digits].** Qnum(B) = <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> on all 21 HW-sector states to 7.9e-111; dictionary <Phi_c|Psi_c>/<x0_c|Psi_c> = <Phi_d|psi_d>/(kappa kappa') to 4.9e-123 (cell 50). Exponents (cell 52): entries -1 ... 1 (exact), brackets as large as eps^-4, Bk2's 0 ... 2, all ten product terms and all four block sums O(eps^0 or eps^1), **total eps^6, eps^5, eps^5, eps^4, eps^4, eps^2** for (4,2,0), (4,1,1), (3,3,0), (3,2,1) x 2, (2,2,2) = the CG slice exponents (-8 + total after the kappa kappa' ~ eps^-8 factor): the cancellations sit inside the Bk2's (up to 26 digits at eps = 1e-6) and in the final sum over the four (c, c') blocks (6, 5, 5, 4, 4, 2 orders = 37, 30, 31, 23, 23, 11 digits).

**6. Six CG data from the Q side alone [numeric].** (cell 54) R_Q = kappa kappa' Qnum equals the chain overlap to 1.6e-113 (72 checks); phi_u(0) = closed forms (a'0, c'0, g'0) at w2, w2' -> infinity (checked against the direct Gauss decomposition); errors and spreads = O(eps) (ratio 4.00), Richardson limits Q vs chain <= 1.2e-18: 2.609467455621 (top), 46.67453891138, 10.92853891670, octet functionals 184.3305612334 and -238.5587523317 (slice vectors from two grid points), singlet -5118.538314405; octet mixing -1.6943666102 and 1.0955642150 (cell 56); all reference numbers of the earlier notebooks reproduced, normalisation <x0|psi> = 1.

**7. Cost statement.** Ten products = 20 Bk2 + 4 block sums; 18 + 15 distinct ladder vectors = 66 brackets <Q^2 F>, <Q^3 F> (80 without sharing), 16 Q-values (Q^2, Q^3 on the four points of each ladder); det6: 36 bracket entries + one 6 x 6 determinant; direct sum dim V^2 = 64. Not a determinant; scales with ladder length and block ranks (fixed by the boxes), not with dim V.

**8. [3,1,0] note [exact, one point].** Blocks 10 x 10 of rank 6 (sum 24); full pivot scan and 400-restart greedy elimination give best sum P = 45 (greedy 48): the lower bound 24 is NOT attained by the searched decompositions; det6 = 40, generic 54 (cell 68).

**9. Timings (s).** Setup cells 2.1 in total; Task 0: 0.7; Task 1: 0.2 + 0.6 + 0.6; Part B: setup 2.4 (branches at one eps 1.5), Qnum + dictionary 0.3, exponent table 3.8 (6 branches x 3 eps), CG (12 x 24 Q-side and chain overlaps) 0.9; Task 2: driver 48 (15 parameter points, 3 s each), fit 1.9, u-form 0.8; Task 4: 71 (setup 24, pivot scan 20, greedy 28). Whole notebook run from the sources: about 2.5 minutes.

**10. Surprises and things to know.** (i) The 12 vs 10 question was a pivot-choice artefact; the lower bound is attained. (ii) The whole family is governed by the single variable s = a'/a (torus covariance); the t-dependence is rational with poles only at t = 0, +-1 in the kernels. (iii) The Stage B regression point (a, a') = (2/3, -5/7) has w1 = 1/a = z1 (degenerate vacuum): 22 of the 64 states are orthogonal to the covector there (42 nonzero overlaps; (3/2, 1/4), w1 = z3, behaves the same); the identity holds there too, but non-vacuity is only claimed at the generic points (all 64 overlaps nonzero). (iv) The total Qnum is smaller than every individual term by eps^2 ... eps^6: the formula needs ~ 40 digits of headroom at eps = 1e-6; with the 200-digit chain it is exact to 1e-110. (v) No natural u-form exists for the three-point planes. (vi) For [3,1,0] the family is not cheap in the product-of-2 x 2-brackets metric. Not done: the O(eps) layer of the Q-number; a proof of minimality for [3,1,0]; Moebius tests.
